- [ ] **GATE:** The GUI ADR records measured 24 MP pan/zoom/rotate frame times (median, p95) for Tauri 2.x and the Slint smoke on Windows (Surface), macOS and Linux (Ubuntu 24.04, Fedora, Wayland and X11, NVIDIA and Intel, real touchscreen); each cell PASS, FAIL or UNMEASURED.
- [ ] **GATE:** The B8 rule is applied once before any UI and its outcome recorded: Tauri confirmed (Linux touch best-effort, limits documented), Slint triggered (owner waiver needed), or Tauri failing on Windows or macOS (no UI code, owner asked).
- [ ] **GATE:** 500-image tile serving scrolls at >= 30 fps (PROVISIONAL), IPC p95 (tile, Channel) is recorded, webview caches hold no tile bytes, and all folder-drop fixtures (symlink loop, OneDrive placeholder, SMB share, 10k files, long path, Unicode) pass without hang or crash on three OSes.
- [ ] **GATE:** The ort vs rten vs tract ADR records measured 256x256 MobileNetV3+FPN-class latency, cold-load, binary size, op coverage, the int8 check method with a smoke result, and the macOS x86_64 and Windows arm64 story; ONE backend is chosen behind a trait.
- [x] **GATE:** Decode-only libheif >= 1.23.5 plus libde265 build as separate shared libraries on `windows-2025`, `macos-latest` and `ubuntu-22.04` via CMake, and `cargo xtask check-native` proves no x265 or x264 symbols and no GPL libraries are linked.
- [ ] **GATE:** turbojpeg builds on three OSes with libjpeg-turbo >= 3.1.4 and a CI version assertion; the Lanczos ADR records speed and memory at 12 MP for kornia-imgproc f32 vs own strip-wise u8/u16 against the OpenCV oracle (PROVISIONAL: PSNR >= 45 dB, <= 90 ms at 8 threads).
- [ ] **GATE:** The sandbox ADR records per-OS feasibility (job object + restricted token or AppContainer, Landlock + seccomp, `sandbox_init`) with escape-test results, the achieved level per OS and the M6, M8 and M9 work implied.
- [x] **GATE:** The 8:1 long-strip failure rate per aspect bucket (4:1, 6:1, 8:1, 10:1) is recorded as an M4 input, the ML part marked dev-only or skipped per the MakeACopy licence outcome.
- [ ] **GATE:** Writing plans are approved for the HEVC legal read, the private golden set (consent and redaction) and model provenance, and the MakeACopy DocQuadNet weights licence has an answer (a reply, or a documented no-reply after 14 days).
- [ ] **GATE:** Tier-M is defined and the Apple-silicon access decision made; >= 3 real files per size class of the 12/48/100 MP corpus logged with licences (gaps recorded); the HEIC plan is merged; the golden-set dry run publishes aggregates only and the fork-PR test exposes no secret.
- [ ] **GATE:** In the scratch repo an App-created tag triggers `release.yml` and the draft, upload-all, publish flow works under immutable releases with `SHA256SUMS`, `gh attestation verify` and a CycloneDX SBOM; a fresh devcontainer builds with `cargo xtask doctor` green.
- [ ] **GATE:** Every spike has an ADR with a go/no-go line, PLAN.md is consistent with them, the A-4 gate-miss question is asked of the owner, and AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md and CONTRIBUTING carry the ticking rule with `roadmap-check` required.

## M1 - Measurement harness and core engine skeleton

<!-- ms: version=none | platforms=CI only (Windows, macOS, Linux) | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Build the benchmark and accuracy harness, the synthetic corpus generator, the first private golden set (v0) and the UI-agnostic core data model and kernels, so every later gate is a number this harness produces rather than a feature checklist.
**Release:** none (no version tag, no artifacts; all crates stay `0.0.0` with `publish = false`); CI-only on Windows, macOS and Linux runners plus the maintainer's machines.
**Depends on:** M0 (workspace, `xtask`, CI skeleton, `cargo-deny`, warp ADR M0.38, inference ADR M0.35, libjpeg-turbo pin M0.44/M0.45, golden-set policy M0.50-M0.53); where an M0 artefact is missing, the M1 item that needs it creates it.
**Size:** XL (definition in X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL).
**Design:** [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md) (harness, corpus, budgets), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md) (data model, engine skeleton), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md) (decoders, limits, EXIF), [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md) (warp, line detector), [PLAN 5](docs/plan/05-enhancement.md) (feasibility spike), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md) (CI, security); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md).
**Exit gate:** G0: `eval` runs end to end on >= 5,000 synthetic images (PROVISIONAL) and once on the locked private golden v0 (>= 150 images, >= 25 per slice), publishing aggregate JSON only, and a 200-image smoke gate blocks PRs; every kernel benchmark and budget row is measured on real 12/48/100 MP files on a Tier-M x86 laptop and Apple silicon. A missed gate is never relaxed silently (A-4).
**Risks / spikes:**
- Synthetic-to-real gap and labelling labour: synthetic data only detects regressions and never backs the B6 silent-failure claim; golden capture (M1.39) starts in week 1 because labelling is the critical path; slices with n < 80 stay advisory.
- Wall-clock benchmarks on shared runners are noisy: only gungraun instruction counts gate PRs, wall-clock runs nightly on one stable machine and never blocks; the analysis budget row is a labelled STAND-IN (M1.55) until M2 and M4.
- Two time-boxed spikes: LSD port versus EDLines (M1.28) and enhancement feasibility on golden receipts (M1.65-M1.68, n <= 40, so the "no receipt benchmark" risk is only partly retired).

### Core
- [ ] **M1.01 Core crate boundary** - Extends M0.30: `crates/core` is model and ports only (`serde`, `thiserror`; no I/O, threads, `rayon`, OS APIs) with `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]`; the M0.30 `check-deps` guard enforces it.
- [ ] **M1.02 SourceRef and SourceId** - `SourceId` = blake3 of the whole file (PLAN 2.3), streamed and computed lazily, inside `SourceRef` (path, size, mtime, dims, orientation, icc). Tests: stable across rename and mtime change; same-size files differing in the middle get different ids.
- [ ] **M1.03 Geometry model** - `Geometry::{Identity, Quad(QuadWarp), Grid(Arc<GridWarp>)}`; `QuadWarp` holds four f32 corners (0..1 EXIF-oriented, may exceed) plus the item's turns and mirror; `GridWarp` is an opaque serde payload (M12.27). Tests: 1e-4 px round trip at 12k px; EXIF 1-8; serde.
- [ ] **M1.04 EditState v1** - `EditState {orientation (Identity/convert-only), items, margin = PaperEdge (B14), enhance = Original (B13)}`, `Item {id, include, geometry, enhance_override, origin, confidence}`; `render_hash()` skips provenance. Tests: corner change alters it, provenance does not.
- [ ] **M1.05 Schema migrations and snapshots** - `migrate(serde_json::Value) -> Result<EditState, ErrKind>` as pure `v(n) -> v(n+1)` fns; a newer schema gives `SchemaTooNew`, never data loss; `fixtures/editstate/v1_*.json`, a test-only v2 shim, `insta` snapshots. Acceptance: fixtures round-trip.
- [ ] **M1.06 OutputSpec** - PLAN 2.3 type outside `EditState`: `colour Srgb|PreserveWideGamut` (sRGB default only for HEIC to JPG and enhanced output), `collision Rename|Skip|Replace`, `verify Full|Fast`, target InPlace or Copy; no backup switch. Property test: no value skips backup or verify.
- [ ] **M1.07 History and SessionCmd** - `commit()` replaces the top entry on a matching `GestureId`, else truncates redo and pushes; unchanged `render_hash` dropped; cap 200 per image; `SessionCmd` command/memento. Tests: proptest `undo(redo(x)) == x`, bounds, cap. _(gate: proptest green)_
- [ ] **M1.08 Core ports** - In `core`: `Raster`/`RasterView` (Gray8, Rgb8, Gray16, Rgb16; `est_bytes()`), `Probe`, `Want`, `DecodeLimits`; traits `Decoder` (bytes, never paths), `Encoder`, `Renderer`, `InferenceBackend`, `HeicBackend` with stubs. Acceptance: mocks, object-safety compile test.
- [ ] **M1.09 ErrKind and CancelToken** - `ErrKind` via `thiserror` (names per PLAN 2.10; M2.05 extends) with `user_message_key()` (B20); hierarchical `CancelToken` (batch, job, stage) with generation, deadline, `check_band(row)`. Tests: every variant has a string; parent cancels children only.
- [ ] **M1.10 MemoryBudget** - `engine` skeleton: weighted semaphore, cap min(25% RAM, 4 GiB, 50% free RAM), job weight pixels x 9 + 64 MiB, `acquire(bytes, &CancelToken)`, FIFO; an oversize job runs alone. Tests: oversize completes in 5 s; 8-thread stress stays under the cap. M2.03 wires it.
- [ ] **M1.11 Local logging** - `tracing` with a daily `tracing-appender` file in the OS data dir (7 days, 20 MB; PROVISIONAL); paths redacted above `debug` to `blake3(path)[..8]` plus extension; no network sink (B18, C4). Tests: info-level decode log has no raw path; rotation under a fake clock.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M1.12 Format sniffing and header probe** - `sniff(&[u8])` by magic bytes (JPEG, PNG, TIFF, WebP decodable; HEIC/AVIF, GIF, BMP, JXL recognised only) and `probe()` for dims, depth, frames, orientation, ICC, no pixel allocation. Tests: >= 30 fixtures; a 100 MP-declared file probes in < 1 MB.
- [ ] **M1.13 DecodeLimits** - PLAN 3.10.1: 100 MP default (500 MP via Advanced, `--max-pixels` or "allow this file"), file bytes, metadata 16 MiB, scans 100, frames 10,000; order probe, cap, `est_bytes`, `image::Limits`. One test per field; a 60000x60000 header fails in < 1 s and < 64 MB.
- [ ] **M1.14 Decode guard** - `guard_item` wraps decode, warp, encode: a panic becomes `InternalPanic` and the batch continues; `max_decode_ms` gives `DecodeTimeout` but the `MemoryBudget` hold lasts until the thread ends. Tests: panic then next item OK; pools install a `panic_handler`.
- [ ] **M1.15 JPEG and PNG decode** - `image` 0.25.x with zune-jpeg 0.5.15 (not the 0.5.16 RC) and `png` to `Raster`; fixtures incl. progressive, CMYK/YCCK (RGB or `UnsupportedFeature`, never a panic), 12-bit, interlaced. Acceptance: mean abs diff vs libjpeg-turbo <= 1.5 LSB (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M1.16 TIFF and WebP decode** - `tiff` (8/16-bit; LZW, Deflate, PackBits; IFD count capped; multi-page = first page plus `tiff.multi_page`) and `image-webp` (animated = first frame plus `anim.first_frame_only`). Acceptance: a libtiff-made 1-bit G4 TIFF reads or the failure is recorded.
- [ ] **M1.17 EXIF orientation and ICC** - Orientation 1-8 (JPEG, TIFF, WebP, PNG) applied once at decode, tag kept on `SourceRef`; ICC kept (JPEG APP2 multi-segment, PNG, TIFF, WebP), no conversion; HEIF half: M6.25. Tests: 8 orientations x 4 formats match one reference; 3-segment ICC byte-exact.
- [ ] **M1.18 turbojpeg feature switch** - Extends M0.44/M0.45: enable `turbojpeg` 1.5.x behind cargo feature `turbojpeg` in `codecs` (off = zune-jpeg only) on the pinned libjpeg-turbo >= 3.1.4; no new ADR. Acceptance: the CI version check is red with 3.1.0, green with the pin on 3 OSes.
- [ ] **M1.19 turbojpeg scaled decode** - `Want::Scaled{min_edge}` picks the smallest of 1/8, 1/4, 1/2, 1 keeping the long edge >= `min_edge`; EXIF orientation applies after scaling. Tests: on 40 fixtures scaled vs full decode + area downscale >= 35 dB PSNR (PROVISIONAL); odd sizes like 4001x3001.
- [ ] **M1.20 JPEG lossless transform** - `transform(bytes, Op)`: rotate, flip, crop (`perfect`/`trim`); the crop snaps to the iMCU grid and returns the realised rectangle and a `perfect` flag. Tests: matches decode-then-transform if aligned; 1000 reused buffers ASan-clean; Orientation untouched.
- [ ] **M1.21 Baseline encoders** - `Encoder` impls for JPEG (turbojpeg q90 and `jpeg-encoder` with IJG attribution; default picked from M1.57) and PNG (`png`, 8/16-bit, dpi) for the harness and stage-sum only, not the M2 write path. Tests: JPEG round trip >= 40 dB at q90; 16-bit PNG bit-exact.

### Imaging kernels
- [ ] **M1.22 Proxy pyramid and tiles** - `imgproc::pyramid`: one decode to 256 px thumbnail, 1024 px detection proxy, ~1.5 MP analysis proxy (long edge <= 3072 px), 2-4 MP display proxy, never upscaling; `tiles(level, 512)`. Tests: tiles rebuild a level bit-exactly; same bytes at 1/8 threads.
- [ ] **M1.23 Homography solver** - `imgproc::homography`: normalised (Hartley) DLT in f64, inverse, point mapping, degeneracy check (duplicate or collinear corners give `ErrKind::Degenerate`). Tests: proptest round trip < 1e-9; matches `cv2.getPerspectiveTransform` fixtures within 1e-6.
- [ ] **M1.24 Strip-wise Lanczos3 warp** - `imgproc::warp` per the M0.38 ADR: Lanczos3 (Rgb8, Gray8, 16-bit), rayon over 64-row bands, no full-image f32 copy, `CancelToken` per band. Tests: cancel within 2 band-times; 100 MP extra heap <= output + 16 MB + bands; same bytes at 1 and 8 threads.
- [ ] **M1.25 Warp oracles** - Identity and 90-degree turns exact; sub-pixel shifts within 1 LSB of a NumPy Lanczos3 reference; PSNR >= 45 dB vs `cv2.warpPerspective` (fixtures checked in; PROVISIONAL); SSIM references via `image-compare` (>= 0.99 same OS, >= 0.98 across; never `dssim-core`).
- [ ] **M1.26 Warp minification guard** - Above local scale 1.5 (PROVISIONAL) warp from the nearest pre-reduced pyramid level, as a 6x6 Lanczos footprint aliases. Tests: a zone plate at 2x and 4x stays within 3 dB alias energy of an area average (PROVISIONAL); no level-switch seam (<= 2 LSB).
- [ ] **M1.27 Threshold kernels** - `imgproc::threshold`: Otsu, Sauvola, NICK with u64 strip integrals, memory O(width x window) not ~192 MB at 12 MP. Tests: equals naive versions; constant-255 images above the u32 limits do not overflow; within 0.5 F-measure of Doxa on 3 DIBCO images.

### Detection
- [ ] **M1.28 LSD decision: port or EDLines** - 3-day ADR (`docs/adr/NNNN-lsd.md`): port the OpenCV `lsd.cpp` only from OpenCV >= 4.5.4 with recorded provenance (never the original LSD source) or EDLines; compare recall, false segments, ms, effort on 1024 px proxies. Decision only; code is M2.11.
- [ ] **M1.29 Baseline adapters** - Dev-only adapters: `tools/baselines/cv_quad.py` (written from scratch), ImageMagick `-deskew`, `unpaper` (GPL-2; external process, never linked or bundled). Acceptance: per-slice table in `docs/perf/baselines.md`; oracle > cv_quad > full-frame on tilt > 30 deg.

### Corpus and labelling
- [ ] **M1.30 Synthetic generator** - `tools/synth/` (Python, hashed `requirements.lock`, Augraphy 8.2.6 behind a `degrade.py` seam; AlbumentationsX banned, AGPL): `python -m synth --seed S --count N`, `manifest.jsonl` v1. Acceptance: the same seed gives identical manifests and hashes on Linux.
- [ ] **M1.31 Known-text pages and receipts** - OFL fonts, public-domain text, generated line items (prices, decimals), barcodes and QR; Letter/A4 pages and receipts incl. aspect > 4:1 and > 8:1, plus transcripts and renders. Acceptance: Tesseract 5 CER <= 5% on the clean render (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M1.32 Camera geometry, ground truth** - Pinhole homography (pitch/yaw to +-45 deg; tagged partial framing and curl), analytic ground truth from the f64 matrix. Acceptance: inverse warp matches the clean render at SSIM >= 0.98 (PROVISIONAL); CI fails if `tools/synth` imports app code.
- [ ] **M1.33 Backgrounds and degradations** - CC0 or procedural backgrounds only (DTD excluded; licence in the manifest); clutter, shadows, colour casts, blur, noise, JPEG q40-95, thermal fade; lighting and clutter tags. Acceptance: tag histogram meets quotas; 100-image contact sheet reviewed.
- [ ] **M1.34 Format, EXIF and ICC variants** - JPEG, PNG, TIFF, WebP outputs; orientation 1-8 variants with pre-rotated pixels so ground truth stays valid; sRGB and Display P3 variants. Acceptance: the M1 decoders reproduce the upright reference for every variant (feeds M1.17).
- [ ] **M1.35 Synthetic suites and splits** - `xtask synth --suite smoke|full`: full >= 5,000 images, every slice cell >= 200 (rare slices 150-500; PROVISIONAL), never committed; smoke = 200 images, <= 5 MB archive, no Git LFS; `xtask check-splits` fails when a `scene_id` spans two splits.
- [ ] **M1.36 fetch-corpus framework** - `xtask fetch-corpus [--sample] <name>`: `corpus.lock.toml` records URL, size, SHA-256, SPDX licence, attribution; verified before use, refused on mismatch; cache outside the repo, never vendored. Test: a local HTTP fixture serving bad downloads.
- [ ] **M1.37 SmartDoc and CORD adapters** - SmartDoc 2015 Ch.1 (CC BY 4.0; cite, email the organisers; 21 MB sample first) with every Nth frame per clip and clip-level `scene_id`; CORD (CC BY 4.0) transcripts for CER. Acceptance: manifests validate; 20 quads checked on a contact sheet.
- [ ] **M1.38 MIDV, DIBCO, raw.pixls.us adapters** - MIDV-500 quads (licence checked per release); DIBCO fetch-only with binary ground truth for threshold tests; raw.pixls.us filtered to CC0-only. Acceptance: manifests validate; a mixed-licence fixture tests the CC0 filter.
- [ ] **M1.39 Golden v0 capture plan** - Capture >= 150 images, >= 25 per M0.51 slice (long receipts, thermal-fade, partial-frame, touching, phone photos, flatbed single/multi, EXIF photos, negatives; tags overlap), incl. >= 40 receipts. Acceptance: `xtask golden-status` prints quota counts.
- [ ] **M1.40 Label schema and validator** - `xtask check-labels` validates `labels/<image_id>.json` against the M0.51 schema (quad, top edge, flags, slice tags); rejects non-finite quads and missing tags; labels live in the private repo, images on the runner disk. Tests: valid/invalid fixtures.
- [ ] **M1.41 Blank-quad labeller** - Label Studio (local) or a minimal `tools/labeler/` (lockfile, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`, licence allow-list; not `ui/`) on 127.0.0.1 with a per-run token, no-store, private profile; blank quad, never a model suggestion. Acceptance: 10 images at median <= 60 s.
- [ ] **M1.42 Label golden v0** - The maintainer labels every image with no model output visible; `check-labels` green; median labelling time logged to plan v1 (>= 500) and v2 (>= 800 locked). Acceptance: >= 150 labelled images meeting the M1.39 quotas.
- [ ] **M1.43 Second annotator and noise floor** - A second annotator labels a random 20% (>= 30 images) blind; `eval noise-floor` reports IoU and corner error between annotators (median, p95); no target is tighter than the p95 disagreement. Fallback: own re-label after >= 7 days (weaker).
- [ ] **M1.44 Lock splits** - `golden/splits.lock.json` (private repo): scene-disjoint dev/locked splits (PROVISIONAL 30/70), SHA-256 per image and label, append-only `eval-log.jsonl`; never tuned or trained on. Acceptance: `check-splits` green; editing a locked label turns the private check red.
- [ ] **M1.85 Golden storage, backup and restore** - Images live only on the maintainer's encrypted disk (A-8), outside AI-tool-readable folders, with an encrypted backup of images and labels on a second disk (no cloud). Acceptance: a restore test matches every SHA-256 in `splits.lock.json`.

### Quality/Perf: accuracy harness
- [ ] **M1.45 eval crate and Predictor** - `crates/eval` (bin `auto-crop-eval`: `run`, `compare`, `noise-floor`; wrapped by `xtask eval`): `Predictor` trait with a JSON-lines adapter, `FullFrame`; missing or crashed predictions count as failures. Acceptance: runs the 200-image smoke set.
- [ ] **M1.46 Geometry and skew metrics** - IoU after canonical warp (own homography and clipper, independent of `imgproc`); corner error in % of diagonal; skew p50/p95/p99; failure at IoU < 0.90, success at >= 0.95/0.98; orientation confusion. Tests: analytic cases, shapely to 1e-6.
- [ ] **M1.47 Slices and statistics** - Slices (lighting, clutter, tilt, aspect, format) with worst-slice gating; n < 30 never public, n < 80 advisory; one-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson silent-failure bound (PLAN 7.4), bootstrap CI for mean IoU; `eval compare`. Tests: SciPy fixtures to 1e-6.
- [ ] **M1.48 Risk-coverage curve and ECE stub** - Risk-coverage curve, reliability diagram (10 equal-mass bins), ECE, Brier, AUROC vs failure; reported, not gated until M4. Tests: a calibrated predictor gives ECE near 0; an overconfident one matches the analytic ECE.
- [ ] **M1.49 Determinism and self-validation** - Results byte-identical across runs and at 1 and 8 threads; header records commit and host. Every-PR self-check: oracle IoU 1.0, 0 failures; jittered oracle on the analytic curve; `FullFrame` = area fraction; crashes counted.
- [ ] **M1.50 Per-PR smoke gate** - `eval compare` on the 200-image smoke set against `main` fails a PR at -0.3 pt mean IoU or +0.5 pt failure rate; an `accuracy-waiver` label overrides; Linux, forks too, no secrets. Mutation test: jittered fails, identical passes. _(gate: smoke)_
- [ ] **M1.51 Private golden workflow** - Private repo `auto-crop-golden` (no images) with a self-hosted runner attached to it only; it evaluates the dev and locked sets and a reviewed publisher pushes allow-listed aggregates to `metrics`. Acceptance: one dispatched run publishes v0 aggregates.
- [ ] **M1.83 Golden run isolation** - A hosted runner builds the public SHA (no data, no token); only the built `auto-crop-eval` runs beside the images, in a network-less container, set read-only, no third-party actions. Acceptance: a planted `build.rs` reading the set or opening a socket fails.
- [ ] **M1.84 Golden triggers and token** - Nightly (dev tier), `workflow_dispatch` (locked set, once per RC; a non-main SHA after a recorded diff review) and release `repository_dispatch`; never push, PR or fork. Dispatch token: fine-grained, private repo only, used from main or tag workflows.
- [ ] **M1.52 Publishing guard and fork test** - `PublishableMetrics` has no per-image field; publish only from main nightlies and releases, slices n >= 30 only, contact sheets stay local. Acceptance: leak and small-slice negative tests fail; a scratch-fork PR re-runs M0.53 with the real workflow.

### Quality/Perf: benchmarks
- [ ] **M1.53 Perf corpus builder** - `xtask make-bench-images`: real 12, 24, 48, 100 MP JPEGs (q85-95, 4:2:0/4:4:4, baseline and progressive) developed from CC0 raw.pixls.us RAWs or own captures; sizes with no CC0 source are recorded gaps (M0.48's public JPEGs bridge); SHA-256 recorded.
- [ ] **M1.54 Stage spans and skeleton** - `engine::skeleton` (`auto-crop dev-pipeline`) chains read_probe, decode, proxy, analyse (stand-in), rectify, enhance (prototype), encode with budget-table spans and `stage_done{stage, ms, px}` (`--timings`). Tests: span order; same bytes at 1/8 threads.
- [ ] **M1.55 Analysis stand-ins** - Time equivalent work labelled STAND-IN until M2/M4: (a) a public 256x256 MobileNetV3-class net (random weights) via the M0.35 ADR runtime; (b) Canny and contours on the 1024 px proxy with `imageproc`; the budget table shows a separate stand-in column.
- [ ] **M1.56 Decode and resize benchmarks** - criterion 0.8: turbojpeg (full, scaled) vs zune-jpeg, plus PNG/TIFF/WebP; `fast_image_resize` vs `image::imageops::resize` and `pic-scale`, with PSNR/SSIM. ADR `docs/adr/NNNN-codec-kernel-choices.md` picks the decoder, resizer and linear-light rule.
- [ ] **M1.57 Kernel and encode benchmarks** - criterion: warp from 12/48/100 MP to 2480x3508 and to a heavy-minification output (M2.15 gate: <= 90 ms at 8 threads); Otsu, Sauvola, NICK at windows 31/51/101; JPEG q90 (turbojpeg vs `jpeg-encoder`) and PNG encode; 1 and N threads.
- [ ] **M1.58 gungraun gate** - gungraun 0.20 (Valgrind, Linux only) on small fixed inputs for decode, resize, warp, threshold kernels per SIMD level (scalar, SSE4.1, AVX2); `main` and PR head in one job; > 5% worse blocks, 2% warns. Acceptance: an injected 10% regression fails. _(gate: > 5%)_
- [ ] **M1.59 Nightly wall-clock** - hyperfine (`--warmup 3 --runs 20`) on the dev-pipeline for 12/48/100 MP on the Tier-M laptop as the private repo's self-hosted runner (AC power; binary built on a hosted runner); an issue opens when p50 or peak RSS regresses > 10%; never gates a PR.
- [ ] **M1.60 Memory profile and batch throughput** - DHAT or heaptrack peak heap at 12/48/100 MP vs 3x decoded RGB8 + 64 MB (175 MB, 500 MB, 1.0 GB; PROVISIONAL); 200 x 12 MP batch on cores - 1 workers under `MemoryBudget` at 1-8 workers: images/s, scaling efficiency (70% checked), peak RSS.
- [ ] **M1.61 Tier-M x86 baseline** - Full matrix (decode, resize, warp, threshold, encode, dev-pipeline, batch) on the Windows Tier-M machine (6C/12T AVX2, 16 GB, NVMe, AC; record the model) with a host fingerprint JSON; published as `host.tier = M` on `metrics`.
- [ ] **M1.62 Baseline on Apple silicon** - The same matrix on an M-series Mac (macOS arm64, NEON paths); if only the shared `macos-latest` runner (3 vCPU M1, 7 GB) is available, the result is indicative, flagged noisy and never a gate.
- [ ] **M1.63 Measured budget table** - `docs/perf/budgets.md`: measured stage p50/p95 vs PROVISIONAL budgets (700 ms p50 and analysis <= 40 ms p95 on T1 exits, >= 4 img/s on 6 cores, overlay <= 150 ms) plus stage-sum check; > 1.5x over budget needs a redesign; unmeasured rows stay PROVISIONAL.
- [ ] **M1.64 Metrics branch and dashboard** - Orphan `metrics` branch (`auto-crop-metrics/1`) and a uPlot Pages site: per-slice trends, latency vs failure Pareto, stage times vs budget, gate table; built by the single `pages.yml` created here (M9.19, M13.77 extend it); aggregates only.

### Enhancement
- [ ] **M1.65 Flatten spike** - Throwaway `spikes/enhance/`: ~1.5 MP proxy, per-block 85-90th percentile luma, barcode/black-band blocks inpainted, gain <= 3x. Acceptance: residual shading <= 3% of paper on synthetic gradients (PROVISIONAL); no barcode ghosts.
- [ ] **M1.66 Tesseract oracle** - Dev-only `tools/ocr_oracle/` runs Tesseract 5.x (version recorded, settings fixed and logged, notably `thresholding_method`) and computes CER and numeric-token accuracy. Acceptance: clean synthetic receipts CER <= 5% (PROVISIONAL); a blurred copy is worse.
- [ ] **M1.67 Transcribe golden receipts** - Hand-transcribe >= 30 of the >= 40 golden receipts (PROVISIONAL), covering > 4:1, thermal-fade and partial-frame cases, every amount and decimal point exact; transcripts stay private; 20% checked by a second reader.
- [ ] **M1.68 Enhance sweep** - On rectified golden receipts: grey vs flatten + Otsu/Sauvola/NICK, windows 21-201 (PROVISIONAL), no-flatten ablation; CER change, share worse > 1 pt. ADR `docs/adr/NNNN-enhance-feasibility.md` (aggregates only) names the M7 default or "inconclusive". _(gate: CER)_

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M1.69 Hostile-file corpus** - `xtask make-hostile` writes bombs and corruptions (60000x60000 and 100 MP headers, zlib bomb, IFD floods, truncations, cyclic EXIF); each runs in a subprocess and returns an `ErrKind` or bounded decode, 3 OSes per PR. _(gate: 0 panics, aborts, hangs)_
- [ ] **M1.70 cargo-fuzz targets** - `fuzz/` (cargo-fuzz 0.13, nightly, Linux only): `probe`, `limits`, `metadata` (EXIF, ICC), `editstate_json` (parse and migrate never panic); seeds from fixtures and hostile files; ASan on turbojpeg targets. Acceptance: 5 minutes per target without a crash.
- [ ] **M1.71 Fuzz CI and crash replay** - 60 s smoke per target on PRs touching `codecs`, `core` or `fuzz/` (path filter inside the job) and 1 h per target nightly (counts toward >= 72 h per target before 1.0); crashers become plain `#[test]` inputs on 3 OSes. _(gate: fuzz smoke green)_
- [ ] **M1.72 CI policy guards** - Extends M0.31 (its `check-profiles` owns the `panic = "abort"` ban): `xtask ci-guards` adds `unsafe` only inside `ffi/` and `simd/` with `// SAFETY:` comments and no HTTP, TLS or socket crates in shipped crates (B18, C4). Acceptance: each guard has a red test.
- [ ] **M1.73 Licence gates for M1 deps** - `cargo deny check` green for the M1 crates (`image`, `fast_image_resize`, `turbojpeg`, `blake3`, `image-compare`, bench/test tools); bans confirmed (`dssim-core`, `heic`, `jpegxl-rs`, x264/x265, `purecv`, `opencv`); IJG notices in THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.
- [ ] **M1.74 Provenance register and data policy** - `docs/provenance.md` lists every tool, font, dataset and weight in use with SPDX licence, use and source (datasets, weights: models-repo log) plus an excluded list; CONTRIBUTING gains the B21 rule: no real photos in PRs, aggregate-only metrics.

### Model supply (bootstrap)
- [ ] **M1.75 Training repo bootstrap** - Create `WelFedTed/auto-crop-models` (MIT OR Apache-2.0 code, OSI-only weights, `provenance/`, `manifests/`, manifest CI; no app code, no golden data). The training-vs-golden overlap check runs in the private `auto-crop-golden`, red on a planted id.
- [ ] **M1.76 Data manifests** - One manifest per candidate source in the models repo (SPDX licence, URL, SHA-256, uses, status): SmartDoc, CORD, synthetic set `cleared`; UVDoc/Doc3D textures, DTD, DocAligner `blocked`. DocQuadNet-256 status is read from M0.26; if unresolved, train from scratch.
- [ ] **M1.77 $0 compute plan** - Training-repo plan: local GPU, else free-tier notebooks (verify quotas), nothing paid (B15); a timed 1-epoch run of a small corner-heatmap net on the synthetic set estimates GPU-hours for M4 and records the ONNX export path (PROVISIONAL; names the machine).

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M1.78 Cross-OS lanes** - windows-2025, macos-latest (arm64), ubuntu-22.04 run nextest for all M1 crates; numeric thresholds enforced on Linux, parity on the others (<= 0.2 pt mean IoU); ARM64 (Windows, Linux) and Intel-Mac lanes best-effort until 1.0; lane <= 15 min (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M1.79 Dev toolchain** - Extends M0.11/M0.39: `doctor` also checks Valgrind, Python 3 (hashed `requirements.lock`), Tesseract 5.x, ImageMagick, unpaper and prints install commands without installing; `.devcontainer/` gains them. Acceptance: a fresh devcontainer runs smoke and nextest.
- [ ] **M1.80 Workflow hardening** - New workflows use SHA-pinned actions, least-privilege `permissions`, no `pull_request_target`, zizmor clean; required jobs always report (path filter in the job); crates `0.0.0`, no release workflow. Acceptance: docs-only PR green; a test tag creates nothing.
- [ ] **M1.81 Nightly public suites** - Linux nightly runs `eval` on the >= 5,000-image synthetic suite and the SmartDoc, CORD, MIDV samples, publishes to `metrics`, opens an issue on a regression past the smoke thresholds; non-blocking. Acceptance: two nightlies give identical synthetic numbers.

### Docs
- [ ] **M1.82 Developer guide** - `docs/dev/eval-harness.md` and `docs/dev/benchmarking.md`: running suites locally and on fork PRs, metric definitions incl. silent failure (PLAN 7.4), slices, predictor protocol, noise rules. Acceptance: a newcomer reproduces the smoke result from the guide alone.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** `eval` runs end to end on >= 5,000 synthetic images (PROVISIONAL) with app-independent exact ground truth and reports IoU after canonical warp, corner error, skew p50/p95/p99, failure rate at IoU < 0.90 and per-slice tables, byte-identical at 1 and 8 threads.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** Private golden v0 has >= 150 hand-labelled real images, >= 25 per slice and >= 40 receipts, a locked scene-disjoint split passing `check-splits`, and an annotator noise floor from a second annotator on >= 20% of the images.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** The private workflow evaluates golden v0 once and publishes aggregate JSON only: leak test green, scratch-fork PR shows the private job skipped and secrets empty, and a planted `build.rs` that reads the golden path or opens a socket fails.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** The 200-image smoke gate blocks a degraded predictor at -0.3 pt mean IoU or +0.5 pt failure rate and passes an identical one; public suites run in CI, the Pages dashboard is live and ECE reads near 0 on a synthetic calibrated predictor.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** Benchmarks cover decode, resize, warp and threshold on real 12, 48, 100 MP files: criterion trends recorded, gungraun (> 5%) fails an injected 10% regression on Linux CI, the nightly wall-clock job has run, Tier-M x86 and Apple-silicon baselines recorded.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** `docs/perf/budgets.md` holds measured p50 and p95 for every stage that exists, publishes the stage-sum check against the 700 ms p50 budget (PROVISIONAL), and labels every unmeasured row (stand-in analysis, commit, IPC, HEIC) PROVISIONAL.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** `EditState` v1 round-trips all fixtures with migration and insta snapshot tests passing and proptest proving `undo(redo(x)) == x`; the hostile-file corpus (100 MP bombs, truncated files) gives 0 panics, aborts or hangs on 3 OSes; fuzz smoke is green on Linux CI.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** Enhancement feasibility is recorded on golden receipts (flatten + Otsu vs Sauvola vs NICK, window sweep to >= 151 px, Tesseract 5 CER with thresholding fixed) with a named default for M7 or an explicit "inconclusive", and the "no receipt benchmark" risk entry says n <= 40.
- [ ] **GATE (G0):** Licence and policy guards are green on all three OS lanes (`cargo deny` bans, no-network, unsafe and `panic = "abort"` guards, libjpeg-turbo >= 3.1.4 check), and the provenance register and model manifests list a licence for every dataset, font, tool and weight in use.

## M2 - Classical pipeline, safe writes and CLI

<!-- ms: version=v0.1.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=L -->

**Goal:** Ship the first public preview: a headless CLI with a classical detect, crop, rotate, deskew and perspective pipeline and the complete overwrite-with-backup safety stack (B3, A-1), honest about accuracy (per-slice numbers, confidence labelled uncalibrated) and starting the SignPath clock. Deliberately absent: ML, HEIC, enhancement, multi-item splitting (held), content-tight toggle.
**Release:** `v0.1.0` (preview), Windows 10 22H2 and 11 x64 with AVX2 (C1), CLI only, unsigned; artifacts (names PROVISIONAL): `auto-crop-0.1.0-windows-x64.zip` (portable, static CRT), `SHA256SUMS`, build attestation, CycloneDX SBOM, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES`; macOS and Linux build in CI but publish nothing (B9).
**Depends on:** M1 (3-OS CI, `xtask` corpus/synth/eval, synthetic generator, eval crate, criterion and gungraun harness, Tier-M baseline, golden v0, codec and kernel owners M1.12-1.26, `MemoryBudget` M1.10) and the M0 decisions (warp choice, libjpeg-turbo pin M0.44/0.45).
**Size:** L (5-8 part-time weeks; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md); decisions in [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md).
**Exit gate:** G1 (first Windows preview): classical detector mean IoU >= 0.95 with <= 8% failures on synthetic, golden v0 published per slice (n >= 30), >= 1,000 random kills and the restore matrix 100% byte-identical, lossless and EXIF-once exact, speed and memory inside budget (all PROVISIONAL), then v0.1.0 verified on clean VMs with the SignPath application submitted. A missed gate is never relaxed silently (A-4).
**Risks / spikes:**
- Overwrite-by-default CLI (A-1) is a data-loss hazard: backup before swap, verify, journal recovery, one-time notice, `--dry-run` and the M2.58/M2.59 gates; every threshold is PROVISIONAL until M2.52 and M2.56.
- Classical-only accuracy on cluttered backgrounds will look weak: per-slice numbers (n >= 30) are published, confidence is uncalibrated, Strict is the default, a high held share is by design (B4).
- Spikes: Windows swap primitive and Store-MSIX virtualisation (M2.28); unverified: Smart App Control on an unsigned CLI (M2.77); antivirus sharing violations are retried and injected (M2.31, M2.58).

### Core
- [ ] **M2.01 Preset table** - `engine::presets` (versioned TOML): `receipt`, `document`, `photo`, `flatbed`, `convert-only` with route order, aspect snap, per-route margins (A-11), deskew mode, default triage; `convert-only` disables geometry. Acceptance: `insta` snapshot per preset.
- [ ] **M2.02 Transform composer** - Compose quarter-turn/mirror (EXIF applied at decode), rotation, perspective and the per-route margin (quad 0 + 1 px inward, bed +0.5% up to 12 px, border trim 0) into one f64 homography. Acceptance: one resample per output; >= 40 dB PSNR vs an f64 reference.
- [ ] **M2.03 Batch scheduler and MemoryBudget** - `engine::BatchScheduler`: rayon pool of cores - 1, below-normal priority, admission via M1.10 `MemoryBudget` (oversize job runs alone). Acceptance: cap held except by a lone job, 100 MP admitted on 8 GB, 500 jobs identical on 1, 2, 8 threads.
- [ ] **M2.04 Failure isolation** - Extends M1.14: run job bodies under `guard_item` with a pool `panic_handler`; `panic = "unwind"` stays CI-checked (M0.31). Acceptance: panics injected in decode, analyse, warp, encode over 200 items fail only those items, exit 3, no temp files remain.
- [ ] **M2.05 Error codes** - Extend `ErrKind` with `ReadOnly`, `CloudNotLocal`, `DiskFull`, `FileInUse`, `VerifyFailed`, `SourceChanged`, `BackupFailed`, `AlreadyProcessed`, `NotReplaceable`, `Held`, `MultiItemSuspected`. Acceptance: exhaustive test needs an English string and docs entry per code.
- [ ] **M2.06 Settings and crash log** - `settings.toml` (`schema_version`, safe writer); the CLI reads only store, retention, language, `notice_ack` (A-1); panic hook writes `crash/<ts>-<version>.txt`, no image content or paths. Acceptance: precedence matrix; a forced panic leaks no input path.

### Detection
- [ ] **M2.07 Proxy planes and Canny edge map** - On the M1.22 1024 px proxy add Lab and saturation planes, blur and `kornia-imgproc` Canny (thresholds from the median gradient, 3x3 close). Acceptance: `proxy` <= 25 ms at 12 MP; Canny matches OpenCV on >= 99% of pixels (50 images).
- [ ] **M2.08 Contour quad candidates** - `imageproc` contours, `approximate_polygon_dp` (1-3% epsilon), `min_area_rect`; convex 4-gons ranked by area, edge support, squareness, also on Lab chroma and saturation. Acceptance: truth quad in the top 3 (IoU >= 0.9) for >= 95% of images.
- [ ] **M2.09 Acceptance rules and edge support** - Accept a convex quad covering 5-99.5% of the frame, angles 55-125 deg, edge support >= 0.8 on all four edges; each rejection has a typed code. Acceptance: true quads >= 0.8 for >= 95%, 3%-diagonal shifts < 0.5.
- [ ] **M2.11 Line detector implementation** - Implement the M1.28 choice as `imgproc::lines` (any LSD port only from OpenCV >= 4.5.4, provenance recorded), lazy on borderline or failed contours. Acceptance: >= 90% segment agreement with the OpenCV oracle, <= 15 ms at 1024 px, no shipped `opencv`.
- [ ] **M2.12 Line-group quad fallback** - For an occluded corner or failed contours, cluster segments into four line groups and intersect them into a quad that passes M2.09. Acceptance: recovers >= 60% of contour-rejected quads on the occlusion slice, no extra silent failures (golden fold).
- [ ] **M2.13 Sub-pixel refinement** - Full-resolution parabolic edge peaks, Huber line fits, intersected corners; a corner moving > 1% of the diagonal falls back to the proxy corner and caps confidence. Acceptance: error median <= 2 px, p95 <= 5 px at 12 MP (synthetic); `refine` <= 10 ms.
- [ ] **M2.14 Aspect recovery and snapping** - Extends M1.23: Zhang-He aspect (principal point at centre); snap to A4 1.414, Letter 1.294 or ID-1 1.586 only within 3%, never for `photo`; log `aspect_raw`, `aspect_snap`. Acceptance: median error <= 3% on aspect 1.0-8.0; a 6:1 receipt never snaps.
- [ ] **M2.15 Lanczos3 warp** - Extends M1.24-1.26: wire into the composed transform (premultiplied alpha, 16-bit kept). Acceptance: PSNR >= 45 dB and SSIM >= 0.99 vs OpenCV `warpPerspective`, identical at 1 and 8 threads, `rectify` <= 90 ms at 12 MP on 8 threads, no alpha-edge fringe.
- [ ] **M2.16 Scene triage and flatbed trim** - Route choice (`--preset` overrides); bed-like scans trim to the largest non-bed component plus bed margin; >1 component >= 1% of area is held `MultiItemSuspected`. Acceptance: route accuracy >= 95%, trim IoU >= 0.97, 100% multi-item held.
- [ ] **M2.17 Deskew and photo straighten** - Clean-room port of Leptonica's skew sweep: apply at >= 0.1 deg and peak ratio >= 3; `photo`: segment-angle histogram (>= 40% in one mode) else `suggested_straighten_deg`. Acceptance: angles within 0.05 deg, sparse receipts abstain, `deskew` <= 20 ms.
- [ ] **M2.18 Confidence v0 and held semantics** - Uncalibrated score: Good >= t (Strict 0.95, Balanced 0.90, Aggressive 0.80 until `calibration.json`), Check 0.60 to t, Failed < 0.60 or implausible; only Good is written. Acceptance: AUROC >= 0.85; held files keep SHA-256 and mtime (M2.82), exit 4.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M2.19 Input probe and hard limits** - Extends M1.12-1.13: `max_pixels` 100 MP, `--max-pixels` raises it, 500 MP ceiling; per-job timeout between stages; HEIC, AVIF, JXL, RAW, PDF give `UnsupportedFormat` naming the release, never `Corrupt`. Acceptance: bombs rejected within 1 s and 64 MB.
- [ ] **M2.20 Decoders and pixel formats** - Extends M1.15-1.16: add BMP; multi-page TIFF and animated WebP/APNG read frame 0 with notice `tiff.multi_page` or `anim.first_frame_only`; CMYK, arithmetic, 12-bit JPEG: `UnsupportedFormat`. Acceptance: lossless bit-exact, 12 MP decode <= 120 ms.
- [ ] **M2.22 EXIF orientation written once** - Extends M1.17: every output carries Orientation = 1; the lossless path turns via Transform then patches the tag, never both. Acceptance: 8 orientations x 4 formats x 2 paths all upright, tag 1; no re-turn on reprocess. _(gate: EXIF once)_
- [ ] **M2.23 Metadata and strip-location** - `exif_patch` edits in place (Orientation reset; thumbnail, MPF, gain maps dropped); complete `--strip-location` (EXIF, XMP, IPTC), GPS kept otherwise (C2); ICC byte-exact. Acceptance: 50 fixtures change only intended tags; the flag leaves no GPS byte.
- [ ] **M2.24 Lossless JPEG fast path** - JPEG turn, flip or axis-aligned crop (skew < 0.05 deg) via M1.20: origin snaps outward to the iMCU grid (growth <= 16 px or 0.5%, else re-encode), `perfect` only. Acceptance: scan data byte-exact vs `jpegtran`. _(gate: lossless byte-exact)_
- [ ] **M2.25 JPEG encoder** - turbojpeg primary, `jpeg-encoder` fallback; Balanced clamp(q_est + 5, 80, 95), Small clamp(q_est - 10, 60, 85), Best clamp(q_est + 10, 90, 97) for JPEG sources (M2.80), else q90/q80/q95; optimised Huffman. Acceptance: PSNR >= 40 dB, 12 MP encode <= 110 ms.
- [ ] **M2.26 PNG, TIFF and WebP encoders** - `png`, `tiff` (LZW, DPI kept) and lossless-only `image-webp`, metadata and ICC kept; 1-bit TIFF/PNG and lossy WebP have no writer yet (M7.28/7.29, M11.21): `format.write_unavailable`. Acceptance: round trips bit-exact, `tiffinfo` tags correct.
- [ ] **M2.80 JPEG marker parser and quality estimator** - Own parser (SOF type, subsampling, scan count <= 100, DQT, APP segments) for M2.20/23/24; IJG `q_est` by log-ratio least squares, q90 for custom tables. Acceptance: within +-3 on IJG-table files; no panics on malformed input (M2.61).

### Safe writes and backups
- [ ] **M2.27 FsPlan** - Plans all names before any write (NFC+case-fold key, sanitiser, `Rename`/`Skip`/`Replace`); sole replaceability gate (D3): only single-frame sources this build can write are replaced, else `Skipped(NotReplaceable)`. Acceptance: proptest >= 10,000 cases, no key collisions.
- [ ] **M2.28 Windows filesystem decision spike** - Compare `ReplaceFileW` with `rename` on NTFS, ReFS, exFAT, SMB; sideload a throwaway MSIX writing under `%LOCALAPPDATA%`, uninstall, check for deletion. Acceptance: ADR with the swap choice and where a Store build keeps `backups/` and `library.db`.
- [ ] **M2.29 Path and file-state hazards** - Verbatim `\\?\` paths; cloud placeholders skipped as `CloudNotLocal` (`--hydrate` opts in), read-only skipped, symlinks replaced at the target, sync roots warn and suggest a copy. Acceptance: 300-character, non-ASCII and symlink paths round trip.
- [ ] **M2.30 Temp write and verify-before-replace** - Write `.autocrop-<ulid>.tmp` beside the target (mtime, mode, xattrs, fsync); verify: re-read and match the encoder's blake3, then re-decode (pixel hash, or lossy 64x64 luma error <= 3/255). Acceptance: truncation and bit flips always caught.
- [ ] **M2.31 Atomic swap with retry** - Re-stat the source (size, mtime, file id; moved: `SourceChanged`, backup kept); swap via `rename`/`ReplaceFileW`, retrying 10-640 ms then `FileInUse`; 1175-1177 re-stat the target; Unix dir `fsync`. Acceptance: 300 ms lock succeeds, 5 s gives `FileInUse`.
- [ ] **M2.32 Journal and startup recovery** - Journal (`synchronous=FULL`); recovery requeues pre-Committing rows, rolls Committing forward if temp hash matches and target is source or missing, else Saved/`Failed(SourceChanged)`; sweeps journalled temps. Acceptance: every state x temp x target.
- [ ] **M2.33 Backup store and library.db** - `%LOCALAPPDATA%\AutoCrop` store (user-only ACL): `backups/<ulid>/{original.<ext>,manifest.json}`; `library.db` keeps `outputs` a list from day one. Acceptance: `backups reindex` rebuilds rows; concurrent CLIs are safe; a v1 fixture migrates.
- [ ] **M2.34 Backup methods and preflight** - Reflink, else hardlink (same volume), else a re-hashed copy; fsync files and dirs, insert the `backups` row, only then journal BackedUp; 2x free-space headroom, exit 6 below 500 MB. Acceptance: same/cross-volume matrix shows the expected method.
- [ ] **M2.35 Retention and purge** - Retention 30 days (7, 30, 90, 365, Never); purge at start (not in dry-run, doctor, listings) at most every 24 h, only past expiry inside the store; warn at 10 GB used or < 5 GB free. Acceptance: hostile paths never escape the store; day-short entries survive.
- [ ] **M2.36 Conversion replaces the source (B3)** - `convert-only` and BMP to PNG: verify the temp, back up the source, no-clobber rename, re-stat, unlink the source; recovery unlinks only after re-checking the backup hash. Acceptance: fault injection per step; `restore` returns the source.
- [ ] **M2.37 Restore** - By id, path or batch: unchanged output restores silently, else `if_modified = fail | backup | copy`; same verify and swap; metadata restored; works after a restart or `library.db` loss. Acceptance: byte-identical in 100% of the M2.59 matrix. _(gate: restore)_
- [ ] **M2.38 Idempotency guard** - A file whose blake3 matches a recorded output is `AlreadyProcessed` and skipped (`--reprocess` restarts from the original); a no-op rule (quad >= 97% of frame, |skew| < 0.1 deg) skips without rewriting. Acceptance: a second run classifies >= 99% as no-op.

### CLI
- [ ] **M2.39 CLI skeleton** - Console `auto-crop` (`clap`; `engine` only): `process`, `restore`, `backups`, `doctor`, `analyze`, `render`, `eval` (feature, off in release); `--json`, `--quiet`, `--verbose`, `--no-config`; stdout machine output. Acceptance: `--help` snapshots, usage exit 2.
- [ ] **M2.40 Input expansion and walking** - Files, dirs and Windows globs (`wild`); `-r` via `walkdir` (depth 64); symlinks only with `--follow-symlinks`; skips hidden, system, temp and store entries; warns above 50,000 files. Acceptance: symlink/junction loops end, each file processed once.
- [ ] **M2.41 Output modes** - Default: overwrite in place after a verified backup; at most one of `--in-place` (alias), `--output <dir>`, `--suffix <s>`; `--name-template` (`{name}`/`{name}_{n}`); `--if-exists keep-both|skip|replace`; one-time stderr notice. Acceptance: test per mode; notice once.
- [ ] **M2.42 Dry run** - `--dry-run` decodes and analyses, prints the FsPlan (names, decision, backup method, replaceability) and writes nothing: no temp, backup, journal row, purge or settings change. Acceptance: tree and app-data hashes unchanged over 100 files; plan equals the real run's.
- [ ] **M2.43 Processing options** - `--preset receipt|document|photo|flatbed|convert-only`, `--triage strict|balanced|aggressive` (default strict; balanced experimental), `--min-confidence`, `--jobs --mem-limit --format --quality --strip-location --max-pixels --reprocess`. Acceptance: parser tests.
- [ ] **M2.44 JSON manifest** - `--json` prints one document (`"v": 1`): per item state, route, confidence, `hold_reason` (one registry), geometry, error {code, params}, stage ms; `--ndjson` streams items; schema in `docs/schema/`. Acceptance: CLI outputs validate; notices never hold.
- [ ] **M2.45 Progress and exit codes** - Progress on TTY stderr; exit 0 written or skipped, 1 internal, 2 usage, 3 some failed, 4 held (`--hold-exit-zero` = 0), 5 no supported input, 6 precondition failed (space, store, backup, AVX2), 130 cancel; precedence 1, 6, 3, 4. Acceptance: a test per code.
- [ ] **M2.46 `restore` subcommand** - `restore <path|backup-id>`, `--batch <id>`, `--if-modified fail|backup|copy`, `--json`; exit 3 on failure, nothing left half-restored. Acceptance: a two-invocation `assert_cmd` test (process, then restore from a new process) is byte-identical by SHA-256.
- [ ] **M2.47 `backups` subcommands** - `backups list`, `show <id>`, `purge (--expired | --older-than <days> | --id <id> | --all)` needing `--yes` or confirmation, `reindex`; listings never purge. Acceptance: purge refuses unconfirmed when stdin is not a TTY; usage equals a directory walk.
- [ ] **M2.48 AVX2 floor check and `doctor`** - Baseline x86-64 build, runtime SIMD dispatch; probe `avx2`, else a plain message and exit 6; `--version` lists git SHA, libjpeg-turbo; `doctor` prints CPU, RAM, store usage, no sandbox. Acceptance: probe test, masked-AVX2 VM run logged. _(gate: AVX2)_
- [ ] **M2.49 Windows console and Ctrl+C** - UTF-8 output, colours only with VT and no `NO_COLOR`; `ctrlc` sets the batch `CancelToken` (commit shielded). Acceptance: exit 130 within 2 s, consistent journal, no partial output; accented and CJK names round-trip via `--json`.
- [ ] **M2.81 `analyze` and `render`** - `analyze` (`--emit-edit`, `--ndjson`, `--timings`) prints quad, confidence, stage ms, no images; `render --edit state.json <input> -o out` applies an EditState. Acceptance: M2.50/2.56 run via `analyze --ndjson`; `render` equals `process` (`--deterministic`).

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M2.50 Classical detector eval** - M1 harness on the ~5k synthetic set (IoU after canonical warp, corner error, per slice) and nightly on SmartDoc 2015 Ch.1 and MIDV-500; the PR smoke (M1.50) runs it. Acceptance: mean IoU >= 0.95, failures <= 8% on synthetic. _(gate: detector)_
- [ ] **M2.51 Golden v0 eval and publishing** - M1.51 private workflow on golden v0 (>= 150 images, >= 25 per slice); publish failure and silent-failure rates for slices n >= 30 only with Clopper-Pearson 95% bounds. Acceptance: no per-image data leaves the private repo (B21). _(gate: per-slice)_
- [ ] **M2.52 Threshold tuning** - Freeze PROVISIONAL detector constants (Canny, epsilon, ranges, margins, deskew) on synthetic, public and unlocked golden data, in `docs/tuning-v0.md`; cutoffs stay interim. Acceptance: results hold or improve; locked and public suites regress <= 0.5 pt.
- [ ] **M2.53 Receipt, negative and skew slices** - Synthetic receipts (4:1-8:1, thermal fade, partial, touching), no-document negatives and rotated text pages (+-7 deg). Acceptance: negatives >= 95% held; text-page skew median <= 0.1 deg, p95 <= 0.25 deg; near-zero-skew pages change <= 1%.
- [ ] **M2.54 Benchmarks in CI** - criterion and `gungraun` (Linux) benches for Canny, contours, edge support, refine, DLT, warp, deskew, JPEG Transform and FsPlan per SIMD level; span names equal PLAN Table A; a PR fails above 5% instruction-count growth. Acceptance: a seeded 10% slowdown fails CI.
- [ ] **M2.55 Lossless and EXIF test suites** - Turns, flips and crops of all subsamplings, progressive and odd-size JPEGs vs `jpegtran`; EXIF 8 orientations x 4 containers x both paths. Acceptance: scan data byte-exact (APP1 aside); all upright, tag 1. _(gate: lossless, EXIF once)_
- [ ] **M2.56 Latency measurement (Table A)** - Measure Table A stages on Windows Tier-M (`hyperfine`; 12, 48, 100 MP JPEGs) into `docs/perf-v0.1.md`. Acceptance: 12 MP p50 <= 700 ms for T1 exits (crop-only 580), `analyse` p50 <= 40 ms; a stage over 1.5x gets a redesign note. _(gate: 700 ms)_
- [ ] **M2.57 Throughput, memory and determinism** - 200 x 12 MP JPEGs at cores - 1 jobs on 6 cores, plus 48 and 100 MP files. Acceptance: >= 4 img/s, efficiency >= 70%, peak RSS per 12 MP image <= 175 MB (48 MP 500 MB, 100 MP 1 GB), byte-identical at `--jobs 1` and 8. _(gate: throughput)_
- [ ] **M2.58 Fault-injection harness** - `fault-injection` failpoints at each M2.30-2.37 step plus ENOSPC and source changes; a driver kills the CLI and the target stays original or verified. Acceptance: >= 1,000 random kills on Windows plus Linux/macOS CI, 0 violations. _(gate: fault injection)_
- [ ] **M2.59 Backup and restore matrix** - Formats (JPEG, PNG, TIFF, WebP, BMP) x modes (overwrite, convert) x method (reflink, hardlink, copy) x volume (same, cross) x restart x `library.db` deleted, comparing SHA-256, mtime and attributes. Acceptance: 100% byte-identical. _(gate: restore matrix)_
- [ ] **M2.60 Format and hostile fixtures** - CMYK/arithmetic JPEG, APNG, animated WebP, multi-page and G4 TIFF, lossy WebP, BMP, bombs. Acceptance: 0 crashes or hangs, bombs rejected within 1 s and 64 MB, one file of each non-replaceable case stays byte-identical by default (D3).
- [ ] **M2.61 Fuzz targets** - New M1.71 targets: probe, EXIF/IFD, ICC, marker parser, Transform glue, FsPlan sanitiser, `manifest.json`; 60 s PR smoke, 1 h nightly per target, >= 72 h cumulative before 1.0; crashers become tests. Acceptance: 0 crashes or hangs in a first 1 h run per target.
- [ ] **M2.82 300-image mixed batch corpus** - `xtask gen-batch --n 300 --seed 1`: mixed 12 MP JPEG receipts, documents, photos plus PNG, TIFF, WebP (~25 hard, >= 5 undetectable, 3 corrupt), `tools/synth` ground truth (M5.54 extends). Acceptance: manifest hash identical across runs and OSes.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M2.62 Licence audit, notices and provenance** - Crates pass `cargo deny` (no GPL, AGPL or non-commercial); `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` incl. libjpeg-turbo and SQLite; `docs/provenance.md` lists ported algorithms. Acceptance: `cargo deny` and `reuse lint` green on 3 OSes; notices in the zip.
- [ ] **M2.63 No-network guarantee** - Linux CI runs the fixture batch under `strace -f -e trace=network` and fails on any non-loopback connect; `cargo tree` shows no HTTP client; a Windows manual check is logged in the release checklist. Acceptance: CI green, entry present.
- [ ] **M2.64 Data-loss threat review** - `docs/safety-threat-model.md` maps each overwrite failure mode (crash, power loss, antivirus lock, cloud sync, disk full, Ctrl+C, tampered manifest, purge traversal) to a mitigation and an M2.58-2.61 test. Acceptance: every row links a passing test.
- [ ] **M2.65 SignPath application prep** - `docs/code-signing-policy.md` linked from the README: exact SignPath attribution line, roles (all the maintainer; confirm solo is acceptable), privacy statement, OSI licence; MFA on both. Acceptance: page live before applying. _(gate: policy live)_
- [ ] **M2.66 SignPath application submitted** - After v0.1.0 is public, submit repo URL, release link, policy page and roles; save the confirmation. Acceptance: confirmation in the release checklist; approval time is unpublished, so v0.2.0 may ship unsigned. _(gate: SignPath submitted)_

### Docs
- [ ] **M2.67 Quickstart and CLI reference** - `docs/quickstart.md` (verify checksum, first run, dry run, restore) and a `clap`-generated CLI reference (`xtask docs-cli`). Acceptance: CI fails on drift; a clean-VM walk-through processes and restores a folder in < 5 minutes.
- [ ] **M2.68 "What happens to my originals" page** - `docs/originals.md`: overwrite by default, verify-then-swap, backups and 30-day retention, restore, conversion, never-replaced files (D3), copies, `--dry-run`. Acceptance: claims link test IDs; linked from README and notice.
- [ ] **M2.69 Privacy statement** - `docs/privacy.md`: all local, no accounts, analytics, telemetry or network calls; backups hold full originals; logs hold no image content; EXIF with GPS kept unless `--strip-location`. Acceptance: reused verbatim by the SignPath page and README.
- [ ] **M2.70 Limitations page** - `docs/limitations.md`: harness numbers (synthetic, public, golden n >= 30), failing slices (clutter, receipts > 4:1, white on white), gaps (no ML, HEIC, enhancement, splitting), uncalibrated confidence. Acceptance: `xtask docs-numbers` generates every number.
- [ ] **M2.71 README for the preview** - Preview label, zip install table (unsigned), SmartScreen and Smart App Control notes, verify snippets (`Get-FileHash`, `sha256sum`, `gh attestation verify`), numbers table, links. Acceptance: snippets run on real assets; numbers match the metrics JSON.
- [ ] **M2.72 AI-assistance disclosure** - Extends M0.72-0.74: README and CONTRIBUTING carry "No code is knowingly copied from GPL, AGPL or non-commercial projects; algorithms derived from such work are reimplemented clean-room from papers." Acceptance: texts identical; `check-dco` passes.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M2.73 Windows release build and zip** - Release build for `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc` (`cargo-auditable`, LTO, static CRT incl. `/MT` libjpeg-turbo); `xtask package` zips exe and licences. Acceptance: `dumpbin /dependents` shows system DLLs only, zip < 15 MB, runs from paths with spaces.
- [ ] **M2.74 Checksums, attestation and SBOM** - `release.yml` finalize: `SHA256SUMS`, CycloneDX SBOM, `actions/attest` provenance and SBOM attestations, SLSA build L2; verify job runs `sha256sum -c` and `gh attestation verify`. Acceptance: verify green on the dry run. _(gate: attestation)_
- [ ] **M2.75 release-plz flow** - `release-plz` opens the release PR; a GitHub App token creates the tag; `release.yml` gates on the `release` environment and drafts only platforms in `release-platforms.toml` (Windows x64). Acceptance: a test PR in the M2.78 repo yields a full draft.
- [ ] **M2.76 Cross-OS CI stays green** - Windows-only (`ReplaceFileW`) and Unix-only (`xattr`, dir `fsync`) code sits behind `cfg`; every M2 test that can run on three OSes does; macOS and Linux publish nothing (B9). Acceptance: required checks pass on all three OSes; a broken `cfg` gate is caught.
- [ ] **M2.77 Clean-VM smoke and trust checks** - Run on clean Windows 10 22H2 and 11 VMs: process 5 fixtures (spaces in path), byte-identical `restore`, AVX2 message; record SmartScreen, Smart App Control (unverified), Defender. Acceptance: logs in the release checklist. _(gate: clean VMs)_
- [ ] **M2.78 Release dry run** - Rehearse in a throwaway repo with immutable releases (attach everything to the draft first): release-plz PR, tag, draft, build, attest, verify, publish `v0.0.0-dryrun.N`. Acceptance: verify passes on the published assets; `docs/release-runbook.md` corrected.
- [ ] **M2.79 Publish v0.1.0** - release-plz PR, tag, draft, verify draft, enable immutable releases, publish, verify public assets; notes carry numbers and limitations; tick M2 rows, update status table. Acceptance: `sha256sum -c` and `gh attestation verify` pass on public assets.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Classical detector on the synthetic set has mean quad IoU >= 0.95 and failures (IoU < 0.9) <= 8% (PROVISIONAL, classical-only), measured by the M2.50 harness; synthetic results never satisfy B6.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Golden v0 aggregates published per slice (README, release notes) with Clopper-Pearson 95% bounds for n >= 30 only (n < 80 advisory), pooled silent-failure rate printed, default stays Strict; only aggregates leave the private repo (B21).
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Fault injection over >= 1,000 randomised kill runs (PROVISIONAL count) never loses or corrupts an original and leaves no `.autocrop-*.tmp` after the startup sweep, on Windows and on Linux and macOS CI.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Backup then restore is byte-identical by SHA-256 in 100% of the M2.59 matrix, including after a restart and with `library.db` deleted, across same-volume and cross-volume backups.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Lossless JPEG fast path is byte-exact against a `jpegtran` reference for 90 deg turns and MCU-aligned crops (only the EXIF APP1 may differ, only in Orientation and dimensions).
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** EXIF orientation is applied exactly once across the 8-orientation matrix (100%), including on re-processing.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** On Tier-M a 12 MP JPEG auto-process takes <= 700 ms p50 for T1 exits and the batch reaches >= 4 img/s on 6 cores, with peak RSS per in-flight 12 MP image <= 175 MB and no Table A line over 2x budget (all PROVISIONAL, numbers published).
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** CLI exit codes (one test per code) and the manifest JSON Schema validate on every CLI integration run, and the `analyze`/`render` outputs match `process` for the same EditState.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** Held, failed and non-replaceable (D3) files stay byte-identical (SHA-256, mtime) across the 300-image run, the hostile-file suite has 0 crashes or hangs, each fuzz target is clean for 1 hour (PROVISIONAL), and `cargo deny check` is green.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** The v0.1.0 GitHub Release has the Windows x64 zip, `SHA256SUMS`, build attestation and SBOM, verified on the public assets, and the zip passes the smoke test on clean Windows 10 22H2 and Windows 11 VMs; a CPU without AVX2 gets a clear message and exit 6.
- [ ] **GATE (G1):** SignPath Foundation application is submitted with the code signing policy page live on the default branch.

## M3 - GUI alpha: single-image editor

<!-- ms: version=v0.2.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Ship the touch-first Windows desktop shell (Tauri 2.x + Svelte 5): open files and folders, show the M2 classical auto result, correct it with a quad editor and angle ruler, undo and redo, and save by overwrite-plus-backup or Save as copy. It validates the stack and the touch model before ML, batch review, HEIC and enhancement.
**Release:** `v0.2.0` preview ("GUI alpha, Windows only") for Windows 10/11 x64: NSIS per-user installer and portable zip, each with the console `auto-crop.exe` beside `AutoCrop.exe`, unsigned, with `SHA256SUMS` and attestations, via GitHub Releases only; macOS and Linux shells build in CI but publish nothing (B9).
**Depends on:** M2 (classical pipeline, commit protocol, backups, `restore`, CLI with `render` M2.81), M1 (harness, `xtask bench`, `ui/` QuadEditor M1.41) and the M0.70 GUI verdict (only M3 waits for it).
**Size:** XL (9-16 part-time weeks, definition in X.40; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G8 (interactive, PROVISIONAL until measured): on a Surface-class touch device one image is edited end to end with drag-frame median <= 17 ms (p95 <= 20), release-to-final p50 <= 150 ms (p95 <= 250), undo/redo <= 50 ms and cold start <= 1.0 s; real-touch, single-pointer, 50-image dogfood, 1-hour soak and attested installer checks also pass; a miss goes to the owner (A-4).
**Risks / spikes:**
- Frame rate on WebView2 (highest risk): probe 1-2 days on a real Surface before the overlay is built out; if it misses: 2 MP proxy, one composited layer, rAF-coalesced updates, canvas 2D, loupe every other frame; then the gate is unmet and the owner decides (A-4); Slint only if M0.70 fired and the owner waives B20 RTL (A-10).
- No touch hardware or testers ($0, B15): record touch results UNMEASURED, keep the touch gates open, never tick them from a mouse run (A-9); shipping v0.2.0 past them is the owner's recorded decision.
- Also: overlapping handles on 8:1 receipts (hit priority, auto-zoom, T4); canvas accessibility (real DOM handles, NVDA); TypeScript vs Rust homography drift (shared vectors); overwrite-by-default from the first write (explicit Save, per-image Restore, first-write sheet); unsigned-installer trust friction.

### Core
- [ ] **M3.01 Confirm the M0 GUI verdict** - Read the M0.70 verdict and merge the GUI-stack ADR (`docs/adr/NNNN-gui-stack.md`). Tauri 2.x + Svelte 5 stands unless B8 fired (A-10); Slint needs the owner's B20 RTL waiver, so re-plan before any UI code. Pass: ADR linked from ROADMAP.
- [ ] **M3.02 Scaffold `crates/shell` and `ui/`** - Extend `ui/` (M1.41), add `crates/shell`; `cargo tauri dev` runs; `check-deps` (M0.30) also bans `tao`, `gtk`, `webkit2gtk`, `slint` outside `shell`; `xtask doctor` checks Node, WebView2, Tauri CLI. Pass: a planted `slint` in `engine` fails CI.
- [ ] **M3.03 Engine command and event contract** - `engine` commands (open, edit, undo/redo/jump, save, restore) plus `EventSink`; opaque ids, range-checked inputs, raw-path `open_paths` only behind `e2e`; generated TS types. Pass: fake-sink test drives open-edit-undo-save; stale bindings fail CI.
- [ ] **M3.04 Native drag-and-drop and folder intake** - Rust handles `WindowEvent::DragDrop` on the M2 walk and gives the UI only opaque ids and sanitised names (M0.68: can the webview drop event be denied?).
- [ ] **M3.05 Open dialogs from Rust** - `tauri-plugin-dialog` only inside Rust commands (webview has no dialog, `fs` or opener permission): file picker, folder picker and save-file dialog return opaque ids. Pass: Unicode and > 260-character paths work; Cancel changes nothing.
- [ ] **M3.06 Single instance and Open-with argv** - `tauri-plugin-single-instance` forwards a second launch's args and exits; launches within 300 ms merge into one open request; paths become opaque ids in Rust. Pass: 12 `AutoCrop.exe <file>` starts within 100 ms give one window with 12 images.
- [ ] **M3.07 `acimg` tile scheme** - Custom scheme serving `acimg://localhost/<launch-token>/<item-id>/<level>[/<z>/<x>/<y>]?g=<gen>` from engine caches only (strict parser, 128-bit token, `no-store`, 8 MB cap). Pass: tests reject `..`, bad token, oversize; no tile bytes in the WebView2 profile.
- [ ] **M3.08 Preview pyramid** - Extends M1.22: one decode feeds 256 px thumbnail, 1024 px detection, ~1.5 MP analysis (long edge <= 3072 px) and 2-4 MP display proxies, sRGB once; JPEG opens paint first from the M1.19 scaled decode. Pass: 12/24/48 MP property tests; first paint <= 60 ms at 12 MP.
- [ ] **M3.09 Tile cache and version counter** - Lazy 512 px tiles past the display proxy in a byte-weighted `quick_cache` LRU (256 MB) keyed by (image, level, x, y, version); `g=` makes stale tiles unreachable. Pass: no response > 8 MB; M0.65 script rerun gives p95 tile latency <= 50 ms on Tier-M.
- [ ] **M3.10 Generations and cancellation** - Per-image `gen` bumps per commit; `RenderReq` carries `gen` + a `CancelToken` (every 64 rows); preview rayon pool (`clamp(cores/4, 2, 3)`) apart from batch; duplicates replaced. Pass: 200 random-delay `set_edit` calls deliver only the newest `gen`.
- [ ] **M3.11 Release render and result cache** - `set_edit(phase: "end")` renders via the single `render` path and emits `render-ready{image, gen, url}`; the last 8 results per image are cached by `render_hash`. Pass: `save` output equals CLI `render` (M2.81) for the same `EditState` (golden test).
- [ ] **M3.12 History wiring** - Expose core `History` as `undo`, `redo`, `jump`: one entry per drag, slider release or nudge burst (500 ms), redo tail cut on commit, unchanged `render_hash` dropped, cap 200, labels as message keys. Pass: proptest `undo(redo(x)) == x`; undo/redo repaint <= 50 ms.
- [ ] **M3.13 Open pipeline** - Preview-pool open: probe, first paint, decode, M2 analysis, `EditState`; failed, > 100 MP (C1) or unsupported files stay untouched with typed codes (HEIC: "supported in v0.5.0"). Pass: 12 MP JPEG paints, then the result; M2 hostile fixtures rejected in 1 s/64 MB.
- [ ] **M3.14 Settings as TOML** - Extends M2.06: GUI keys in the same `settings.toml` (`save_as_copy` off, retention 7/30/90/365 days or Never (30), UI toggles, language, first-run); the shell passes the engine's `AppPaths`, never Tauri's resolver. Pass: a corrupt file falls back with a notice.
- [ ] **M3.15 Save command** - `save(id, OutputSpec)` via the M2 protocol, `InPlace` or `Copy{dir}` (Rust-picked id or `<folder>/AutoCrop/`); JPEG Small/Balanced/Best = q_est-10 (60-85) / +5 (80-95) / +10 (90-97), else q80/q90/q95; reports lossless. Pass: backup hash = original, journal `Saved`.
- [ ] **M3.16 Restore original command** - Wires M2 `restore` per image, working after restart; a file changed since saving offers Restore as copy or Replace anyway (pre-restore backup kept). Pass: save, quit, relaunch, restore is byte-identical; a v0.1.0 CLI backup (fixture store) restores too.
- [ ] **M3.17 Session autosave and resume** - Persist the open list and each image's `EditState` history to the M2.33 `library.db` write-behind; after an unclean exit (`session.lock`, M3.18) offer "Resume previous session?". Pass: a mid-edit kill restores list, state and undo cursor; write < 5 ms.
- [ ] **M3.18 Local crash log** - Extends M2.06: add WebView2 version, stage and memory stats to `crash/<ts>-<version>.txt` and a `session.lock` sentinel for native crashes. Pass: a forced panic and a forced `TerminateProcess` each leave a record without the fixture's path or name.
- [ ] **M3.19 Windows file associations** - NSIS `installerHooks` (opt-in; not Tauri `fileAssociations`) write `Applications\AutoCrop.exe` + `OpenWithProgids` for jpg/jpeg, png, tif/tiff, webp; default and UserChoice untouched, keys removed on uninstall; portable zip: same keys via Settings.

- [ ] **GATE (G8):** Real touch and single-pointer - the real-touch run passes (pinch, pan, twist, loupe drag, no page-zoom leakage) and every drag and pinch has a single-pointer alternative (WCAG 2.5.1, 2.5.7 Pass; axe clean); no device = open as UNMEASURED (A-9), never ticked from a mouse run.
