- [ ] **GATE (G8):** Security and privacy - `cargo deny check`, the npm licence check, the capability/CSP allow-list with negative IPC tests and the no-network audit are green, and the v0.2.0 build contains no telemetry, updater or HTTP plugin (M3.64, M3.66 to M3.68).

## M4 - ML detection, orientation and calibrated confidence

<!-- ms: version=v0.3.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Deliver the accuracy promise: a hybrid learned plus classical detector with sub-pixel refinement, a 4-way orientation net and gated deskew, and a calibrated Good / Check / Failed confidence with Strict / Balanced / Aggressive operating points, all with cleared model provenance.
**Release:** v0.3.0 - Windows 10/11 x64 only; NSIS installer and portable zip (both with the models, `onnxruntime.dll` and the console `auto-crop.exe`), `SHA256SUMS`, GitHub artifact attestations, SBOM; unsigned unless SignPath has approved. macOS and Linux keep building in CI (ML backend per M4.73).
**Depends on:** M2 (classical detector, warp, safe writes, CLI) and M3 (editor with quad handles, `EditState`); also M1 (eval crate, `tools/synth`, golden v0, M1.75 models repo) and the M0.35 inference-backend ADR.
**Size:** XL (X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md)
**Exit gate:** G2 (hybrid detection); every number here is PROVISIONAL until measured: on the locked golden v1 (>= 500, scene-disjoint) mean quad IoU >= 0.985 with <= 2% failures (<= 6% hard slice), corner, skew and orientation targets met, ECE <= 0.05, `analyse` <= 40 ms p95 over T1 exits, models <= 25 MB, provenance complete. Balanced is the default only if silent failures are point <= 1.0% and bound <= 2.0%, else Strict; a flag rate above 10% (15% at v0.3.0) is a stage-gate note.
**Risks / spikes:**
- Weights: no cleared pretrained weights exist (DocQuadNet label unclear, DTD lineage; ImageNet init needs an A-7 exception), so from-scratch training is time-boxed (M4.15) and the classical detector carries v0.3.0 if no cleared model passes, recorded and never hidden.
- Accuracy: a stride-4 net may fail on 8:1 receipts (M4.49 early test, M4.32 elongated pass) and the synthetic-to-real gap is open; golden v1 certifies 1% only if nearly clean, so calibration uses real data only, intervals are published and a missed gate is never relaxed (A-4).
- Runtime: ort is 2.0.0-rc.13 (exact pin, `InferenceBackend` trait, M4.59 conformance suite), Windows can load a shadow `onnxruntime.dll` (M4.20) and Intel Macs have no ORT binary (M8 decides). Order: M4.01-M4.06 first, calibration last.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M4.01 DocQuadNet licence outcome** - Extends M0.26: read the recorded MakeACopy reply (or dated no-reply) in `models/PROVENANCE.md`; re-ask only if it changed. The weights stay a dev-only baseline, never shipped as-is (M4.72); derived weights need an owner-granted A-7 exception.
- [ ] **M4.02 Provenance log and check-models** - The models-repo JSON-lines log is the source of truth (CSV and `PROVENANCE.md` are generated); statuses cleared, pending, blocked, banned, exception (owner-granted). `xtask check-models` and models-repo CI fail on an unlogged or unapproved asset.
- [ ] **M4.03 Clear the training data sources** - Cleared rows (archive SHA-256, attribution) for SmartDoc 2015 Ch.1 (CC BY 4.0; cite the paper, send the notice email), CORD, MIDV-500 (per source) and own renders; backgrounds procedural, CC0 (each checked) or own CC0 photos, never DTD.
- [ ] **M4.04 Exclusion and ban list** - Banned or blocked (M0.25 pre-fills most): DocTr, DocGeoNet, DocEnTr, DocAligner weights, RMBG, DIS5K, DTD, SROIE, Doc3D, UVDoc textures (until M4.81), HF DocCornerDataset, AlbumentationsX; ImageNet/torchvision weights pending. Any in a manifest fails CI.
- [ ] **M4.05 ImageNet-initialisation decision** - Log every initialisation source; from-scratch weights (same recipe) are the default. ImageNet-initialised weights need an owner-granted A-7 exception (ADR, status exception); else accept the lower IoU or slip under the M4.15 time-box.
- [ ] **M4.06 Orientation weights licence check** - Verify PP-LCNet_x1_0_doc_ori (Apache-2.0 tag, undisclosed training data): weight and data terms and the `paddle2onnx` conversion (max abs logit diff <= 1e-4 on 100 images). On failure the own net (M4.17) ships and PP-LCNet stays a baseline.
- [ ] **M4.07 ONNX Runtime licence and notices** - Ship ORT's licence and notices with the DLL; add ort and its crates to cargo-about and the `deny.toml` allow-list; `opencv` stays dev-only. Accept: `cargo deny check` green; `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` lists ORT, model licences and CC BY attributions.
- [ ] **M4.08 Model file integrity** - `models.lock` pins SHA-256, size and version; the loader hashes the bytes, then builds the session from memory (`commit_from_memory`). Only `models.lock` files load (user models: B.13, post-1.0). Accept: a flipped byte gives `ModelLoadFailed` (M4.22).
- [ ] **M4.09 No-network proof (B7, B18)** - Disable ORT telemetry, show by `cargo tree` that no HTTP client is reachable from `engine`, `cli` or the ML path (outside the opt-in updater), and complete an analysis under an outbound-block Windows Firewall rule; both run in CI.
- [ ] **M4.10 Provenance audit sign-off** - Record per shipped weight and dataset in `models/PROVENANCE.md` (licence, source, hash, lineage, reviewer, date) and re-read the ban list. Accept: `xtask check-models` green; none pending, blocked or banned. _(gate: provenance log complete)_
- [ ] **M4.81 UVDoc audit rows and author outreach** - Add a pending row per UVDoc lineage source (checkpoint, textures, Doc3D, DTD, Paddle port); ask the authors for a data statement (14 days) and file the reply or dated no-reply in `models/PROVENANCE.md`; M12.01 and M12.02 read it.

### Models and training (separate repo `WelFedTed/auto-crop-models`)
- [ ] **M4.11 Models repo scaffold and fetch scripts** - Extends M1.75: pinned Python lockfile, `configs/`, model-card template, SmartDoc, CORD and MIDV-500 fetch scripts (pinned SHA-256); no weights, datasets or LFS in git. Accept: a fresh clone rebuilds the cache and prints the manifest hash.
- [ ] **M4.12 Training data generator** - Extends M1.30-M1.33: `tools/synth` training mode (Python, OpenCV, Augraphy) with 360-degree rotation, tilt to 45 degrees, partial frames, occlusion, blur, JPEG q40-95, thermal fade, 4:1-8:1 strips and stored seeds. Accept: corner error < 0.5 px.
- [ ] **M4.13 Scene-disjoint splits** - Extends M1.35 and M1.75: train, validation and calibration partitions fixed by manifest (SmartDoc by sequence, MIDV by document); golden images excluded, evaluation seeds never overlap training seeds. Accept: the M1 checker fails on any shared `scene_id`.
- [ ] **M4.14 Corner-net training script** - PyTorch MobileNetV3 or LCNet backbone with FPN, 256x256 letterbox input, stride-4 heads (four Gaussian corner heatmaps, BCE + Dice mask), clockwise corners, multi-scale; start near 1.2M parameters. Accept: overfits 64 images to < 1 px.
- [ ] **M4.15 Bootstrap decision and time-box** - After M4.01 and M4.05 pick checkpoint reuse (cleared or A-7 exception), recipe reuse or from scratch; time-box the first cycle (six weeks, $0). If no cleared model passes, the classical detector stays primary and ML is marked experimental (ADR).
- [ ] **M4.16 ONNX export and parity** - Export opset 17, fp32, static 256x256 (plus a dynamic-size variant if the 384-512 and elongated passes need it), optional `.ort`; corner net <= 15 MB. Accept: PyTorch and ORT-CPU heatmaps agree within 1e-4 on 50 images, in models-repo CI.
- [ ] **M4.17 Orientation net training** - 4-way (0/90/180/270) net of ~7 MB on free labels (rotated upright pages) from cleared document, receipt and text-line data, 224-256 px, three-crop training for long receipts; needed if M4.06 fails. Accept: val top-1 reported, ONNX <= 8 MB, M4.16 parity.
- [ ] **M4.18 Release manifest and model card** - Each release records seed, commit, config and data-manifest hashes, framework versions, metrics, licence and known failures in `MODEL_CARD.md` and weight SHA-256 in `release.json`. Accept: a clean re-run matches val metrics within 0.2 pt mean IoU.

### Core
- [ ] **M4.19 ORT inference backend** - Implement `InferenceBackend` on the M0.35 choice (ort `=2.0.0-rc.13`, ONNX Runtime 1.28, CPU provider); check the `Session::run` receiver; bump ort only when M4.59 passes. Accept: heatmaps within 1e-4; corners within 0.5 px across thread counts.
- [ ] **M4.20 ORT binary supply on Windows** - Pin ONNX Runtime 1.28 in `native-deps.toml` (SHA-256), fetched by `xtask build-native`, never in `build.rs`; load `onnxruntime.dll` from the app directory by absolute path. Accept: analysis works with a mismatched DLL earlier on the search path.
- [ ] **M4.21 Model loader and warm-up** - Create sessions lazily on a background thread after the UI is up; intra-op threads 1-2 in batch, 2-3 interactive (PROVISIONAL); one warm-up inference; cold-load per model in spans and eval JSON. Accept: cold start to usable UI <= 1.0 s (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M4.22 Degraded classical-only mode** - `ModelLoadFailed` never crashes: Detector B runs alone, capped at Check with `ML_UNAVAILABLE` until a classical-only calibration loads; one UI line links to `doctor`. Accept: missing, corrupt and hidden-DLL runs give only Check or Failed.
- [ ] **M4.23 Analysis and EditState schema** - Extend `Analysis` with the M4.41 `signals`, score, band, `reasons[]`, <= 3 `candidates[]`, model ids, `algo_version` and timings; persist in `EditState` and the journal (migration). Accept: insta snapshots, round trip, v(n-1) fixture migrates.
- [ ] **M4.24 Triage and write rule** - `triage(analysis, mode)` is pure over `calibration.json` and returns AutoAccept, Review(reasons) or Failed; the write path refuses unresolved Check or Failed items, and Failed sources stay byte-identical. Accept: zero writes over 200 forced-outcome images.

### Detection
- [ ] **M4.25 Warp SIMD and provider seam** - Extends M2.15: SIMD loop (`wide` or `pulp`) with fixed-point LUT weights and a coordinate-provider seam (dense map: M12.30). Accept: PSNR >= 45 dB vs oracle, <= 90 ms at 12 MP on 8 threads, byte-identical at 1 and 8, RSS in ceiling at 12/48/100 MP.
- [ ] **M4.27 Edge support at full resolution** - Extends M2.09: sample edge support (24-64 points per edge, 10-90% of length) at full resolution on both detectors' refined quads so the M4.33 fuse compares like with like; Detector B's rules and thresholds stay M2.07-M2.09 and M2.12.
- [ ] **M4.28 Refinement proxy stage** - Extends M2.13: add a proxy-level first stage (24-64 samples per edge, parabolic peak, Huber IRLS) before the full-resolution pass within ~8 px; an edge under 60% supported keeps its proxy line. Accept: median <= 2 px, p95 <= 5 px at 12 MP, synthetic only.
- [ ] **M4.29 Partial frames** - Extends M2.09 and M2.18 (the hard geometry gate stays M2.09): a corner fitted outside the frame sets `partial_frame`, clamps that side to the border, skips paper snapping and caps at Check (`PARTIAL_FRAME`). Accept: unit tests plus a partial-frame slice line.
- [ ] **M4.30 ML detector inference wrapper** - Letterbox to 256x256, run the net, decode four heatmaps (peak plus 5x5 soft-argmax), map back, order corners in image space, derive the mask quad; accept if min peak >= 0.5 and mask IoU >= 0.90. Test: within 0.5 px of PyTorch on 50 images.
- [ ] **M4.31 Second pass: ROI and TTA** - If the quad covers < 25% of the frame, re-run on ROI + 15% (kept if mask IoU improves); if unsure, re-run at 0/90/180/270 degrees and take the per-corner median. Accept: fewer failures on small-document and tilt > 30 degree slices, <= 250 ms extra.
- [ ] **M4.32 Long-strip elongated pass** - Masks above 4:1 (minimum-area rectangle) are rotated + 15% to horizontal and re-run at an elongated input (start 512x128) so short-end peaks do not merge. Accept: synthetic 8:1 failures (IoU < 0.9) <= 6% and below the plain pass, else a strip head.
- [ ] **M4.33 Fuse and both-fail route** - Agree (IoU >= 0.95): ML corners; disagree: refine both, keep the better edge support; one: use it; none: M2 bed trim or Failed `NO_QUAD`. Check caps are interim until M4.42/M4.44. Test: strong ML-only result reaches Good, no crop on 40 negatives.
- [ ] **M4.34 Orientation net integration** - Run the 4-way net on the rectified proxy, four rotations; accept at top-1 >= 0.8 and rotation consistency, else `ORIENT_UNSURE`; aspect > 3:1 averages three square crops; photos and blank pages use EXIF only. Test: long-receipt top-1, crops vs none.
- [ ] **M4.36 Deskew: flatten before Otsu** - Extends M2.17: binarise the ~1.5 MP analysis proxy after a local illumination flatten (M1.65), independent of the enhancement mode. Accept: synthetic -7 to +7 degrees: text pages median <= 0.1, p95 <= 0.25 degrees; photos p95 <= 0.5.
- [ ] **M4.37 Deskew gating** - Extends M2.17: on quad-rectified pages skew is only a verification signal (residual > 0.3 degrees feeds confidence and raises `RESIDUAL_SKEW`); no-quad flatbed pages use the component rectangle's rotation first. Accept: false-apply <= 1% below 0.1 degrees.
- [ ] **M4.38 Photo border trim (B1)** - Extends M2.17: add the border-trim proposal (first non-uniform row and column, at most 40% per side, ignoring the outer 2 px); LSD segments: M2.11 port (OpenCV >= 4.5.4, provenance recorded). Accept: no auto-apply off by > 1 degree on the photo slice.
- [ ] **M4.39 No-document handling** - Document likelihood (peak mask plus best edge support): hint Photo, or low likelihood with clean borders, gives NoChange at Good (no write); Auto with no quad: Check `NO_DOCUMENT`; `ContentHint` in `AnalyzeOptions`. Accept: negatives (n >= 30) cropped <= 2%.
- [ ] **M4.40 Crop target and content-tight (B14)** - Owns the toggle: off by default; ink mask on the flattened analysis proxy, pad 2% of the shorter side, under 0.5% ink falls back to the paper edge; enables M3.35 (undoable). Accept: clipping <= 0.5%, crop IoU mean >= 0.97, p5 >= 0.92.
- [ ] **M4.41 Confidence signal extraction** - Inputs: min corner peak, mask-quad IoU, edge support (mean, min), geometry sanity, A-vs-B IoU, TTA spread, residual skew, orientation margin, per-detector fired flag. Accept: finite, in range on 200 synthetic images (single-detector cases included).
- [ ] **M4.42 Calibrator fit** - `xtask calibrate`: logistic regression, then isotonic, on the unlocked real dev tier plus SmartDoc, MIDV and CORD (never synthetic), plus a classical-only variant; writes `calibration.json` (knots, cutoffs, `algo_ver`, hashes). Accept: deterministic, ECE reported.
- [ ] **M4.43 Bands and HoldReason registry** - Good s >= t(mode), Check 0.60 <= s < t(mode), Failed s < 0.60 or a hard gate (implausible geometry); interim t = 0.95/0.90/0.80 for Strict/Balanced/Aggressive until `calibration.json`. One `HoldReason` registry (codes in M4.82); notices never hold.
- [ ] **M4.44 Operating points (B6)** - Cutoffs from cross-validated dev-tier risk-coverage: Strict <= 0.3%, Balanced <= 1%, Aggressive <= 3% silent failures. Balanced is the default only if locked-golden silent failures are <= 1.0% (bound <= 2.0%); else Strict, Balanced experimental.
- [ ] **M4.45 Confidence sanity tests** - Per PR on synthetic images: score falls with added blur, occlusion and tilt; moving one classical corner by 5% drops a Good result out of Good; rotating, mirroring or halving the input keeps corners within 1% of the diagonal. Accept: three property tests.

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M4.46 Golden set v1** - Grow the private golden set to v1: >= 500 locked images, >= 50 per slice (receipts > 4:1, thermal fade, partial frames, touching items, phone photos, flatbed, general, negatives) plus the ~300-image dev tier; blank-quad labels. Accept: counts per slice.
- [ ] **M4.47 Split discipline** - Extends M1.44: `xtask calibrate` refuses golden inputs and CI fails on any `scene_id` overlap across training, dev, calibration and golden; golden confirms cutoffs once per release candidate, every run is logged and guiding images are retired.
- [ ] **M4.48 Silent-failure metric** - Silent failure: quad IoU < 0.90 (canonical warp), ink clipped > 2 px, not upright or skew > 1.0 degree, no-document image cropped, wrong item count or IoU; report risk, flag rate, Clopper-Pearson bounds as `silent_failure {k, n, rate, ub95}` per preset.
- [ ] **M4.49 Early 8:1 receipt test** - Collect >= 30 real long receipts (> 4:1, >= 10 near 8:1; dev tier, not the locked set) and run the 256x256 net with and without M4.32 before training closes. Accept: failure rate (IoU < 0.9) with n and interval vs <= 6%; if missed, mitigate and re-run.
- [ ] **M4.50 Per-slice regression gates** - Changes to `models.lock`, `calibration.json` or thresholds run the M1.50 smoke and, only via the private `auto-crop-golden` self-hosted workflow (A-8: dispatch or nightly, never PR or fork), the per-slice run; a slice > 1 pt worse blocks release.
- [ ] **M4.51 Calibration evaluation and plots** - Held-out ECE (<= 0.05 pooled, <= 0.10 worst slice, n >= 30), Brier, AUROC, reliability diagram (10 equal-mass bins, Wilson) and risk-coverage curve with the three operating points, aggregate JSON only (<= 20 points, >= 30 images per cell).
- [ ] **M4.52 Detector ablation report** - Run `both | ml | classical` over public, dev and golden sets: per-slice IoU, failure rate, latency and the latency-versus-risk Pareto; decide whether the classical cross-check is always on or escalation-only (cheaper option holding <= 1% risk).
- [ ] **M4.53 Accuracy evaluation run** - Golden and public suites, each number labelled pre- or post-refine with n: mean quad IoU, failures, corner error (golden before refine in % diagonal, synthetic after in px), skew p50 and p95, orientation top-1, selective accuracy >= 99.5% at 95% coverage.
- [ ] **M4.54 Receipt slice reports** - Failure rate per receipt stratum (long > 4:1 and > 8:1, thermal fade, partial frame, touching items, the last against the single-item detector) vs the long-strip bar <= 6%; n < 30 suppressed, n < 80 advisory with intervals, a slice blocks only at n >= 80.
- [ ] **M4.55 Latency benchmarks** - Windows Tier-M, Table A spans: `analyse` <= 40 ms p95 over T1 exits (mixed: p50 <= 40, p95 ~290), first overlay <= 150 ms p95 in the built app, 700 ms p50 at 12 MP (hyperfine at 12/48/100 MP); > 1.5x over budget forces a redesign. _(gate: analyse <= 40 ms)_
- [ ] **M4.56 Kernel benchmark gates** - Extends M2.54: criterion plus gungraun counts (Linux CI) for heatmap decode, fuse, orientation and each detection kernel at 12/48/100 MP; > 5% regression blocks, 2% warns. Peak RSS with both models loaded <= 175 MB, 500 MB, 1 GB per in-flight image.
- [ ] **M4.57 int8 QDQ quantisation study** - Static QDQ of both nets (training images; `reduce_range`, S8S8, fp32 heads) on Windows x64 with and without VNNI. Ship int8 only if mean IoU delta <= 0.3 pt, p95 corner error rise <= 0.05% of diagonal and the win is real (ADR). _(gate: int8 <= 0.3 pt)_
- [ ] **M4.58 Execution-provider benchmark** - Benchmark DirectML (and CUDA if available) against CPU on both nets for latency and corner agreement; a provider becomes opt-in only if p95 improves and corners match CPU within 0.5 px (CoreML: M8). Results in the ADR; default stays CPU.
- [ ] **M4.59 Backend conformance suite** - 20 fixed tensors with reference outputs; any `InferenceBackend` must reproduce corners within 0.1 px. Runs on Windows CI now and gates an rten backend or a macOS and Linux ORT build later.
- [ ] **M4.61 ML-path robustness** - Fuzz `analyze()` with random and pathological rasters (blank, 1x1, 16:1 strips, NaN or zero heatmaps, injected ORT errors): zero panics or hangs; panics become `Failed(InternalPanic)`. Nightly `cargo-fuzz` (Linux, 1 h per target), crashers replayed on Windows.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M4.62 Confidence badge and reason line** - The editor header shows Good, Check or Failed as icon plus word, never colour alone or a raw score, with one reason line looked up by code (M4.82), `role="status"`. Accept: Playwright and axe pass in light, dark and forced-colors.
- [ ] **M4.63 Try another outline on the Failed banner** - Extends M3.39 (banner text and Draw crop stay there): add **Try another outline**, cycling the M4.64 candidates, every step undoable. Accept: E2E on a Failed item shows both actions and the file hash is unchanged until the user accepts.
- [ ] **M4.64 Candidate switcher** - "Try outline 2 of 3" cycles up to three `Analysis` candidates (ML, classical, alternative); each switch is one undoable entry; keyboard operable, >= 44 px targets. Accept: Playwright with mocked candidates; Undo restores the previous outline.
- [ ] **M4.65 Straighten suggestion and no-document notice** - For photos a "Straighten by X degrees" chip applies the M4.38 suggestion in one undoable tap; NoChange shows "No document found. The image is left as it is." with **Draw crop**. Accept: component tests for both states.
- [ ] **M4.66 Advanced > Detection settings** - Sensitivity (Strict / Balanced / Aggressive; Balanced default only if M4.44 passes, else Strict; moves to Settings > Review in M5.34), content type, deskew mode, content-tight, detectors, maximum skew; re-runs never overwrite manual edits.
- [ ] **M4.67 Numeric confidence diagnostics** - An Advanced panel shows the calibrated score, band, every signal, reason codes, model ids and hashes, tier reached and per-stage timings for the current image, with **Copy details** that includes no file paths (for issue reports).
- [ ] **M4.68 Progressive overlay wiring** - Run hybrid `analyze` off the UI thread: proxy overlay first, then full-resolution refinement unless a handle moved; a generation counter drops stale results; cancellation reaches the ML stages. Accept: first overlay <= 150 ms p95 in the built app.
- [ ] **M4.69 Touch and accessibility pass** - New controls have 44-48 px hit areas in touch mode (>= 24 px always), keyboard paths, `aria-live` announcements and untruncated pseudo-locale and pseudo-RTL screens. Accept: zero serious or critical axe findings and a keyboard-only walk-through.
- [ ] **M4.70 Usability round for Check and Failed** - 5 testers on a Windows touch device (PROVISIONAL, open until run; A-9): >= 4 of 5 say a Failed original is untouched, find Draw crop unaided and understand Check means "please look"; findings feed M4.62, M4.63 and M4.82 wording.
- [ ] **M4.80 Corner magnet snap_corner** - `snap_corner` snaps a dragged corner to the nearest edge-line intersection within 12 px at 12 MP using the M4.28 fits; Snap to edges toggle in Adjust (off until measured), one undoable entry per snap. Accept: median <= 2 px on 50 quads offset 10 px.
- [ ] **M4.82 Code-to-copy table** - One row per HoldReason code (`DETECTORS_DISAGREE`, `WEAK_EDGE`, `PARTIAL_FRAME`, `ORIENT_UNSURE`, `NO_DOCUMENT`, `NO_QUAD`, `ML_UNAVAILABLE`, `RESIDUAL_SKEW`) in the i18n catalogue (PLAN 6.7); UI text is looked up by code; a missing row fails the build.

### CLI
- [ ] **M4.71 CLI detection flags** - Extends M2.43: `--triage strict|balanced|aggressive` with `--min-confidence`, `--hint`, `--detectors`, `--deskew`, `--content-tight`; default follows M4.44; `--json` adds `Analysis`; `doctor` shows ORT, model hashes, calibration. Test: flags, never-write rule.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M4.72 Model fetch and bundle checks** - `xtask fetch-models` downloads assets by `models.lock` SHA-256 (build time only); `xtask check-models` fails CI on bundled models > 25 MB, hash mismatch, unlogged or non-cleared (non-excepted) rows, missing licence files or an M4.01 baseline weight.
- [ ] **M4.73 Cross-OS CI stays green** - macOS arm64 and Linux x64/arm64 use prebuilt ORT and run the ML unit tests; macOS x86_64 builds classical-only until M8 picks rten or ORT `load-dynamic`. Accept: all three OS jobs pass; the README support table says Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0.
- [ ] **M4.74 Windows installer with models** - Extends M3.72 and M3.73: the NSIS installer and portable zip add the models, `onnxruntime.dll`, notices and the console `auto-crop.exe` beside the GUI exe; size delta recorded. Accept: a fresh install analyses a sample image offline.
- [ ] **M4.75 Artifact self-test** - `auto-crop doctor --self-test` from the installed artifacts verifies model hashes, creates sessions and analyses one embedded synthetic image (corners within 0.5 px, orientation right); run on clean Windows 10 22H2 and 11 VMs or Windows Sandbox before tagging.
- [ ] **M4.76 Release pipeline v0.3.0** - release-plz 0.3.0; NSIS installer, portable zip, `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, SBOM, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` with ORT and model attributions; unsigned unless SignPath approved. Accept: README `sha256sum -c` and `gh attestation verify` pass on the tag.

### Docs
- [ ] **M4.77 README table and limitations** - Publish the measured accuracy table per preset with n, Clopper-Pearson intervals and a private-set caveat; limitations cover long receipts, white-on-white, tilt, partial frames, touching items, curved pages, lens distortion and Good/Check/Failed.
- [ ] **M4.78 Model docs and ADRs** - `docs/models.md` and per-release `MODEL_CARD.md` (data-manifest hash, licences, known failures, reproduction, reporting failures without personal images); ADRs: fuse rules, calibration, operating points, bootstrap vs scratch, int8, ORT pin and upgrade rule.
- [ ] **M4.79 Release notes with stage gates** - Release notes list every exit-gate number; a missed target gets its actuals and a stage gate. Balanced is labelled experimental unless G2 passes (M4.44); a flag rate above 15% is a stage-gate note only; the silent-failure bar is never relaxed.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Locked golden v1 (PROVISIONAL): mean quad IoU >= 0.985; failures (IoU < 0.90) <= 2% overall, <= 6% on the hard slice; corner error before refinement median <= 0.15%, p95 <= 0.5% of the diagonal; after refinement median <= 2 px, p95 <= 5 px at 12 MP (synthetic only).
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Skew error: text pages median <= 0.1 and p95 <= 0.25 degrees, photos p95 <= 0.5; orientation top-1 >= 99% with n and interval (EXIF double-rotation = 0 is gated in M2.22/M2.55, the HEIF half in M6.25).
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** ECE <= 0.05 pooled, <= 0.10 worst slice (n >= 30); presets published as risk-coverage points; Balanced is the unlabelled default only if locked-golden silent failures are point <= 1.0% and bound <= 2.0% (never synthetic data), else Strict default and Balanced experimental.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Flag rate per preset is measured and published (expected ~15-20% Strict, ~8-10% Balanced, ~3-5% Aggressive); a Balanced flag rate above 10% (15% at v0.3.0) is a stage-gate note and never changes the default; the silent-failure bar is never relaxed.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Receipt slices (long > 4:1 and > 8:1, thermal fade, partial frame, touching items) each report failure rate with n and interval; the long-strip slice is <= 6% failures at n >= 80 (advisory below) or the documented mitigation (M4.32, M4.49) is in place.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Runtime (PROVISIONAL): `analyse` <= 40 ms p95 over T1 exits and first overlay <= 150 ms p95 in the built app; bundled model files <= 25 MB; cold start <= 1.0 s with model load off the critical path.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** int8 versus fp32: mean IoU delta <= 0.3 pt and p95 corner error rise <= 0.05% of the diagonal on Windows, or int8 is not shipped and the fp32 bundle still meets the size and latency gates.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Provenance log complete for every weight and training data source (each cleared or an owner-granted exception, OSI lineage per A-7); training reproduces from a pinned manifest in `auto-crop-models`; `cargo deny check` and `xtask check-models` are green.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** Check and Failed items are never written and Failed originals stay byte-identical (hash and mtime); a second run over outputs is >= 99% no-op (M2.38); analysis makes no network access (firewall-block run and dependency-graph check green).
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** The installed v0.3.0 artifacts pass `auto-crop doctor --self-test` on clean Windows 10 and Windows 11 environments, and checksum and attestation verification pass.
- [ ] **GATE (G2):** The README and limitations page show the published accuracy table with interval caveats, and the v0.3.0 release notes state every gate result.

## M5 - Batch review workflow, backups panel and trust features

<!-- ms: version=v0.4.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=L -->

**Goal:** Deliver the batch-first workflow (B4, B5, B6): auto-process in the background, hold low-confidence items for review (flagged first), then Save all, backed by a crash-safe SQLite journal, a Backups panel with Restore original and the trust features (opt-in updates, network-blocked privacy CI, Windows signing when SignPath approves).
**Release:** v0.4.0, Windows 10/11 x64 only (B9); NSIS installer and portable zip (Authenticode-signed via SignPath Foundation only if approved, else unsigned with the pending status documented), `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, CycloneDX SBOM, `latest.json` + `.sig` (direct builds), published as an ordinary GitHub release (A-12); the Store-style variant (updater compiled out) is built and tested in CI, not published; winget and Scoop are drafts only (M5.70, submitted by M13.65); macOS and Linux build in CI without artifacts.
**Depends on:** M2 (commit protocol, backups, `library.db` journal, CLI, M2.82 corpus), M3 (shell, `acimg`, editor, M3.17/M3.41/M3.45), M4 (`triage()`, calibrated Good/Check/Failed, M4.44 operating points, M4.63 banner).
**Size:** L (5-8 part-time weeks; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md), [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G9 (batch review): a 300-image mixed batch auto-saves confident results once triaged and holds the rest first, failed originals stay byte-identical, kill and power-loss runs lose 0 originals, Restore works to the retention limit, GUI batch >= 3 img/s at <= 175 MB per in-flight 12 MP image (PROVISIONAL), and the only network use is a user-started or opt-in update check.
**Risks / spikes:**
- Nothing saves until the batch is triaged, so many flagged items feel slow; the silent-failure bar is never relaxed to cut flags (default Strict unless G2 passes, A-3).
- The journal and file system can drift after a crash (M5.56-M5.58); if `FaultFs` is not credible, a kill loop plus a VM power-off per RC is the fallback (M5.57).
- Unverified Windows points are recorded, not hidden: SignPath approval time (M5.68/M5.69), the Explorer verb multi-select limit (M5.26), network attribution on hosted runners (M5.52).

### Core
- [ ] **M5.01 Two-pool BatchScheduler** - Extends M2.03/M3.10: batch pool `max(1, physical_cores - preview)` threads at below-normal priority, one thread per image while queued jobs >= workers, strip-parallel for a lone job. Accept: preview release-to-final p95 <= 250 ms during a 300-image batch.
- [ ] **M5.02 Cancellation, pause and progress** - Pause stops admission (in-flight jobs reach a state boundary); Cancel never interrupts `Committing`; progress over `tauri::ipc::Channel` at 10 Hz. Accept: cancelling a 48 MP job stops its threads within 100 ms; journal and temp files clean.
- [ ] **M5.03 Byte-weighted MemoryBudget** - Extends M1.10/M2.03: public `MemoryBudget::reserve(bytes)` for M6 decode workers and M7 scratch, job weights journalled, `TooLarge` above `max_pixels` (100 MP). Accept: proptest never deadlocks or exceeds the cap except for one lone oversize job.
- [ ] **M5.04 Streaming walk and visible-first queue** - Extends M2.40/M3.04: bounded-channel walk, header probe only when a job starts or its tile is visible, `set_visible(ids)` at 10 Hz. Accept: first 50 of 10,000 files queued in 500 ms; a jump to item 400 lands within 2x pool size.
- [ ] **M5.05 Batch tables and migrations** - Extends M2.32/M2.33: add `sessions`, `images`, `history_entries`, `batches` and `jobs` tables by numbered `user_version` migrations. Accept: every older schema opens, migrates and restores; `integrity_check` clean after 1,000 random kill points.
- [ ] **M5.06 Journal writer thread** - Extends M2.32: one writer thread coalesces progress rows (100 ms flush) and writes `Committing` and `Saved` synchronously before the UI is told; readers are read-only. Accept: <= 3% overhead on the 300-image batch; a shown `Saved` survives `kill -9`.
- [ ] **M5.07 Held-state and history persistence** - Extends M3.17: persist triage results (held reason, `DetectionFailed`, `Skipped`, 256 px thumbnail) and history (cap 200 per image), no sidecar files. Accept: after `kill -9` undo cursor and flagged order are unchanged with zero analyser calls.
- [ ] **M5.08 Startup reconcile and temp sweep** - Extends M2.32: at launch reconcile the M5 batch and session rows with the PLAN 2.7 recovery rules and the one M2.32 temp pattern. Accept: a `FaultFs` test per state; 0 stray temps after the crash suite; an unrelated `.autocrop-x.tmp` stays.
- [ ] **M5.09 Resume commands** - `list_resumable`, `resume_batch(id)`, `discard_batch(id)`: `Saved` skipped, in-flight reconciled, the rest re-queued with recorded parameters (a new engine or model re-analyses). Accept: kill at 40%, resume, hashes equal an uninterrupted `--deterministic` run.
- [ ] **M5.10 Batch triage state machine (B4)** - Wire M4.24 `triage()` into `JobState`: `Approved(Auto)` for Good, `HeldForReview{reason}` for Check or a HoldReason code (notices never hold), `DetectionFailed` for Failed. Accept: proptest: no held or failed job is written without `Approved(User)`.
- [ ] **M5.11 Batch-outlier hold reason** - Armed after whole-batch triage (off in Streaming, UI says so): a robust z-score (area, aspect, skew; 20+ items) caps an outlier at Check (`BATCH_OUTLIER`). Accept: >= 8 of 10 seeded odd crops fire pre-commit; without a net-gain ablation it stays opt-in.
- [ ] **M5.12 Auto-save modes** - `CommitMode`: `AfterAnalysis` (default: one background pass saves confident results after all items are triaged), `Manual` ('Review before saving'), `Streaming` (opt-in, no BATCH_OUTLIER); held items need acceptance; frozen grid sort. Accept: a test per mode.
- [ ] **M5.13 Strictness presets per batch (B6)** - Presets read `calibration.json` cutoffs; Balanced is default only if the locked golden set passes G2, else Strict, Balanced experimental (A-3). Accept: switching re-buckets unwritten items in <= 100 ms; flags Strict >= Balanced >= Aggressive.
- [ ] **M5.14 Session commands with memento** - `SessionCmd` accept, skip, rotate 90, reset over `ThisImage | Selected | All`, journalled with a memento; accept-all means Good only, Check needs explicit selection and a count confirmation. Accept: proptest `undo(redo(x)) == x` on 500 items.
- [ ] **M5.15 Batch name plan and already-processed** - Extends M2.27/M2.38: one name plan for the whole drop (GUI copies in `<source folder>/AutoCrop/`, 'Keep both' = Rename) and an 'Already processed' prompt (Skip, Re-process, Restore). Accept: re-dropping a processed folder writes 0 files.
- [ ] **M5.16 Write-failure pause and summary** - Extends M2.31/M2.34: disk full, permission denied, file in use after retries and name clashes pause affected jobs; summary '309 done, 2 skipped, 1 failed' plus `Retry failed`. Accept: injected faults pause, resume and leave originals byte-identical.
- [ ] **M5.17 Backup listing and index rebuild** - Extends M2.33/M2.47: paged `list_backups` (outputs as a list, lazy `changed_since_saved`); a missing or corrupt `library.db` is set aside, rebuilt from manifests. Accept: a deleted `library.db` rebuilds the same list; 10,000 rows page <= 100 ms.
- [ ] **M5.18 Retention and purge** - Extends M2.35: retention 7, 30, 90, 365 days or Never (default 30) with Keep pin, `Purge now` and an expiry warning; purge at start, every 24 h and on demand, skips pinned runs. Accept: fake-clock test keeps day 29, purges day 31. _(gate: 30-day purge)_
- [ ] **M5.19 Restore original (run level)** - Extends M2.37/M3.16: `restore(run)` ('Restore all from this run') with per-file results. Accept: restored SHA-256 equals the pre-batch SHA-256 after save, restart and at retention day 29 on NTFS. _(gate: restore to the retention limit)_
- [ ] **M5.20 CLI on the shared scheduler** - Extends M2.39-M2.49: `auto-crop process` runs through `BatchScheduler` and the same journal (exit codes 0, 3, 4). Accept: CLI and GUI give identical output hashes on the 300-image corpus (`--deterministic`); a killed CLI run is resumable in the GUI.
- [ ] **M5.82 Per-variant `backups_dir`** - Store-style builds keep `backups/` and `library.db` out of virtualised package data: a real user-folder default chosen at first run, or `unvirtualizedResources` if allowed. Accept: a sideloaded MSIX keeps backups across uninstall/reinstall (M2.28, M6.81).
- [ ] **M5.83 No backup, no overwrite pause** - `BackupFailed` pauses the run with 'Can't back up originals' [Free space] [Change location] [Save copies for the rest]; no job passes `BackedUp` without a verified backup. Accept: read-only store leaves originals unchanged; survives `kill -9`.

### I/O and OS integration
- [ ] **M5.21 Symlink, junction and hardlink loops** - Extends M2.40: the GUI walk counts skipped loops in the folder strip; `xtask make-fixtures` builds symlink-loop, junction-loop and hardlink-pair trees (elevated token on Windows). Accept: the scan terminates and each file is processed once.
- [ ] **M5.22 Cloud placeholders never hydrated** - Extends M2.29/M3.04: `FileAttrs` trait (mockable) drives 'N online-only [Download] [Skip]'; downloads run 2-4 at a time (60 s timeout) only on request, else `CloudNotLocal`. Accept: no hydration before Download (mock tests, manual OneDrive run).
- [ ] **M5.23 Long paths, Unicode and network paths** - Extends M2.29: UNC and mapped-drive roots, NFC/NFD twins, emoji and RTL names, invalid-UTF-8 Linux names (raw bytes in SQLite). Accept: a generated tree round-trips process, backup and restore on Windows and Linux CI by SHA-256.
- [ ] **M5.24 Real-app folder-drop checklist** - `docs/dev/folder-drop-manual.md` run on the packaged app per RC: symlink loop, OneDrive online-only files, SMB share, 5,000 files, extension-less images, 300-character paths. Accept: all rows pass on the v0.4.0 candidate; failures become M5 items.
- [ ] **M5.25 Single-instance aggregation** - Extends M3.06: a launch during an active batch appends to it and toasts 'Added 12 images to the current run'; Open with and the Explorer verb share this 300 ms merge path. Accept: 12 launches in 100 ms mid-batch give one session, no second window.
- [ ] **M5.26 Explorer classic context-menu verb** - Opt-in per-user verb 'Crop and straighten with Auto Crop' on the M3.19 extension list (NSIS `installerHooks`; portable: Settings toggle), defaults untouched, removed on uninstall. Accept: 1, 2, 15, 16, 100 files give one session via M5.25.

- [ ] **M5.28 Tile anatomy and badges** - Icon-plus-word badge (Good, Check, Failed), a reason line for Check and Failed looked up by HoldReason code, state dots; never colour-only, survives `forced-colors`. Accept: Playwright screenshots in light, dark and forced-colors; axe-core clean.
- [ ] **M5.29 Filters, sort and default view** - Filters Needs review, All, Edited, Skipped, Failed, Saved; sort by confidence, name, date; opens on Needs review or 'All N look good'; Saved and All show lowest confidence first. Accept: T8 finds 2 seeded bad auto-accepts among 100 in 3 minutes.

[Review flagged first] [Save 289]'. Accept: E2E on the 300-image corpus writes exactly the Approved set.
- [ ] **M5.31 Failed and held items in grid** - Extends M4.63: Failed tiles and Review show the original under the 'Draw crop' banner, Check tiles their reason line; Draw crop opens Adjust on a 5% inset quad. Accept: an undetectable fixture keeps its SHA-256 after Skip; a drawn crop saves on Accept.
- [ ] **M5.32 Review mode** - Full-screen view with chevrons, swipe (fit zoom only), Accept & next (Enter), Skip (X), Adjust (E); 'Review flagged' walks the flagged queue then offers the rest or Save all; '7 of 23'; focus returns to the tile. Accept: a keyboard-only pass through 25 flagged items.
- [ ] **M5.33 Review versus Adjust gestures** - Extends M3.23: Review swipes with no handles; Adjust has handles, no swipe, `touch-action: none`; the recogniser gains a SWIPE state. Accept: replays show 0 accidental navigations in drags and 0 accidental grabs in swipes; manual touch pass.
- [ ] **M5.34 Strictness and Review settings** - Strict, Balanced, Aggressive selector in the bar and in Settings > Review (the only strictness setting) beside commit mode and 'Open on flagged'; plain captions and the measured share ('23 of 312 flagged'). Accept: captions quote only M5.63 numbers.
- [ ] **M5.35 Progress bar and resume prompt** - Extends M3.17: progress bar with count, rate and ETA, Pause and Cancel; after a crash Home shows a 'Resume previous session?' card (Resume, Discard, Later) with done, held and pending counts. Accept: `kill -9` then relaunch shows it within 1 s.
- [ ] **M5.36 Session actions UI** - Undo and Redo name the operation ('Undo Skip 12 images'); Ctrl/Cmd+Z follows focus (grid = session, editor = per image); History drawer; scope selector (This, Selected, All). Accept: undo of 'Skip 12 images' restores 12 cursors; Selected touches only its ids.
- [ ] **M5.37 Multi-select** - Long-press then tap, Ctrl/Shift click, rubber-band (mouse and pen only), Space, Shift+arrows and Ctrl+A feed a pure selection reducer and a bulk bar with the count. Accept: recorded sequences give expected selections; no rubber-band during a touch scroll.
- [ ] **M5.38 Context menus** - Press-and-hold (400 ms), right-click and Shift+F10/Menu key open one tile menu (Accept, Skip, Adjust, Rotate 90, Restore original, Show in folder); tiles keep native `contextmenu`, the canvas suppresses it. Accept: Playwright covers all triggers; manual touch check.
- [ ] **M5.39 Keyboard review and shortcut sheet** - Extends M3.49: a shortcut registry drives handlers, '?' sheet and docs (M5.76); character shortcuts skip text inputs and can be turned off (WCAG 2.1.4). Accept: drop, review and Save all work keyboard-only; a registry test flags unhandled keys.
- [ ] **M5.40 Accessible names and live regions** - Tile `aria-label` e.g. 'IMG_07, needs review, not saved'; polite progress live region every 5 s or so; summary changes announced; focus to the editor heading on entry. Accept: axe-core clean on grid, review, Backups and Issues; manual NVDA pass.
- [ ] **M5.41 First-run card and first-write sheet** - Extends M3.41: the one-time first-write sheet (not a blocking dialog) shows file count, mode and backup size against free space, with `Replace originals` or `Save copies instead`. Accept: zero writes before it is answered, in both modes.
- [ ] **M5.42 Overwrite prompt and Saving settings** - Save all says 'N files will be replaced, originals kept 30 days'; the global Save as copy switch (off) mirrors on Home, review bar, Save sheet, Settings > Saving. Accept: a toggle updates all surfaces; copies go to `<source folder>/AutoCrop/`.
- [ ] **M5.43 Backups panel** - Expandable runs with thumbnail, name, size, age, expiry, truncated source path and a 'changed since saved' flag; header shows location (M5.82), retention, usage and free space (warn at 10 GB used or 5 GB free); virtualised. Accept: 10,000 rows scroll at >= 30 fps.
- [ ] **M5.44 Backup actions** - Restore (file or run), Keep (pin), Show in folder (Rust, by backup id), Purge now and confirmed 'Delete backup now'; a changed file offers Restore anyway, as copy or Cancel, a missing path Choose folder. Accept: Playwright and engine tests; each confirmation once.
- [ ] **M5.45 Undo versus Restore, summary bar** - Extends M3.38/M3.45: Restore original is in tile menu, Adjust, Backups, summary bar, never on Ctrl+Z; the persistent bar reads '309 done, 2 skipped, 1 failed' with Restore all, Open Backups, Retry failed. Accept: >= 2 of 3 testers choose Restore.
- [ ] **M5.46 Issues tray** - Extends M3.45: batch rows from stable `ErrKind` codes (localised reason, Retry, Show in folder, redacted Copy details); Report issue URL carries only version, OS, webview, channel and error code. Accept: 25 corrupt files give 25 rows and 0 modal dialogs.
- [ ] **M5.47 M5 strings and pseudo-locale** - Extends M3.51/M3.52: M5 strings in the Fluent catalogue with plurals, no English from Rust; pseudo and pseudo-RTL locales run over grid, Review, Backups, Issues, dialogs, update banner. Accept: CI fails on untranslated, clipped or unmirrored text.
- [ ] **M5.79 Try sample images** - Home button copies a synthetic set (`tools/synth`) to a temp folder and runs the batch flow with one deliberately hard image so a Check tile appears; offline, temp files removed on exit. Accept: a fresh profile reaches the flagged tile in < 60 s (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M5.80 Local silent-failure proxy** - Count Restore-original actions on `Approved(Auto)` items in `library.db` ('N of M' in Advanced > Diagnostics and Copy details, never the URL); nothing is sent. Accept: an auto-saved restore increments it, a user-accepted one does not; it survives restart.

### Updates and privacy (B18)
- [ ] **M5.48 Updater compile-out flag and proof** - Feature `updater` (on for NSIS and portable; off for Store-style, Flatpak, winget-managed, distro and no-hevc builds) gates plugin, HTTP client, capability, UI, URLs. Accept: a Store-style build has none (`cargo tree`, strings, capabilities, UI).
- [ ] **M5.49 Manual Check for updates** - One unauthenticated GET of static `latest.json` from github.com (no API host); Install hands off to `tauri-plugin-updater` after the click, portable opens release page. Accept: mock tests: no download before the click; a 0.x build is offered the next 0.x.
- [ ] **M5.50 Opt-in weekly notify-only check** - Off by default; when on, one silent background `latest.json` check per 7 days at idle launch shows a dismissible 'Version X is available' banner, never downloads; no identifier. Accept: fake-clock test: one request per 7 days, none when off.
- [ ] **M5.51 Update key and threat tests** - `tauri signer generate` key kept only in the gated `release` env (loss plan in SECURITY.md), HTTPS-only `github.com` allow-list (+ asset CDN). Accept: mock tests (test-only override, never shipped) reject a wrong key, lower version, off-list redirect.
- [ ] **M5.52 Network-blocked privacy CI** - Extends M4.09: Linux job runs the CLI in `unshare --net` with `strace`; Windows job adds a firewall block and asserts zero Filtering Platform 5157 events outside an update check; WebView2 traffic listed apart. Accept: red on any unexpected destination.
- [ ] **M5.53 Update round-trip test** - `xtask update-e2e` on a clean VM installs `v0.4.0-rc.1`, serves `rc.2` from a mock server, then check, confirm, install, relaunch; `library.db`, backups and settings survive, Restore works, weekly check only shows a banner. Accept: green on Windows 10 and 11.

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M5.54 300-image batch end-to-end test** - Extends M2.82: `xtask batch-e2e` over the corpus asserts Approved files changed and verified, held or failed ones byte-identical, replaced ones backed up. Accept: ground-truth manifest identical on Windows and Linux (hashes: Linux container only).
- [ ] **M5.55 GUI end-to-end on Windows** - Extends M3.57: same harness opens the corpus via the `e2e`-feature `open_paths`, answers the first-write sheet, waits for the auto-save pass, checks flagged tiles come first, then Accept all good and Save all. Accept: green nightly on windows-2025.
- [ ] **M5.56 Crash-kill harness** - Extends M2.58: `xtask data-safety crash-loop` kills a batch at random points and failpoints, relaunches, sweeps; each target is original or verified output, backups exist, resume offered. Accept: 200 iterations a night, >= 1,000 by the tag, 0 violations.
- [ ] **M5.57 Power-loss model (`FaultFs`)** - An `Fs` trait routes writes; `FaultFs` records write, rename and fsync, replays a random prefix dropping un-synced data and directory entries (ALICE-style), then recovers. Accept: 1,000 cuts per commit path (in place, copy, convert) keep the invariant.
- [ ] **M5.58 Journal drift check** - `auto-crop doctor --fsck` reports stale `Saved` hashes, replaced files without rows, backups without rows, rows without backups and orphan temps; read-only, `--repair` only rebuilds index rows. Accept: 5 seeded drift kinds found; crash suite ends fsck-clean.
- [ ] **M5.59 Throughput benchmark and profile** - Extends M2.57: `xtask bench batch` measures JPEG to JPEG img/s at 12 MP over the 300-image corpus at 1, 2, 4, 6, 8 threads with journalling on. Accept: CLI >= 4 img/s on 6 cores, GUI batch >= 3, >= 70% scaling to 8 cores (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M5.60 Memory profile** - Extends M2.57: DHAT (Linux) and `samply` (Windows) over a 300-image batch give a per-stage allocation table and peak working set per in-flight job. Accept: peak RSS <= 175 MB (PLAN Table B) per in-flight 12 MP image and no growth across 3 runs (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M5.61 Large-image batch on 8 GB** - 100 x 48 MP JPEGs in an 8 GB Windows VM and under a capped job object in CI: no OOM, peak RSS within the budget cap, >= 1.2 img/s (PROVISIONAL), a 100 MP file admitted alone; budget-disabled run is the negative control. _(gate: no OOM on 8 GB)_
- [ ] **M5.62 Re-baseline the budgets** - Extends X.04: after M5.59-M5.61 on Tier-M and Tier-L (4C, 8 GB) machines, publish `docs/perf/baseline-m5.md` and update `docs/perf/budgets.md`, each number MEASURED (hardware, date) or PROVISIONAL with a reason. Accept: the PR cites the JSON artefacts.
- [ ] **M5.63 Operating points in batch context** - Run the 300-image corpus and dev tier on all presets; record flag share and silent failures with Clopper-Pearson bounds (synthetic never satisfies B6); confirm M4.44 cutoffs with `BATCH_OUTLIER`. Accept: captions and docs quote only these numbers.
- [ ] **M5.64 Usability run** - >= 3 people (one on touch): T1 first run (Try sample images), T2 restore after reopen, T3 300 images with ~25 flagged in <= 2 min, T8 find 2 seeded bad auto-accepts; no telemetry. Accept: `docs/dev/usability-m5.md` (no significance claimed); findings become items.

- [ ] **M5.66 Licence gate for new crates** - `cargo deny` and `cargo about` pass with `tauri-plugin-updater`, `tauri-plugin-single-instance`, `winreg`, `same-file` and rustls HTTP (no OpenSSL); notices regenerated. Accept: no unexplained exception, no AGPL, GPL or non-commercial crate.

- [ ] **M5.81 Clear session history and thumbnails** - Settings > Privacy action (never touches originals or backups); finished-batch thumbnails expire after N days (PROVISIONAL 30). Accept: after clearing, `library.db` has no thumbnails of finished batches and the resume prompt omits them.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** 300 mixed images (about 25 designed-hard, >= 5 undetectable): once triaged, confident results auto-save, held ones are NOT written and show first, failed originals stay byte-identical with the 'Draw crop' banner; Accept all good and Save all work (M5.30, M5.54, M5.55).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Crash safety: >= 1,000 cumulative kill iterations (200 per night, PROVISIONAL) and `FaultFs` cuts resume from the journal with 0 originals lost, 0 stray `.autocrop-*.tmp`, the resume prompt shown and `doctor --fsck` clean (M5.56-M5.58).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Restore original returns a byte-identical original (SHA-256) after save, after reopening and up to the retention limit; purge removes a backup at day 31, not day 29, with the default 30 days (M5.18, M5.19).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Throughput and memory (PROVISIONAL, re-baselined by M5.62): CLI >= 4 img/s and GUI batch >= 3 img/s at 12 MP on 6 cores; peak RSS <= 175 MB per in-flight 12 MP image (M5.59, M5.60).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** A 100-image 48 MP batch completes on an 8 GB machine with no OOM through the byte-weighted budget, and a 100 MP file is admitted alone (M5.61).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Usability run with >= 3 people: reviewing 300 images with about 25 flagged takes <= 2 minutes (PROVISIONAL) and the 500-image grid scrolls at >= 30 fps (PROVISIONAL) in WebView2 (M5.27, M5.64).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Folder-drop edge cases pass on the packaged app (symlink loops, OneDrive placeholders not hydrated, network paths) and the Explorer verb works for multi-select (M5.22, M5.24, M5.26).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Manual and opt-in weekly update checks work against static `latest.json`; the updater is compiled out of the Store-style build; no silent update and no other network call, per the network-blocked CI (M5.48-M5.53).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** If SignPath has approved, every first-party `.exe`, `.dll` and the installer is Authenticode-signed by SignPath Foundation (vendor DLLs keep valid signatures); otherwise the pending status and SmartScreen guidance are documented (M5.68, M5.69).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** Strict, Balanced and Aggressive flag and silent-failure rates are measured with n and Clopper-Pearson bounds and quoted in captions and docs; default is Strict (Balanced experimental) unless the locked golden set passes G2 (A-3); flags above 10% are a stage-gate note (M5.63).
- [ ] **GATE (G9):** v0.4.0 is published as an ordinary release with `SHA256SUMS` and attestations that verify, the batch, backups, shortcut and held-for-review guides are live, and every budget number is MEASURED (hardware, date) or still PROVISIONAL (M5.73-M5.78).

## M6 - HEIC/HEIF, sandboxed decoding and format read breadth

<!-- ms: version=v0.5.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Deliver HEIC/HEIF to JPG conversion with bundled libheif and libde265 (separate dynamic libraries) in a sandboxed worker pool, the Convert-only preset and Tier 1 read coverage. Official builds always bundle libde265 (A-5); the $0 HEVC read is due before the first published bundling build if practicable, no later than 1.0.

**Release:** v0.5.0, Windows 10/11 x64: NSIS installer and portable zip (signed only if SignPath approval landed after M5, else unsigned with the SmartScreen warning) with `auto-crop-worker.exe`, libheif and libde265 DLLs, `native-sources-0.5.0.tar.zst`, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES`, SHA256SUMS and attestations. macOS and Linux are CI build-and-test only (sandboxes in M8 and M9).

**Depends on:** M5 (batch review, Backups panel); M2.27 and M2.36 (FsPlan, conversion commit); M0.23 and M0.39-M0.45 (sandbox ADR, native recipes).

**Size:** XL (part-time weeks 9-16; definition in X.40; PROVISIONAL)

**Design:** [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)

**Exit gate:** G10 (HEIC): a real-device corpus (>= 30 files, PROVISIONAL) converts with 0 crashes and dE2000 inside the colour gate; HEIC to JPG >= 2.5 img/s; injected worker crashes, bombs and hangs cannot take the app down; libheif >= 1.23.5 with a separate libde265 and no GPL library; legal, fuzz, memory, Convert-only, replace-and-restore and HEIF-orientation gates pass (listed below).

**Risks / spikes:**
- libheif churn (61 advisories in 2026, latest-release-only fixes): sandboxed worker, advisory watcher, 7-day SLA (PROVISIONAL); two missed quarters reopen B12 with the owner.
- HEVC exposure: the $0 read is due before the first published bundling build if practicable, no later than 1.0; no automatic `no-hevc` fallback; never buy the Windows extension (A-5, B15).
- Unmeasured: the 2.5 img/s floor, Windows sandbox depth (job+token leaves network open; AppContainer is M6.07), ImageIO references (maintainer's Mac, A-9). Read-breadth items (M6.34-M6.36, M6.38, M6.68) may slip to v0.5.x (A-4).

### Security/Legal

- [ ] **M6.01 HEVC patent read** - $0 read (about 3 days) per the M0.27 plan (pool policies re-read with dates, distro practice, free-clinic reply, gaps disclosed) in `docs/legal/hevc-read.md`; not legal advice. Due before the first published HEVC build if practicable, no later than 1.0 (A-5).
- [ ] **M6.02 HEVC decision ADR** - ADR `hevc-bundling`: official builds always bundle libde265 (B12, A-5); records the read's outcome, who accepted it, when, and a review date. A `no-hevc`-only release or post-claim change needs the owner's recorded decision; no automatic fallback.
- [ ] **M6.03 Legal gate in release tooling** - `xtask check-legal-gate` fails packaging and the release workflow unless the ADR records the read or the owner's decision to ship with it due before 1.0; it never switches to `no-hevc` (A-5). Test: flip the ADR on a fixture branch.
- [ ] **M6.04 Patent-risk note** - Add the PLAN 8.7 paragraph (ADR-linked) to README and SECURITY: HEIC usually uses HEVC (pool patents); official builds include libde265; if you distribute it or are concerned, use `no-hevc` or your OS decoder; not legal advice. `xtask docs-check` greps a marker.
- [ ] **M6.05 Windows job object** - `windows` 0.62: one job per worker with kill-on-close, active-process limit 1, UI restrictions and a per-item memory limit from the probed size. Test: a worker allocating past its limit is killed, the parent sees `MemoryLimit`, the app keeps running.
- [ ] **M6.06 Restricted token and mitigations** - `CreateRestrictedToken` (privileges dropped, admin SIDs deny-only), low integrity, only pipe and shared-memory handles inherited, mitigations (no win32k, no remote image loads; PROVISIONAL), no WER dialog. Test installed and portable layouts.
- [ ] **M6.07 AppContainer spike and decision** - Extends M0.23: act on the M0.21/M0.23 AppContainer result in a 2-day check of portable-layout ACLs, handle inheritance, shared memory and WIC codec loading; ADR: adopt it, or keep job+token and state what that level does not block.
- [ ] **M6.08 Loader hardening** - `SetDefaultDllDirectories(APPLICATION_DIR | SYSTEM32)` in app and worker; plugin directory loaded by one absolute path before the sandbox applies; `LIBHEIF_PLUGIN_PATH` and extra `PATH` dropped. Test: a planted `heif.dll` or plugin in cwd or `PATH` never loads.
- [ ] **M6.09 libheif limits always set** - Build `heif_security_limits` from `DecodeLimits` (pixels, tiles, items, colour-profile size, memory block; names per 1.23.5) via libheif-rs 3.0 or an audited FFI shim. Test: no path sets unlimited; each limit rejects its fixture with `LimitExceeded`.
- [ ] **M6.10 Fault-injection suite** - Test-only `fault-injection` feature (as M2.58): the worker segfaults, hangs, over-allocates, spawns, writes or opens a socket. App survives, item `Failed(DecoderCrashed|DecodeTimeout|MemoryLimit)`, worker respawns, source intact, blocks match `doctor`.
- [ ] **M6.11 LGPL compliance package** - Ship LGPL-3.0 and GPL-3.0 texts in installer and About; attach `native-sources-<version>.tar.zst` (tags, patches, recipe) plus a written source offer; `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` adds a native section from `native-deps.toml`; nothing statically linked.
- [ ] **M6.12 Automated relink test** - `xtask relink-test` rebuilds libheif from the shipped source archive plus `packaging/relink-test.patch` (version gets `-relink-test`), swaps it into an installed-layout copy and asserts `doctor --self-test` shows the worker loaded it. Windows release job.
- [ ] **M6.13 Native advisory watcher** - Extends M0.10: the daily native-watch issue uses the single label `security-native` and carries the pin-versus-upstream delta and advisory severity for libheif, libde265, dav1d and libjpeg-turbo; links the M6.14 runbook. Test: an older pin raises it.
- [ ] **M6.14 Patch-and-release runbook** - `docs/release-runbook.md`: critical/high advisories patched within 7 days of the fix (72 h for critical), moderate/low within 30 (PROVISIONAL): bump `native-deps.toml`, CI rebuild, patch release. Dry-run a fake bump; two missed quarters reopen B12.
- [ ] **M6.15 Pure-Rust HEIC re-evaluation reminder** - Open a GitHub issue 'Re-evaluate heic-rs and heif-oxide' dated 2027-03-30 (X.18 holds the recurring entry). Adoption bar: MIT/Apache confirmed, full corpus at the dE gate, a fuzzing record, patent question re-read. Not adopted in M6.

### Codecs/I/O

- [ ] **M6.16 libheif binding in `worker`** - Extends M0.43: use the ADR's binding (libheif-rs 3.0 against our prefix, no vcpkg or `embedded-libheif`, else a `[patch]` fork or bindgen crate over ~30 functions) under `worker` only; re-spike (2 days) only if it fails at scale.
- [ ] **M6.17 Format sniffer** - Extends M1.12: magic bytes only: HEIF `ftyp` brands (`heic heix hevc hevx heim heis mif1 msf1`) versus AVIF (`avif avis`), JXL codestream/container, ICO, PNM, QOI, Radiance HDR; TGA by header check. A misnamed `.jpg` HEIC is converted. Property test: never panics.
- [ ] **M6.18 HEIF/AVIF header probe** - Extends M1.12: `codecs::heif_probe`, our ISOBMFF walker with box depth, count and size caps; returns `ispe` size (grid/overlay canvas), bit depth, codec, `irot`/`imir`/`clap`, image count, sequences, Exif/`colr`. The parent sizes shared memory from it.
- [ ] **M6.19 HeicBackend and contract tests** - Extends M1.08: full `HeicBackend` in `core` (probe, thumbnail, `decode_primary(bytes, want, limits, cancel)`), no C (`cargo tree -p core`: no `-sys`); contract suite (orientation, metadata, colour, limits, cancel) for WIC now, ImageIO in M8.
- [ ] **M6.20 LibheifBackend primary decode** - In `worker` only: decode from memory, primary image, RGB(A) 8-bit (U16 for 10/12-bit), libheif applies `irot`/`imir`/`clap`, threads from M6.45, typed errors. Bundled libheif is the deterministic reference; fixtures meet the dE gate.
- [ ] **M6.21 Grid and overlay images** - iPhone grids (512 px tiles, ~48 at 12 MP) and `iovl` overlays compose inside libheif; admission counts the full canvas. Tests: seam error <= 2/255 (PROVISIONAL) at every tile boundary versus the reference; a grid with excessive tiles is rejected (M6.09).
- [ ] **M6.22 Embedded thumbnail** - `thumbnail()` feeds the draft preview and grid tile only, never detection or output; its aspect must equal the primary's after transforms (within 1 px per 1000, PROVISIONAL) else fall back to a scaled full decode. Draft <= 150 ms (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M6.23 Multi-image files and sequences** - Files with several top-level images or `msf1`/`avis` sequences decode the primary image, expose the item list and raise `heic.multi_image` or `heic.sequence`. Never replaced (D3): skipped unless a copy is opted into; source untouched.
- [ ] **M6.24 Auxiliary images and Live Photo** - Detect depth, gain map and matte; keep the SDR base, drop gain map and depth (`heic.gain_map_dropped`, `heic.depth_dropped`; no HDR rebuild). A Live Photo's `.MOV` (same stem or ContentIdentifier) is left alone (`heic.live_photo_video_untouched`).
- [ ] **M6.25 Orientation applied once** - libheif applies `irot`/`imir`, so HEIF Exif Orientation is informational (copying it double-rotates): output Orientation is 1. Test 8 orientations x `irot`/`imir` x HEIC and AVIF pixel-exact vs a rotated reference. _(gate: HEIF double-rotation = 0)_
- [ ] **M6.26 HEIF Exif extraction** - Extends M2.23: extract the HEIF Exif item, skip its 4-byte TIFF-header offset (check `II*\0`/`MM\0*`, else scan 32 bytes, else drop + warn); above 65,527 bytes rebuild a minimal Exif (warn). Property test: non-policy tags survive parse-patch-parse.
- [ ] **M6.27 Strip location on HEIF sources** - Extends M2.23: apply its complete strip-location (GPS IFD, XMP `exif:GPS*`, IPTC location; zero-filled) and stale-data drops to HEIF metadata. Test: no source GPS bytes survive a HEIC-to-JPG output. Device IDs stay by default (C2; M6.78).
- [ ] **M6.28 ICC and CICP reading** - Read ICC first (`prof`/`rICC`), then nclx/CICP (primaries 12 = P3-D65, 9 = BT.2020; transfer 13 = sRGB, 16 = PQ, 18 = HLG) into `ColourTag`. Pre-check profiles (`acsp`, <= 16 MiB PROVISIONAL); unusable = keep pixels, embed original, warn, count (M6.82).
- [ ] **M6.29 sRGB default and wide gamut** - HEIC/HEIF to JPG defaults to sRGB via moxcms 0.9 (relative colorimetric, clipping; Exif ColorSpace = 1); `preserve_wide_gamut` keeps pixels, embeds the source ICC or a P3 profile made from nclx. Matrix-shaper <= 60 ms per 12 MP (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M6.30 HDR tone-mapping** - Own `imgproc::tonemap` for PQ/HLG: 16-bit decode, linearise, 203-nit white to 1.0 (BT.2408), soft-knee roll-off, BT.2020 to sRGB, sRGB OETF; `heic.hdr_tonemapped`. Checked on real HDR files against a dev-only reference plus a visual golden; also HDR AVIF/JXL.
- [ ] **M6.31 JPEG output for conversions** - Extends M2.25: HEIC-source JPEG Balanced q90, 4:2:0; splice JFIF, patched Exif, ICC, XMP; alpha flattened on white (`heic.alpha_flattened`); 16-to-8-bit via fixed 4x4 ordered dither. Decodes identically in turbojpeg, zune-jpeg and Pillow (dev-only).
- [ ] **M6.32 Convert-only output encoders** - Extends M2.25/M2.26: add PNG 8/16-bit (`iCCP`, `eXIf`, `pHYs`), TIFF LZW/Deflate 8/16-bit with ICC and Exif, WebP lossless where missing; G4, AVIF, JXL, lossy WebP stay in M11. Round trip: size, orientation, ICC, Exif, pixel-exact if lossless.
- [ ] **M6.33 Decoder guard worker routes** - Extends M1.13/M1.14: add file-size caps (256 MiB worker formats, 2 GiB streamed TIFF/PNG; PROVISIONAL) and `decode.isolation = native-only | all` (CLI `--isolate`) routing `image`, `png`, `tiff`, `jxl` and zune-jpeg decodes through the worker too.
- [ ] **M6.34 Static raster read breadth** - Via `image` 0.25: BMP (RLE, bitfields, alpha), GIF (first frame, `anim.first_frame_only`), ICO (largest entry), TGA, PNM (16-bit), QOI, Radiance HDR (tone-mapped). Fixtures, typed corrupt failures; animated never replaced (D3); `image-extras` out.
- [ ] **M6.35 PNG and APNG read** - Extends M1.15: `png` 0.18 adds `iCCP`, `cICP`, `sRGB`, `eXIf`, `pHYs` into the Raster, 16-bit kept as U16, APNG default image only with `anim.first_frame_only` (never replaced, D3). PngSuite (licence to verify) plus corrupt-chunk fixtures.
- [ ] **M6.36 TIFF read: multi-page, depth, Fax4** - Extends M1.16: `tiff` adds BigTIFF, tiled, planar, MinIsWhite, IFD loop cap 10,000, CCITT G4 (`tiff`, else `fax` 0.3) and CMYK (warn); multi-page = first page plus `tiff.multi_page`, never replaced (D3). Polarity test vs three readers.
- [ ] **M6.37 AVIF read** - libheif plus dav1d in the worker (not `avif-native`): 8/10-bit, alpha, `clap`, `irot`/`imir`, grids, `avis` first frame with notice; nclx via M6.28-M6.30; within dE <= 0.5 of `avifdec` (PROVISIONAL); works in `no-hevc`. Not replaced until M11 (D3).
- [ ] **M6.38 JPEG XL read** - `jxl` 0.7.x (jxl-rs) in-process behind the guard, dimensions capped pre-allocation; ICC/CICP into `ColourTag`, animation first frame with notice, orientation applied once, PQ/HLG via M6.30; libjxl testdata. Not replaced until M11 (D3, `format.write_unavailable`).
- [ ] **M6.39 16-bit through the warp** - Extends M1.24: keep U16 through crop, rotate, deskew and perspective where the target allows (PNG, TIFF); JPEG and WebP reduce to 8-bit last. Test: identity and integer shifts bit-exact; 3-degree rotation within 2 LSB (PROVISIONAL) of an f64 reference.
- [ ] **M6.40 WIC backend and setting** - `WicBackend` (`windows` 0.62, in-memory `IStream`) behind `heic.engine = bundled | system | auto` (default `bundled`); record if the Store codec loads under the M6.06 token. `auto` stays off until WIC passes M6.19 and dE parity; no purchase (B15).
- [ ] **M6.82 Spike S3.8: moxcms coverage** - Compare moxcms with lcms2 (dev-only oracle, never shipped) on P3, nclx-synthesised, CMYK LUT, gray and v4 profiles; report the corpus rate of unusable profiles (M6.28). Output: an ADR on moxcms coverage and the CMYK route for M6.83.
- [ ] **M6.83 JPEG variant decode policy** - Extends M1.15/M2.20: CMYK/YCCK JPEG decodes to sRGB, warn (moxcms LUT per M6.82, else naive), else typed `UnsupportedFormat`; arithmetic and 12-bit fail typed unless turbojpeg decodes them. Narrows PLAN 3.11 to baseline, progressive, gray, YCbCr.

### Core

- [ ] **M6.41 Worker crate and protocol** - Crate `worker` (bin `auto-crop-worker`), sole libheif crate; `postcard` frames over pipes (Hello, Decode, Thumbnail, Cancel, Result, Error; 32 MiB cap PROVISIONAL); reply fields untrusted. CI: `cargo tree -i libheif-sys` lists only `worker`.
- [ ] **M6.42 Shared-memory pixel return** - Input segment (file bytes, read once) and output segment sized from the M6.18 probe and `max_pixels`; Windows `CreateFileMappingW` by handle inheritance; `Shm` trait for M8/M9. Test: a worker writing past its mapping fails safely; wrapper under `miri`.
- [ ] **M6.43 Persistent worker pool** - `ceil(batch_threads / 2)` workers (PROVISIONAL), respawned, recycled on anomaly, a decode over 50% of a cap or 32 decodes; cancel kills after 1 s. Crashes block retry; two offer "Report this file" (Report issue). Test: 1,000 random crashes, no orphans.
- [ ] **M6.44 Caps and errors** - 100 MP cap (500 MP: Advanced, `--max-pixels`, "allow this file"); memory: job limit (M6.05) and watchdog; timeout 10 s + 1 s/MP (PROVISIONAL). `ErrKind` (M2.05) adds `MemoryLimit`, `DecodeTimeout`, `DecoderCrashed`, `HevcDecoderMissing`, `UnsupportedHeifCodec`.
- [ ] **M6.45 Memory weights and thread clamp** - Extends M1.10/M2.03: measured HEIC weights from peak RSS per MP (8/10-bit, grid, 48 MP; PROVISIONAL); oversize jobs run alone without starving the queue; libheif threads (batch 1-2, lone job <= `min(cores, 4)`), process total <= cores + 1.
- [ ] **M6.46 Notices, colour tag and journal** - `Raster` carries `ColourTag` and `notices`; stable codes (`heic.*`, `anim.*`, `tiff.*`, `format.*`, named where raised) are Info/Warn chips that never hold a job; copy from a code-to-copy table (B20); notices persist per item in the journal.
- [ ] **M6.47 Convert-only pipeline** - Extends M2.36/M2.43: `Preset::ConvertOnly` skips `analyse()` (identity geometry); unchanged format stays `Unchanged`, a format change always encodes; multi-frame and multi-image sources never replaced (D3). Test: JPEG to PNG rotates once, Orientation = 1.
- [ ] **M6.48 HEIC replace and restore (B3)** - Extends M2.36: a HEIC becomes a same-stem JPG (source to the backup store); Restore returns the HEIC (JPG to a pre-restore backup). NTFS tests: crash between the last two steps recovers, a collision gives `IMG_0001 (2).jpg`, paired MOV untouched.

### UI/UX

- [ ] **M6.49 Convert-only preset and flow** - Home preset "Convert only" (remembered): after import skip detection and open the Convert sheet; HEIC/HEIF default to JPG; batches with progress, cancel, summary; only formats this release writes. Playwright: drop 20 HEICs, convert, restore one.
- [ ] **M6.50 Colour and location controls** - Convert sheet: "Preserve wide gamut (Display P3)" toggle (off) and "Strip location" toggle (off) with the chip "N files contain a location" for GPS batches; remembered; maps to `OutputSpec` `preserve_wide_gamut`, `strip_location` (C2). Test both.
- [ ] **M6.51 HEIC notices and error messages** - Summary line and chips name what was dropped (depth, gain maps, Live Photo video, tone-mapping) with a Details drawer; icon plus word. Issues tray (M3.45): `HevcDecoderMissing`, `heic.unsupported_codec`, too large, "could not be decoded safely".
- [ ] **M6.52 Thumbnail-first open** - Opening a HEIC shows the embedded-thumbnail draft (<= 150 ms, PROVISIONAL), then swaps in the display proxy when the full decode lands ("Decoding" state); leaving cancels (M6.43). The draft never feeds detection; no crop overlay before the full decode.
- [ ] **M6.53 Multi-image picker** - For files with several top-level images, an "N images in this file" sheet lists thumbnails; choosing one converts it to a new file in `<folder>/AutoCrop/` and keeps the source (M6.23). Touch (>= 44 px targets), keyboard and screen-reader labels.
- [ ] **M6.54 Settings and About additions** - Settings: HEIC engine (Bundled, System), max pixels, decode timeout (Advanced). About: library versions, sandbox level (`appcontainer`, `job+token`, `process-only`), a refuse-HEIC-unsandboxed option, stale-build banner after 120 days (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M6.55 Accessibility, i18n and touch pass** - All new M6 strings externalised (B20) and correct in the pseudo-locale; axe-core clean on the Convert sheet, notices drawer and picker; full keyboard path from Home to converted files; 44 px minimum targets verified in a touch-emulated run.
- [ ] **M6.84 Usability task T7** - Run T7 (convert HEIC to JPG, location stripped) with >= 3 participants on mouse and Surface touch (PROVISIONAL): they find the default and the Strip location toggle unaided and read the notices. Notes without personal data in `docs/dev/usability-m6.md`.
- [ ] **M6.85 Associations for new formats** - Add M6's read formats (HEIC, HEIF, AVIF, JXL, ...) to the one extension list (M3.19, M13.20): opt-in NSIS `installerHooks` (`OpenWithProgids`, HKCU; default untouched), portable toggle, M5.26 verb keys; uninstall removes them. Clean-VM test.

### CLI

- [ ] **M6.56 `convert` subcommand** - Extends M2.43: `convert <inputs...>` = `process --preset convert-only`; at most one of `--in-place`/`--output`/`--suffix` (default in place with backup); adds `--colour`, `--heic-engine`, `--isolate`, `--decode-timeout`; GUI-identical (`--deterministic`).
- [ ] **M6.57 CLI notices, errors and doctor** - Extends M2.45/M2.48: `--json`/`--ndjson` carry UI notice and error codes, text names drops; `--require-sandbox` exits 6 without one. `doctor` adds library versions and hashes, sandbox level, patent note; `doctor --self-test` decodes a 64x64 HEIC.

### Quality/Perf

- [ ] **M6.58 Real-device HEIC corpus** - Extends M0.49: >= 30 files (PROVISIONAL) with a manifest: >= 8 iPhone P3, >= 3 gain map, >= 2 Live Photo, >= 2 burst, >= 3 grid (>= 2 at 48 MP), >= 5 Android, >= 2 10-bit HDR, >= 1 alpha or depth. GPS stays, so files stay on the maintainer's machine.
- [ ] **M6.59 Reference decodes and dE harness** - `xtask heic-eval`: non-libheif references: ImageIO on the maintainer's Mac only (references stay private), colour charts, and WIC only with a free or OEM HEVC extension, else UNMEASURED (never buy, B15). CIEDE2000 and tag diff (colour GATE).
- [ ] **M6.60 HEIF fixture generator** - macOS CI makes public HEICs via `sips`/ImageIO (no x265; <= 5 MB, CC0): 8 orientations, grid, P3, 10-bit, alpha, 64x64 self-test; a Rust mutator adds unsupported-codec, multi-image, sequence, bad-`ispe` variants. SHA-256s in `testdata/heif/MANIFEST`.
- [ ] **M6.61 Hostile HEIF corpus** - 60000 x 60000 headers, tile, item-count, `iloc` and profile bombs, a solid ~90 MP HEIC, one above 100 MP. Header bombs rejected within 1 s and 64 MB; > 100 MP rejected pre-decode; the 90 MP file is killed under a low memory cap, app up. Every Windows PR.
- [ ] **M6.62 Worker versus in-process differential test** - The `worker` lib exposes the same decode in-process; every fixture (and corpus file, on the maintainer's Mac) decodes both ways with identical pixels, metadata blobs and notices; a mismatch means IPC or shared memory corrupts data.
- [ ] **M6.63 cargo-fuzz targets** - Adds to M1.70: `heif_probe`, `sniff`, `exif_patch`, `icc_validate`, `worker_protocol` (parent side) and `heic_glue` (libheif under ASan on Linux, fixture-seeded). Nightly 1 h per target, 60 s PR smoke on `codecs`/`worker`, crashers replayed on Windows CI.
- [ ] **M6.64 Speed benchmarks** - Tier-M, M0.48 HEIC files (PROVISIONAL): draft <= 150 ms, 12 MP overlay (decode+proxy+analysis) <= 700 ms (48 MP: 400 ms, 3 s), batch HEIC to JPG >= 2.5 img/s on 6 cores (5 = non-gating stretch); tune workers x threads (3 x 2 start); gungraun on glue kernels.
- [ ] **M6.65 Batch memory test** - 100 x 48 MP HEIC batch (real files duplicated under distinct names if needed) on an 8 GB Windows VM, or an outer job object capped at 8 GB in CI. Assert no OOM, no worker killed for memory, oversize jobs ran alone (budget log); record peak commit.
- [ ] **M6.66 OS-decoder benchmark** - WIC versus libheif on public fixtures and M0.48 files: decode p50/p95, peak RSS, dE and metadata parity, no-HEVC-extension behaviour; `docs/perf/heic-os-decoders.md` and an ADR on `heic.engine = auto`. Look up the Store price only (S.13); never buy (B15).
- [ ] **M6.67 HEIC slice in accuracy gates** - Add `format=heic` to eval slices and the worst-slice gate: orientation exact (8 x HEIC, 100%); phone-photo HEIC detection (IoU, failures) within 1 pt of the JPEG slice (PROVISIONAL); >= 30% of phone-photo golden entries HEIC; n rules per PLAN 7.4.
- [ ] **M6.68 Read-format fixture matrix** - Every Tier 1/1b read format: valid files decode 100%, orientation exact, ICC byte-exact where carried, corrupt/truncated files fail typed, no panic, multi-frame notices present; Pillow, `avifdec`, `djxl` (dev-only) agree within a dE bound. 3-OS CI.
- [ ] **M6.69 Windows path robustness** - HEIC and TIFF read and convert with a path over 260 characters, non-ANSI Unicode names, a UNC path, a read-only source and a file open elsewhere; Rust `std::fs` reads the bytes. A test fails if any code path gives libheif a file path.

### CI/Release

- [ ] **M6.70 Promote Windows native build to release** - Extends M0.39/M0.40: `native-deps.toml` pins libheif >= 1.23.5, libde265 >= 1.1.3, dav1d; `xtask build-native` (MSVC, no vcpkg) adds AVIF via dav1d, tile parallelism, other decoders off; libde265 direct-linked if plugin loading fails.
- [ ] **M6.71 macOS arm64 and Linux native recipes** - Extends M0.41: same recipe on `macos-latest` and `ubuntu-22.04`, cached by pin hash; adds process-only fixture decodes (`doctor` reports `process-only`; M8/M9 add sandboxes) and feeds the Linux fuzz jobs. No artifacts.
- [ ] **M6.72 `check-native` release guards** - Extends M0.42: fail CI on libheif < 1.23.5, libde265 not separate, x265/x264 or any HEVC/AVC encoder present, `embedded-libheif`, vcpkg use, tile parallelism off, or a library missing from `packaging/allowed-libs.txt`; a bad prefix fails each.
- [ ] **M6.73 `no-hevc` variant** - Extends M0.43: Cargo feature `hevc` (on in official builds; off = `no-hevc` for distro packagers or an owner-decided fallback) and `xtask build-native --no-hevc`; HEVC files give `HevcDecoderMissing`; updater compiled out; CI-tested; never auto-published (A-5).
- [ ] **M6.74 Windows packaging and self-test** - NSIS and portable zip hold `auto-crop-worker.exe`, console `auto-crop.exe`, `heif.dll` and the `libde265.dll` plugin. Release job: silent install, `doctor --self-test`, relink test (M6.12), uninstall, `check-native` on the tree; portable zip too.
- [ ] **M6.75 Private-corpus HEIC workflow** - Extends M1.51: the HEIC corpus (dE, metadata, speed, memory) runs only on the maintainer's Mac via the private-repo runner (release dispatch), never hosted CI or fork PRs; only aggregates (n >= 30 slices) published; a regression blocks the release.
- [ ] **M6.76 v0.5.0 release** - release-plz notes (HEIC, Convert-only, read formats, sandbox level, patent note, limits); attach `native-sources-0.5.0.tar.zst`, notices, SHA256SUMS, attestations. Tag after every GATE passes; an unfinished legal read goes to the owner (A-5), not to `no-hevc`.
