- [ ] **M6.81 Store dry run** - After the S.28 fee check (fee = owner's call, B15) start M13.01 and submit a hidden direct-link `makeappx` package to learn if the Store accepts bundled HEVC, `runFullTrust`, a helper; record in `docs/dev/store-status.md`. Store-only `no-hevc`: owner's call (A-6).

### Docs

- [ ] **M6.77 Formats doc and README paragraph** - `docs/formats.md`: read matrix by tier with per-format behaviour (first frame, notices, never-replaced cases per D3), limits table (100 MP, file sizes, timeouts; PROVISIONAL), decoder location; README "Formats" matches, planned formats labelled.
- [ ] **M6.78 `docs/heic.md`** - Guide: engine, colour and wide gamut, HDR and gain maps, Live Photo, burst, multi-image, orientation, privacy. Says what "Strip location" removes (GPS IFD, XMP GPS, IPTC location) and what stays (Make, Model, serials, MakerNote, dates); full policy in M11.
- [ ] **M6.79 `docs/replacing-libheif.md`** - Windows steps to swap `heif.dll`, the plugin and `libde265.dll`, rebuild from `native-sources` and verify with `doctor`; macOS, AppImage and Flatpak sections are added in M8 and M9.
- [ ] **M6.80 SECURITY.md decoder section** - What the worker isolates and what the Windows level does not block (restricted token: network, user files); caps, reporting a bad file, patch SLA, stale-build banner; fixes for `HevcDecoderMissing`, "could not be decoded safely", antivirus quarantine.

### Exit gate

- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Corpus - the real-device HEIC corpus (>= 30 files, PROVISIONAL) decodes through the worker with 0 crashes or hangs, run only on the maintainer's Mac (M6.58, M6.63, M6.75).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Colour and metadata - dE2000 vs independent decode: pre-encode mean <= 0.2, max <= 1.0; final JPEG vs same-settings one mean <= 0.5, p99 <= 1.5, max <= 3 (PROVISIONAL); tags kept bar Orientation, thumbnail, dimensions, ColorSpace, opt-in GPS; kept ICC byte-exact (M6.59).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Speed (PROVISIONAL) - HEIC to JPG >= 2.5 img/s on 6 cores at 12 MP (5 is a non-gating stretch); 12 MP final overlay <= 700 ms and thumbnail draft <= 150 ms on Tier-M; a miss goes to an ADR and the owner, never relaxed silently (M6.64).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Sandbox - an injected crash, hang or bomb never kills the app (item failed, worker respawned, source intact); the memory cap kills a bomb HEIC; 100 MP and `10 s + 1 s/MP` caps hold; `doctor` reports the job+token or AppContainer level (M6.05, M6.06, M6.10, M6.44, M6.61).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Native libraries - shipped libheif >= 1.23.5 with libde265 as a separate dynamic library; `check-native` finds no x265, x264 or GPL library; the relink test passes; LGPL notices and source archive ship; the `no-hevc` variant builds and passes CI (M6.11, M6.12, M6.72, M6.73).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Legal - the HEVC read is done and the `hevc-bundling` ADR accepted before the first published bundling build, or the owner records shipping with the read due before 1.0 (A-5); README and SECURITY carry the patent note; no automatic `no-hevc` fallback (M6.01-M6.04).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Fuzzing - cargo-fuzz on probe, sniff, metadata, ICC, worker protocol and heic glue runs nightly with 0 crashes for 7 consecutive nights (PROVISIONAL) (M6.63).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Memory - a 100 x 48 MP HEIC batch completes without OOM under the byte-weighted budget on an 8 GB machine (M6.65).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Convert-only - the preset works end to end in the GUI and via `--preset convert-only` for HEIC, HEIF, AVIF, JXL and Tier 1 read formats to JPEG, PNG, TIFF and WebP lossless, also without the Windows HEVC extension (M6.47, M6.49, M6.56, M6.68); slip rule in Risks.
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** Replace and restore - with overwrite on, a HEIC becomes a same-stem JPG, the source sits in the backup store and Restore original returns the HEIC byte-identical, including under crash injection (M6.48).
- [ ] **GATE (G10):** HEIF orientation - 8 orientations x `irot`/`imir` x HEIC and AVIF come out upright with Orientation reset to 1 and 0 double-rotations, pixel-exact against a rotated reference (M6.25); completes the M4 EXIF/HEIF gate.

## M7 - Enhancement and bilevel output

<!-- ms: version=v0.6.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Deliver the readability promise: a classical, CPU-only, pure-Rust flatten-then-threshold pipeline with a one-tap live before/after suggestion, four modes (Original, Auto, Grayscale, B&W), five sliders and 1-bit export (PNG, TIFF CCITT G4), gated on Tesseract 5 CER and DIBCO metrics. Enhancement is suggested, never forced (B13): B&W is anti-aliased 8-bit, 1-bit is an export option and despeckle is a separate Advanced switch, off for receipts.
**Release:** `v0.6.0`, Windows 10/11 x64; NSIS installer and portable zip (unsigned unless SignPath has approved, B15), `SHA256SUMS`, GitHub artifact attestations; CI still builds macOS and Linux with no release artifacts; no new models or native DLLs.
**Depends on:** M1 (kernels, oracles and corpora extended here: M1.27, M1.30-M1.38, M1.65-M1.68), M2 (`analyze`, M2.81), M4 (quad and page mask, `ort`, M4.36 flatten), M5 (review grid, Backups panel, Restore original, batch presets). M6 precedes M7 in release order but is not a dependency.
**Size:** XL (definition in X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 5](docs/plan/05-enhancement.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G3: pinned Tesseract 5 CER (thresholding fixed) median delta <= 0, >= 20% relative cut on the low-contrast slice, <= 2% of images worse by > 1 pt; zero lost amount tokens on the synthetic suite (private bound no worse than -0.5 pt); DIBCO F-measure within 1 pt of a tuned Sauvola. Slider tick <= 16 ms on the 2 MP display proxy, `enhance` 120 ms (Auto/Grayscale) and up to 250 ms (B&W/Faded), no overflow at 100 MP, >= 20 artefact cases, 1-bit PNG and TIFF G4 readable in >= 3 readers; all PROVISIONAL until measured.
**Risks / spikes:**
- Changed amounts are the worst failure: B&W or despeckle can erase decimal points and faint print. Guards: clamp [64, 208] (M7.16), context-aware despeckle (M7.23), risk holds (M7.24, M7.42), 100% tiles (M7.45), amount-token gates (M7.50, M7.55); no receipt validation exists yet (Bako et al. credit none).
- Work order: harness and data first (M7.50-M7.55), then spikes E1a (M7.56), E1b (M7.57), E2 (in M7.29), E3 (M7.58), E4 (M7.59); every default is a literature value, PROVISIONAL until they report. Tesseract is a dev-only proxy (OCR stays a non-goal, B19).
- Overwrite-by-default (B3) plus a lossy edit makes M5 backup and Restore a precondition (M7.67, M7.80); flattening artefacts (M7.07-M7.09, M7.62); own kernels and TIFF wrapper to maintain. Deferred: PDF/G4 and multi-page TIFF (M11), per-item enhance (M10), dewarp mask (M12), arm64 re-measure (M8, M13); JBIG2 not planned.

### Enhancement
- [ ] **M7.01 Extend Enhance in EditState** - Extends M1.04 `enhance`: `look`, `bw` Sauvola|Nick, Advanced overrides, `est` (`algo_ver`, `text_h`, levels, gamma, paper RGB) with migration and `render_hash`; no bit-depth field. Accept: proptest `undo(redo(x)) == x`, JSON snapshot, unknown fields.
- [ ] **M7.02 Enhancement module and stage cache** - `imgproc::enhance` stages P proxy, A analysis/flatten, T tonal, S window stats, R threshold/ramp, E export, cached by the `EditState` fields each reads. Accept: Sharpness re-runs only T, Text darkness only R; stale gen stops within 64 rows.
- [ ] **M7.03 Validity-mask input** - takes the geometry page mask (0 = outside paper) and excludes it from all statistics; B&W fills it white, Auto and Grayscale keep the nearest gain. Accept: a receipt on a dark table gets a paper estimate within 2 levels of the same paper cropped tight.
- [ ] **M7.04 Analysis proxy** - area-average downscale (`fast_image_resize` 6.1) to ~1.5 MP by area, long edge <= 3072 px, never upsampled (a long-edge rule leaves an 8:1 receipt at ~0.3 MP). Accept: 8:1 and 1:1 fixtures land in 1.2-1.8 MP; build <= 10 ms at 12 MP on Tier-M.
- [ ] **M7.05 Text-height and dpi_eq estimator** - median height of the dominant ink-component size = `text_h` (< ~30 components: short side / 40); photos `dpi_eq = 300 x text_h_px / 26` (26 calibrated in M7.57), scans use DPI metadata. Accept: pages at 100-400 dpi give `text_h` within +-15%.
- [ ] **M7.06 Block percentile grid** - Extends the M4.36 flatten (M1.65 prototype, one shared kernel): 1.75 x `text_h` blocks (16-64 px), half-block stride, mean RGB above the 88th luma percentile via 256-bin histograms. Accept: within 1 level of a sort-based percentile; grid <= 15 ms at 1.5 MP.
- [ ] **M7.07 Block rejection rules** - reject blocks with luma < 0.60 x median (black bands), ink > 35% (barcodes), chroma > 12 Cb/Cr units off paper or outside the mask; > 40% rejected raises `unreliable_estimate`. Accept: barcode, black-band and coloured-header fixtures reject the right cells.
- [ ] **M7.08 Inpaint and smooth the illumination grid** - push-pull diffusion fills rejected cells on the coarse grid (~10k), then median and Gaussian smoothing; no full-resolution map, no content inpainting. Accept: no ghost patch behind a reverse-video header; identical at 1 and 8 threads.
- [ ] **M7.09 Gain map and apply kernel** - `L_eff = P + s (L - P)` (`P` 90th percentile per channel, `s` 1.0 Receipt, 0.6 Document), gain `clamp(255 / L_eff, 1, 3.0)` (1.5 Document), Q8.8 bilinear, in place; Keep paper tint = luma-only gain. Accept: soft shadows leave paper std <= 3 levels.
- [ ] **M7.10 Tone stage** - black point at the 0.75th percentile of flattened luma, white point 242, gamma 0.8-1.0, one LUT; almost no ink (black point > `w - 60`) skips the stretch and sets `low_contrast`. Accept: blank pages get no noise amplification; Auto on a clean scan moves mean luma <= 2.
- [ ] **M7.11 Chroma clean-up and unsharp** - blur Cb/Cr (sigma 2 px scaled) and core chroma so stamps keep colour; luma denoise only if post-gain MAD noise > 4 levels; luma unsharp (sigma 1.0, amount 0.5). Accept: stamp fixture within delta-E2000 <= 6; noisy-shadow fixture triggers denoise.
- [ ] **M7.12 Stored estimates and re-estimation** - `est` (with `algo_ver`) drives sliders as offsets and export reuses it; no stored grid; an `algo_ver` bump re-estimates and shows "result changed after update". Accept: preview equals export byte for byte; an old `algo_ver` shows the notice.
- [ ] **M7.13 SIMD dispatch** - `wide` or `pulp` (stable Rust) for gain, LUT, unsharp, threshold; AVX2 runtime dispatch (C1 floor), scalar fallback, NEON compiled; integer stages bit-exact across paths, f32 within 1 level. Accept: cross-path golden passes on Windows and Linux CI.
- [ ] **M7.14 Flatbed Document path** - `V = (p95 - p5) / P` on the smoothed map; `V` < 3% skips flattening (one LUT, ~4 ms at 12 MP), else a mild flatten (4-6 x `text_h` blocks, `s` 0.6, gain clamp 1.5). Accept: flat scan takes the LUT-only path; a lid-shading fixture (V ~ 8%) ends below 2%.
- [ ] **M7.15 Look selection and suggestion heuristic** - `suggest_enhance(analysis) -> Option<Look>`: Document for scan-like sources, else Receipt; fires at paper >= 55%, ink 1-25% and a visible Auto effect, never under Photos. Accept: dev precision >= 90%, no suggestion on general photos.
- [ ] **M7.16 Sauvola kernel with u64 strip integrals** - Extends M1.27: `R` 128, `k` 0.25, `T` clamped to [64, 208], ~256-row strips with `r_window` halo, f32 SIMD. Accept: within 1 level of naive; identical at 1 and 8 threads and any strip height. _(gate: no overflow at 12, 48, 100 MP)_
- [ ] **M7.17 NICK kernel and Faded preset** - Extends M1.27: NICK (`k` -0.10, `T_hi` 232) on M7.16 stats; Faded preset (light denoise) offered as a chip when the faded-print detector trips, never silent. Accept: within 1 level of naive; on the fade ladder NICK keeps more ink than Sauvola.
- [ ] **M7.18 Soft ramp and B&W pipeline** - `out = 255 x smoothstep((Y_f - (T - rho)) / (2 rho))`, `rho` 6 (swept 2-24), gives anti-aliased 8-bit after flatten, luma and unsharp 0.3; window = sweep value x `dpi_eq / 300` (51 px until M7.56). Accept: 1-channel; ink within ~25% of flattened Otsu.
- [ ] **M7.19 Mode wiring and defaults contract** - Original bypasses the enhancer (lossless JPEG path kept), Auto keeps colour and whitens paper, Grayscale = Rec. 709 luma, B&W per M7.18. Accept: defaults test: mode Original, B&W 8-bit, despeckle off for receipts, no bit depth in `EditState`.
- [ ] **M7.20 Five sliders and Advanced overrides** - Brightness, Contrast, Text darkness, Clean-up (colour and noise; never despeckle), Sharpness: -100..100 offsets from `est`, mapped per PLAN 5.5; Advanced: shadow strength, window size. Accept: all at 0 reproduce `est`; each monotonic.
- [ ] **M7.21 Luma-only CLAHE (Advanced, off)** - own implementation (clip ~2, 8x8 tiles, bilinear blend), Advanced only and off in every look; MIT `clahe` 0.1.3 is a dev-dependency reference only. Accept: mean abs difference <= 2 levels vs the reference; unreachable from defaults.
- [ ] **M7.22 Keep colour marks** - chroma mask (>= 24 units, area >= 150 px^2 scaled) keeps stamps and signatures in colour inside B&W; output is 3-channel, so 1-bit and G4 are disabled with a reason. Accept: hue within the M7.11 bound; 1-bit with marks returns a typed error.
- [ ] **M7.23 Context-aware despeckle** - separate Advanced switch (off for receipts, on for documents): removes specks <= 4 px^2 at 300 dpi with no neighbour within 1.0 x `text_h`, sparing decimal points and commas; never on proxies. Accept: decimal points and commas survive at 150-300 dpi.
- [ ] **M7.24 Readability-risk signals** - `ReadabilityRisk` HoldReason codes: faded print, over/under-inking (ink > ~25% off Otsu), lost marks (> ~5% of small dark components missing), unreliable estimate (M7.07).
- [ ] **M7.25 Tiled full-resolution render** - `render_tile(region, scale)` with a halo of `r_window + r_unsharp`, so a full-res tile equals the same crop of a whole-image render and tonal modes match the proxy within rounding. Accept: pixel-exact at 5 random regions for Auto and B&W on 12 MP.
- [ ] **M7.26 Colour management rule** - enhanced output is sRGB (PLAN 3.7): wide-gamut input converts with `moxcms` first (ICC before nclx); Original keeps pixels and profile; "Preserve wide gamut" shows a note. Accept: P3 chart through Auto within mean delta-E2000 <= 1 of an sRGB reference.
- [ ] **M7.27 ONNX model slot (empty)** - trait `GainModel` returns only a low-passed gain map replacing stage A's estimator; none ships; 1.0 loads only a `models.lock`-pinned file (user models B.13, no download). Accept: a pinned identity ONNX gives a neutral gain; empty slot = M7.09 output.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M7.28 Bilevel conversion and 1-bit PNG** - `to_bilevel(gray8, cut = 128, invert)` from the anti-aliased result (no dithering); 1-bit gray PNG via `png` 0.18 with `pHYs` from source dpi. Accept: black text on white decodes black in 3 readers; pixel-exact round trip.
- [ ] **M7.29 TIFF CCITT G4 wrapper (includes spike E2)** - confirm `fax` polarity, then own TIFF wrapper (`fax::tiff::wrap` hardcodes 200 dpi): Compression 4, both photometrics, real resolution (none if unknown), `RowsPerStrip`. Accept: IFD byte snapshot; pixel-exact round trip (`fax`, `tiff`).
- [ ] **M7.30 True single-channel 8-bit outputs** - Grayscale and B&W write 1-channel files in the source format (gray JPEG via `turbojpeg` >= 3.1.4, q >= 90; PNG and TIFF stay). Accept: files open in 3 readers; text-region SSIM >= 0.98 vs PNG at q90.
- [ ] **M7.31 1-bit export plumbing** - `OutputSpec.bit_depth: Auto8 | OneBit{Png | TiffG4}` via the M2 commit protocol, typed error for colour; makes 1-bit PNG/TIFF sources replaceable (D3). Accept: B&W then 1-bit TIFF verifies against the bilevel raster; write-failure leaves the original intact.
- [ ] **M7.32 Independent reader interop matrix** - `tools/interop/` script plus Windows checklist: 1-bit PNG and G4 TIFF fixtures (100-600 dpi, no dpi, multi-strip, both polarities) in >= 3 readers (libtiff, ImageMagick, Photos). Accept: pixels, polarity, dpi match; mismatch blocks the gate.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M7.33 IPC contract for enhancement** - extend `set_edit` with the Enhance patch; add `get_enhance_suggestion(id)` and `render-ready` events for the 2-4 MP display proxy; one render in flight. Accept: TS bindings diffed; a 100-tick slider script never shows out-of-order renders.
- [ ] **M7.34 Suggestion chip** - chip when `suggest_enhance` fires, hidden under Photos and after dismissal; one tap applies Auto and opens before/after; Revert is one tap and undoable; never auto-applied. Accept: chip on a receipt, none on a landscape photo; Revert restores the pre-tap state.
- [ ] **M7.35 Live before/after and hold-to-compare** - on the M3.34 compare: divider (48 px hit area, arrow keys 1% and 10%), hold-B compare, both sides from one display proxy, button alternative. Accept: drag frame median <= 17 ms, p95 <= 20 on a 2 MP proxy; works by finger on a Surface.
- [ ] **M7.36 Enhance panel: modes and Strength** - segmented control Original | Auto | Grayscale | B&W (44-48 px, icon plus label), one Strength slider mapped to the five sliders, a "Faded print" chip in B&W. Accept: screenshots at three breakpoints; Strength 0 equals the `est` baseline.
- [ ] **M7.37 Fine-tune sliders** - Fine-tune reveals the five sliders (Clean-up = colour and noise only) with numeric fields, ARIA slider keys and double-tap reset; one render request in flight during a drag. Accept: E2E drags each slider; a screen reader announces the value.
- [ ] **M7.38 Advanced section** - collapsed: shadow strength, window size, method (Sauvola/NICK), Despeckle switch (off for receipts; caption "Can erase decimal points and faint print."), CLAHE, paper tint, colour marks; no bit depth. Accept: E2E toggles give the expected EditState diff.
- [ ] **M7.39 Gesture-coalesced history and Reset to auto** - a slider drag is one named history entry via `gesture` and `phase`; "Reset to auto" is one undoable entry; Undo/Redo and Ctrl+Z cover enhancement. Accept: a 60-tick drag makes 1 entry; undo restores the byte-identical render (proptest).
- [ ] **M7.40 Scope selector (This / Selected / All)** - in the editor and the grid; Selected and All are one session command with memento (M5.14); customised images show a dot; `Item.enhance_override` survives scope changes. Accept: apply to 50 images, then one undo restores all 50.
- [ ] **M7.41 Batch enhance banner and preset default** - presets set `Enhance: Off | Suggest | Auto` (default Suggest, never silently Auto); banner "N look like receipts [Preview 3] [Apply to all]" is one undo, re-renders from the pristine backup (M7.80). Accept: count equals `suggest_enhance`.
- [ ] **M7.42 Review-grid per-item toggles and risk triage** - per-item enhance toggle on cards plus risk badge; a `ReadabilityRisk` hit holds the item in Check, listed first, unwritten until accepted. Accept: 3 faded receipts sort first in Check; a toggle changes only its `enhance_override`.
- [ ] **M7.43 Readability warning banner** - banner "Faint print detected. B&W may drop small marks. Check amounts." (copy by code) with Use Auto, Try Faded preset, Show original, plus badge. Accept: shows on fade-ladder fixtures below threshold; each button edits EditState as named, undoable.
- [ ] **M7.44 First B&W save notice** - one-time dialog on the first B&W or Faded in-place save: original backed up (default 30 days), restorable from Backups; offers "Save copies instead"; hidden after "Don't show again". Accept: E2E: shown once; "Save copies instead" switches to copy mode.
- [ ] **M7.45 100% zoom tile preview** - at zoom >= 100% the view renders from full-resolution tiles (`render_tile`, M7.25), so it matches the export; before/after uses the same tiles. Accept: exported pixels equal displayed pixels at 5 regions; a 512 px tile renders <= 100 ms at 48 MP.
- [ ] **M7.46 Export dialog: 1-bit options** - Advanced "1-bit output" as PNG or TIFF G4, disabled for colour modes, warning when G4 exceeds the 8-bit PNG; default 8-bit; the sheet suggests PNG for B&W. Accept: B&W then 1-bit TIFF opens in 3 readers; warning fires on a speckled fixture.
- [ ] **M7.47 i18n and accessibility pass** - new strings externalised (B20); pseudo-locale CI shows no truncation at +40%; RTL mirror check on the panel; keys N cycles mode, hold B compares, Ctrl+Shift+A resets; axe-core clean. Accept: pseudo-locale and axe jobs green; NVDA reviewed.
- [ ] **M7.48 Usability task T6 and touch check** - run T6 (compare, apply, revert) with >= 3 participants on mouse and Surface touch; success = revert in one tap without help; fix blockers. Accept: notes (no personal data) committed; zero unrecovered failures; drags verified on real touch.

### CLI
- [ ] **M7.49 CLI enhancement flags** - `--enhance off|auto|gray|bw` (default off; risk-flagged items held), `--bits 8|1`, `--bilevel png|tiff-g4`, look/faded/despeckle/slider flags; `analyze` (M2.81) adds suggestion, risk. Accept: `--preset receipt` = `--enhance off`; 1-bit with colour refused.

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M7.50 Eval: Tesseract CER and amount-token metric** - Extends M1.66: enhance suite with pinned Tesseract 5.5.x, thresholding 0 and 2; paired CER, amount tokens (`\d+[.,]\d{2}`), share worse by > 1 pt, bootstrap CI; slices n < 30 suppressed. Accept: `xtask eval --suite enhance` repeatable.
- [ ] **M7.51 Eval: DIBCO metrics and tuned Sauvola baseline** - Extends M1.36/M1.38: fetch Doxa BinBench (CC0) with DIBCO; F-measure, pseudo-F, PSNR, DRD cross-checked against Doxa; tuned Sauvola baseline from a window and `k` sweep. Accept: within 0.1 pt of Doxa on 3 images. _(gate: F-measure)_
- [ ] **M7.52 Eval: public receipt corpora** - Extends M1.36/M1.37: fetch SmartDoc-QA (CC BY 4.0, ~13 GB zip, transcriptions); CORD comes from M1.37; SROIE stays excluded until its licence is confirmed. Accept: fetch resumes and verifies checksums; filtered CER subset list (paths only) committed.
- [ ] **M7.53 Synthetic receipt generator** - Extends M1.30-M1.33: 203 dpi dot-font receipts with price columns, barcodes, reverse-video headers, coloured paper, stamps, 8:1 strips and a thermal-fade ladder (contrast 10-60%). Accept: per-image JSON (text, tokens, tags); >= 500 images generated.
- [ ] **M7.54 Private receipt slice** - Extends M1.39/M1.67 (X.06): 80-150 transcribed receipts across thermal fade, paper colour, shadow, glare, stamps, aspect > 4:1, paired 300 dpi scans where possible; private (B21). Accept: thermal-fade >= 30, other strata >= 6, else a recorded stage gate.
- [ ] **M7.55 Digit and decimal-point test set** - private and synthetic thermal-fade receipts, amounts with `.` and `,` at 150-300 dpi-equivalent per mode, despeckle on and off; token lost only if the original read it. Accept: a mode losing a token on the synthetic suite is blocked as default.
- [ ] **M7.56 Spike E1a: window, k and ramp sweep** - Extends M1.68: Sauvola window 31-201 px plus a Tesseract-like arm, `k`, NICK `k`, `rho` 2-24, clamp; choose by worst slice, confirm on the locked split. Accept: tables in `docs/enhancement-sweep.md`; PROVISIONAL tags drop where measured.
- [ ] **M7.57 Spike E1b: flatten variants and dpi_eq calibration** - Extends M1.68: flatten-then-global-Otsu vs Sauvola, block percentile and factor sweeps, luma vs per-channel, sRGB vs linear-light gain; calibrate the 26 px `dpi_eq`. Accept: frozen table in `imgproc::enhance::defaults`.
- [ ] **M7.58 Spike E3: kernel benchmarks** - compare u64 strip integrals, sliding column sums and wrapping-u32 accumulators at 12, 48 and 100 MP on Windows Tier-M; pick by speed within the memory budget; re-baseline every PROVISIONAL time. Accept: a different pick amends M7.16 and reruns M7.60.
- [ ] **M7.59 Spike E4: signal thresholds** - fit the faded-print, over-inking, lost-mark and suggestion thresholds on the dev set, precision favoured; confirm once on the locked split. Accept: precision and recall recorded; the M7.15 and M7.24 constants updated and PROVISIONAL tags dropped.
- [ ] **M7.60 Kernel correctness and degenerate-input suite** - proptest: strip-height and thread independence; blank, 1x1, tiny and 8:1 inputs and extreme sliders never panic or NaN; integer stages bit-exact scalar vs AVX2; dev oracles within 1-2 levels. Accept: green on three-OS CI.
- [ ] **M7.61 Large-image overflow and memory tests** - Extends M1.27: Auto and B&W at 12, 48, 100 MP: Sauvola matches naive; `imageproc`'s u32 integral is the negative control; peak RSS <= 3x decoded RGB8 + 64 MB; 4 concurrent 48 MP jobs fit `MemoryBudget`. Nightly. _(gate: no overflow)_
- [ ] **M7.62 Artefact visual regression suite** - >= 20 cases (barcodes, reverse-video headers, hard shadows, coloured paper, stamps, glare) with ghost-patch and seam-strength detectors; `image-compare` 0.5 SSIM goldens (never `dssim-core`). Accept: every case passes its documented threshold.
- [ ] **M7.63 Benchmarks and slider budget** - Extends M1.57: benches per stage and full pipeline at 12, 48, 100 MP; slider test on the 2 MP display proxy (p95 compute <= 16 ms); IPC <= ~50 ms. Accept: `enhance` 120 ms Auto/Grayscale, up to 250 ms B&W/Faded (total <= 830 ms, variant row).
- [ ] **M7.64 gungraun instruction-count gates** - Extends M1.58: add downscale, percentile grid, gain apply, LUT, unsharp and the Sauvola strip at each SIMD level (scalar, AVX2) to the gate (> 5% fails, 2% warns). Accept: an injected 10% slowdown in an M7 kernel fails the workflow (canary).
- [ ] **M7.65 arm64 and NEON CI** - Extends M1.78: run the M7 kernel and NEON bit-exactness tests on best-effort `windows-11-arm` and `ubuntu-22.04-arm` jobs; open an issue to re-measure M7 budgets on Windows arm64 and Apple silicon (closed by M8.65). Accept: arm64 jobs green; issue linked.
- [ ] **M7.66 Fuzz targets** - `cargo-fuzz` on the enhance pipeline (random sizes, masks, extreme parameters), the Sauvola strip and the TIFF G4 wrapper; 1 h per target nightly (counts toward >= 72 h before 1.0), 60 s PR smoke for the G4 target. Accept: 0 crashes, hangs or OOM; corpus committed.
- [ ] **M7.67 Data-safety test for enhanced saves** - Extends M5 suite: enhance (B&W, Faded) then Save in place makes a verified backup; Restore original after restart is byte-identical; a fault never leaves a lossy result without a backup; the guard treats the enhanced output as processed.
- [ ] **M7.80 Test: enhance after a crop-only save** - crop-only auto-save, then batch B&W or Faded and save again: one backup per file (pristine); Restore after restart returns pristine bytes; the guard offers "Re-process from original"; a second-write fault keeps a verified file and the backup.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M7.68 Dependency licence audit** - extend the `cargo-deny` allow-list for `fax`, `wide` or `pulp`, `moxcms` and `png`; no AGPL, GPL, non-commercial or static LGPL; ban `purecv` and `bgustreadimg`; `clahe`, `imageproc` dev-only. Accept: `cargo deny check` green; notices regenerated.
- [ ] **M7.69 Dataset and tool licence register** - Extends M1.74 (`docs/provenance.md`): DIBCO/H-DIBCO per-year licence verified before CI use, Doxa, SmartDoc-QA and CORD (CC BY 4.0), Augraphy, Tesseract, Leptonica, OpenCV; SROIE excluded. Accept: every corpus has a row and a checksum.
- [ ] **M7.70 Oracle isolation** - Tesseract, `opencv-rust`, Leptonica and Doxa are used only from `eval` and `xtask` behind an `oracle` feature no shipped crate uses. Accept: CI runs `cargo tree -e normal` on the shell crate and fails on any oracle crate; release artefact has no oracle binary.
- [ ] **M7.71 Hostile-input limits for the enhancer** - honours the M1.13 pixel cap, registers scratch with `MemoryBudget`, uses rayon `par_iter`/`scope` only, runs under per-item `catch_unwind`. Accept: an injected panic fails only that item; a 100 MP B&W job fits the budget or fails typed.

### Docs
- [ ] **M7.72 Enhance user guide** - `docs/guide/enhance.md`: modes, sliders, why 1-bit is an export option and despeckle is off for receipts, PNG vs TIFF G4 (measured sizes), undo vs Restore original; synthetic screenshots only. Accept: non-author read against the T6 notes; linked from Help.
- [ ] **M7.73 Known-limitations page** - `docs/known-limitations.md`: coloured paper, hard shadows, glare, reverse-video, faded ink, wavy paper, G4 growth, no JBIG2, no receipt validation, Tesseract proxy; each with symptom, cause, workaround. Accept: each cites a fixture; linked from the banner.
- [ ] **M7.74 Design doc and ADRs** - `docs/design/enhancement.md`: parameter table (measured or PROVISIONAL), colour rule, model slot; ADRs: no wgpu, no ML enhancement in v1 (DocEnTr, DE-GAN excluded), no JBIG2, own kernels not OpenCV. Accept: parameter table generated from `defaults`.
- [ ] **M7.75 Publish aggregate evaluation results** - `docs/enhancement-eval.md`: DIBCO F vs tuned Sauvola, pooled CER medians, amount-token match, speed, sweep, defaults; aggregates only, n < 30 slices suppressed; states Bako et al. did not test receipts. Accept: links the release-commit JSON.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M7.76 Per-PR enhancement smoke gate** - `xtask eval --suite smoke --enhance` on the 200-image synthetic set blocks on median delta-CER > 0, amount-token drop, F-measure drop > 0.3 pt on its binary truth (DIBCO runs nightly) or artefact failures. Accept: a broken kernel PR fails.
- [ ] **M7.77 Nightly and private evaluation** - Extends M1.51/M1.52/M1.59: nightly suite and wall-clock; private receipts only via the `auto-crop-golden` workflow (A-8), pooled aggregates (n >= 30), contact sheets stay private. Accept: leak test: no names or paths, 6-image stratum suppressed.
- [ ] **M7.78 Release v0.6.0** - release-plz tag; bundler builds the NSIS installer and portable zip (unsigned unless SignPath approved, B15) with `SHA256SUMS` and attestations; notes list measured and PROVISIONAL numbers, limitations. Accept: `gh attestation verify` passes; size delta recorded.
- [ ] **M7.79 Clean-VM smoke test** - on clean Windows 10 22H2 and Windows 11 VMs: install, open a receipt photo, accept the chip, compare, revert, switch to B&W, export 1-bit TIFF G4 and PNG, Restore original, uninstall. Accept: all steps pass on both with no manual runtime installs.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** OCR - Tesseract 5.5.x pinned, thresholding fixed (Otsu, Sauvola arms), paired, bootstrap 95% CI: median delta CER <= 0, >= 20% relative cut on the low-contrast slice (thermal-fade plus fade ladder), <= 2% of images worse by > 1 pt; n < 80 slices advisory, n < 30 unpublished.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Amounts - zero lost amount tokens on the synthetic fade and price-column suite in every mode (a failing mode is blocked as a default); private slices: one-sided 95% bound of the mean paired difference no worse than -0.5 pt, lost-token receipts listed, n < 30 advisory.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** DIBCO and sweep - DIBCO/H-DIBCO F-measure within 1 pt of the tuned Sauvola baseline (PROVISIONAL); the window sweep (31-201 px at 300 dpi plus the ~0.33 x dpi arm) is recorded and the shipped defaults are chosen from it.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Speed and memory - slider tick <= 16 ms compute on the 2 MP display proxy (<= ~50 ms round trip); `enhance` 120 ms Auto/Grayscale, up to 250 ms B&W/Faded (total <= 830 ms); no Sauvola overflow at 12, 48, 100 MP; peak RSS within the per-image budget (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Artefacts - the >= 20-case visual regression suite (barcodes, reverse-video headers, hard shadow edges, coloured paper, stamps) shows no ghost patches or seams beyond the documented thresholds.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** 1-bit output - 1-bit PNG and TIFF G4 exports open correctly in >= 3 independent readers with dpi and polarity preserved (pixel-exact vs the bilevel raster).
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Suggested, never forced - the chip previews live in one tap and reverts in one tap (E2E); batch default Suggest, CLI default off, risk-flagged items held; usability task T6 passes with no unrecovered failures.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Defaults - a test asserts default mode Original, B&W output anti-aliased 8-bit in the source format, no bit depth in `EditState` (1-bit only via `OutputSpec`), despeckle off for receipts.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Data safety and licences - enhanced in-place save, and a second save after a crop-only save, are recoverable via Restore original after restart (M7.67, M7.80); `cargo deny check` green; no oracle tool (Tesseract, OpenCV, Leptonica) in the shipped bundle.
- [ ] **GATE (G3):** Release - `v0.6.0` artefacts built with checksums and attestations; the clean-VM smoke test passes on Windows 10 and Windows 11.

## M8 - macOS preview

<!-- ms: version=v0.7.0 | platforms=macOS 12+ arm64 (Apple silicon) | size=L -->

**Goal:** Ship the full app on macOS for Apple silicon: an ad-hoc-signed, unnotarised DMG and own Homebrew tap, bundled libheif and libde265 dylibs with an optional ImageIO HEIC path, a `sandbox_init` decode worker, trackpad and macOS conventions, and APFS-safe overwrite. Every earlier accuracy, fuzz and fault-injection gate is re-run on macOS.
**Release:** `v0.7.0`, macOS 12+ arm64. Apple silicon (macOS arm64) is first-class; ARM64 on Windows and Linux and Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0 (no Intel binary unless M8.46 decides otherwise). Artifacts: `Auto-Crop_0.7.0_aarch64.dmg` (ad-hoc signed, not notarised), `.dSYM.zip`, `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, cask in tap `WelFedTed/homebrew-auto-crop`; updater `.app.tar.gz` and `.sig` only if M8.11 keeps install-in-place. Windows x64 continues as v0.6.0, Linux stays CI-only until M9; no Apple fee (B15).
**Depends on:** M6 (HeicBackend trait and contract tests, worker pool, `Shm` trait, HEIC fixtures and dE harness), M7 (enhancement gates and kernels); transitively M0-M5 (commit protocol, Backups, `InferenceBackend`); M0.47 (Apple-silicon access decision).
**Size:** L (5-8 part-time weeks, definition in X.40; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G6 (macOS preview, all lines below): M2-M7 accuracy gates re-run on macOS with mean IoU within 0.2 pt of Windows, Apple-silicon Tier-M within budget x1.25 (12 MP auto-process p50 <= 700 ms target on an M1-class Mac), int8 within 0.3 pt of fp32, HEIC dE2000 mean <= 0.5 on both engines, a sandbox that survives injected crashes and bombs, T1-T8 on a trackpad, and an ad-hoc DMG plus own tap that install at $0. Numbers PROVISIONAL unless measured; hardware gates need a real Mac (A-9).
**Risks / spikes:**
- Gatekeeper friction: unsigned builds need Open Anyway (Sequoia removed Control-click); mitigations are the in-DMG hint (M8.06), screenshot docs (M8.73), the tap (M8.09; verify and document its quarantine handling) and honest limits (M8.75).
- Hardware at $0: baselines, trackpad, VoiceOver and the private HEIC corpus need a real Apple silicon Mac (`macos-latest` is no baseline); without one the gates stay open and need an owner-recorded delta (M8.72, A-9).
- Spike first: `sandbox_init` (deprecated) and ImageIO under it (M8.34), per-build ad-hoc identity vs file-access grants and updates (M8.11, M8.36), ORT arm64 `minos` and no Intel binary (M8.43, M8.46); UI checks are manual (no WKWebView automation).

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M8.01 macOS required CI leg** - extend the M0.04/M3.70 `macos-latest` (arm64) jobs from build-only to the full workspace: `MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET=12.0` for rustc, CMake and `cc`, `cargo nextest`, rust-cache keyed on the `native-deps.toml` hash; required check, green on a PR.
- [ ] **M8.02 Nightly OS matrix** - public M6.60 fixtures, M6.61 corpus and `doctor --self-test` on `macos-14`/`15`/`latest` (`max-parallel: 3`, PR `cancel-in-progress`); peak concurrency <= 5 (`docs/ci.md`), 3 green runs, failures open an issue; private HEIC corpus never runs on hosted runners.
- [ ] **M8.03 Intel best-effort job** - non-required nightly (M0.05 smoke promoted) on `macos-26-intel` (fallback `macos-15-intel`) building `core` and `cli` for `x86_64-apple-darwin`, `continue-on-error: true`; 2027-06 runner-retirement recheck in `docs/ci.md`; never blocks a merge or release.
- [ ] **M8.04 macOS bundle config and icon** - `tauri.macos.conf.json`: identifier `io.github.welfedted.AutoCrop` (`check-identity`), `minimumSystemVersion` 12.0, ad-hoc `-`, `frameworks`, `.icns`; the `aarch64-apple-darwin` build yields `Auto-Crop_<ver>_aarch64.dmg`, crisp icon in both themes.
- [ ] **M8.05 Ad-hoc signing step** - `xtask sign-adhoc` signs inner to outer (dylibs, worker, executables, `.app`) with `codesign --force --sign - --timestamp=none` after `install_name_tool` edits; `codesign --verify --deep --strict` passes in the mounted DMG and `codesign -dvv` shows `adhoc`.
- [ ] **M8.06 DMG build and in-DMG hint** - DMG with the app, an `Applications` link and a visible `READ ME - if macOS blocks the app.txt` with the Open Anyway steps (styled background if it works in CI, else plain `hdiutil create`); hint shows on macOS 15 and 26, CI step < 10 min (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.07 Installed-artifact smoke test** - CI mounts the DMG, copies the app to `/Applications`, sets then clears a synthetic quarantine flag, runs `auto-crop doctor --self-test` (M6.74: model hashes, sandboxed worker, 64x64 HEIC via libheif): exit 0; `spctl --assess` logged, not failed.
- [ ] **M8.08 macOS release leg** - macOS arm64 published in `release-platforms.toml`; uncached `release.yml` bundles libde265 (B12; `no-hevc`-only needs an owner decision, A-5), ships DMG, `.dSYM.zip`, `SHA256SUMS`; `shasum -c` and `gh attestation verify` pass, `.ips` symbolicates via `atos`.
- [ ] **M8.09 Homebrew tap and cask** - repo `WelFedTed/homebrew-auto-crop`, `Casks/auto-crop.rb` (arm64, macOS >= 12, Open Anyway caveat, never strips quarantine); `zap` = caches, preferences, logs only, never backups; `brew install --cask welfedted/auto-crop/auto-crop` works on clean macOS 26.
- [ ] **M8.10 Tap version-bump automation** - release sends `repository_dispatch` to the tap (App token); the tap bumps `version`/`sha256` from `SHA256SUMS`, runs `brew style`, `brew audit --cask --strict --online` and install/uninstall tests; a `v0.7.0-rc.1` dry run needs no hash edit.
- [ ] **M8.11 macOS updater spike and ADR** - updater on an ad-hoc bundle: (a) no Gatekeeper prompt, (b) TCC grants survive, (c) works translocated and without `/Applications` write access, (d) Homebrew copies; user-confirmed install only if all hold, else notify-only (M5.49); ADR in `docs/adr/`.
- [ ] **M8.12 Updater configuration per ADR** - per M8.11: a `darwin-aarch64` `latest.json` entry with a user-confirmed dialog (never silent, B18) or a notify-only banner; manual and opt-in weekly checks (M5.49, M5.50) work in the DMG on staging; idle app makes no request (M8.39).
- [ ] **M8.78 Publish v0.7.0** - `v0.7.0-rc.N` dry run; tag only with every GATE below ticked or the owner's recorded delta (M13.42, A-9); draft, publish, verify (`shasum -c`, `gh attestation verify`), then clean-Mac DMG and `brew install --cask` smoke on macOS 15/26; README table matches.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M8.13 Arm64 native build** - promote the M6.71 recipe: `xtask build-native --target aarch64-apple-darwin` builds libheif >= 1.23.5 (shared, plugins) with libde265 and dav1d, every encoder OFF, sources pinned in `native-deps.toml`; clean checkout builds, `otool -L` shows no Homebrew path.
- [ ] **M8.14 Bundle layout and plugins** - executables in `Contents/MacOS`, dylibs and libde265 plugin dir in `Contents/Frameworks` (`@rpath`); `auto-crop-worker` loads the plugin from there before sandboxing, ignoring `LIBHEIF_PLUGIN_PATH`/`DYLD_*`; copied app converts HEIC (M0.41 fallback).
- [ ] **M8.15 Mach-O audit in check-native** - `check-native` (M6.72) audits the `.app`: all Mach-O arm64, `minos <= 12.0`, deps only `@rpath`/`@loader_path`/`/usr/lib`/`/System`, no `x265_`/`x264_` symbols, libjpeg-turbo >= 3.1.4, LGPL/GPL texts and ONNX Runtime present; 2 negative fixtures fail.
- [ ] **M8.16 Apple silicon HEIC parity** - M6.60 `sips` fixtures run on public CI, the private M6.58 corpus only on the maintainer's Mac, through arm64 libheif and libde265; RGB equals Windows x64 or is within 1/255 per channel (PROVISIONAL); orientation, notices and metadata blobs identical.
- [ ] **M8.17 ImageIoBackend probe and limits** - `ImageIoBackend` (`objc2-image-io` 0.3.2): `CGImageSourceCreateWithData` on parent bytes (no paths), probe of size, orientation, depth and colour, `max_pixels`/item caps before decode; M6.61 bombs fail in < 1 s, 64 MB; M6.19 contract tests pass.
- [ ] **M8.18 ImageIoBackend decode and colour** - decode to shared-memory `CGBitmapContext` in the source colour space (no ColorSync), ICC via `CGColorSpaceCopyICCData` (`moxcms` converts, M6.29), T0 thumbnail (M6.22); P3 decodes with ICC, chart dE2000 mean <= 0.5 vs libheif (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.19 ImageIO normalisation and notices** - match libheif: upright pixels, Orientation reset (never twice), straight alpha, 8-bit only (10-bit and PQ/HLG use libheif via `HeicCaps { hdr: false }`), gain map, depth and Live Photo noticed per M6.46; fixtures match on macOS 14, 15 and 26.
- [ ] **M8.20 ImageIO metadata blobs** - Exif, XMP and ICC leave as bytes so the `exif_patch` policy (M6.26) is unchanged: reuse the libheif ISOBMFF item reader (add `iinf`/`iloc` if M6.18 lacks it); blobs byte-identical to libheif on the M6.60 fixtures, GPS-strip test (M6.27) passes on both.
- [ ] **M8.21 HEIC engine selection on macOS** - `heic.engine = bundled | system | auto` (`system` = ImageIO, `auto` = ImageIO then bundled) in `settings.toml` and `--heic-engine`; bundled stays default, `auto` only while M8.22 is green; About and `doctor` show backend and sandbox level.
- [ ] **M8.22 HEIC gate on macOS** - `xtask heic-eval`, both engines, independent references; public CI: M6.60 fixtures and charts only, private M6.58 corpus only on the maintainer's Mac; dE2000 mean <= 0.5, p99 <= 1.5, max <= 3 (PROVISIONAL), Exif/ICC/orientation/notices 100%, seams <= 2/255.
- [ ] **M8.23 clonefile backups on APFS** - Extends M2.34: reflink uses `clonefile(2)` on APFS (`reflink-copy`), same-volume only; `ENOTSUP` (HFS+, exFAT) and `EXDEV` fall back to a re-hashed copy (method recorded); 20 MB backup < 50 ms, < 1 MB extra (PROVISIONAL); Restore works from a clone.
- [ ] **M8.24 Metadata preservation on overwrite** - Extends M2.30: `copyfile(3)` `COPYFILE_METADATA` carries xattrs (tags, label, where-from, quarantine), ACLs, mode, mtime, birth time (`setattrlist`); skip `com.apple.provenance`, warn on `EPERM`; fixture identical after overwrite and Restore.
- [ ] **M8.25 Resource-fork behaviour** - fixtures with `com.apple.ResourceFork` and a custom icon through overwrite, HEIC to JPG conversion and Restore; preserved on overwrite, conversion copies Finder-visible metadata and reports drops; tests assert it, limitation in M8.75.
- [ ] **M8.26 APFS and HFS+ name collisions** - M5.15 NFC plus case-fold key tested on case-insensitive and case-sensitive APFS, journaled HFS+ (NFD) and exFAT images (`hdiutil create -fs`); U+00E9, `e` + U+0301 and case variants keep every file, never overwrite silently, suffix deterministically.
- [ ] **M8.27 macOS folder-scan rules** - skip `._*` AppleDouble, `.DS_Store`, `.Spotlight-V100`, `.Trashes`, `.fseventsd`, `__MACOSX`; never follow Finder aliases; M5.22 `FileAttrs` spots `SF_DATALESS` placeholders so scans and thumbnails never hydrate them (offer download or skip).
- [ ] **M8.28 Durability on macOS** - `File::sync_all` issues `F_FULLFSYNC` on APFS (confirm), parent-directory fsync after rename, fallback to `fsync` on `ENOTSUP`/`ENOTTY` (SMB, exFAT); fail-point tests green on APFS and exFAT images, `commit` cost recorded vs Table A's 100 ms (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.29 Store locations and indexing** - store in `~/Library/Application Support/AutoCrop` (mode 0700); proxies and tiles in `~/Library/Caches/AutoCrop` (Time Machine-excluded); backups dir `.noindex`-named so Spotlight skips it; `mdfind` finds no originals, backups stay in Time Machine.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M8.30 Worker sandbox profile** - `auto-crop-worker` applies a deny-default `sandbox_init` profile (no network, exec or files) after libraries and the libde265 plugin load, with `RLIMIT_CORE=0`; M6.42 `Shm` via `shm_open` + `shm_unlink`; 100 respawns leak < 20 MB parent RSS (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.31 Memory and time watchdog** - parent samples the worker's `phys_footprint` every 50 ms and kills it at the cap (`RLIMIT_AS` is unreliable), plus the `10 s + 1 s/MP` time cap (PROVISIONAL); output sized from the probe; an unbounded allocator dies under cap + 25% with `MemoryLimit`.
- [ ] **M8.32 Sandbox level reporting and conformance** - `doctor` and About show the level; M6.10 probes plus home read, mach-lookup are denied on macOS 14/15/26; if `sandbox_init` fails the worker runs `process-only`, `--require-sandbox` exits 6 and a setting can refuse HEIC.
- [ ] **M8.33 Fault-injection and bomb suites** - M6.10 suite and M6.61 bombs on macOS: each fails only its item (`DecoderCrashed`, `DecodeTimeout`, `MemoryLimit`), worker respawns, sources stay byte-identical, no crash dialog, `.ips` reports hold no pixel data, 100 images with 5 faults finish 95.
- [ ] **M8.34 ImageIO under the sandbox** - spike the `mach-lookup`, `iokit-open` and file-read rules ImageIO's HEIC path needs (hardware HEVC): full profile, minimal allow-list, or ImageIO disabled; it runs only with the M8.32 probes still denied, never silently unsandboxed.
- [ ] **M8.35 Entitlements and hardened-runtime ADR** - no App Sandbox for the main app (B15; overwrite needs user-selected scope), no hardened runtime, minimal entitlements, Info.plist usage strings for Documents, Desktop, Downloads, volumes; `codesign -d --entitlements -` matches.
- [ ] **M8.36 File-access (TCC) matrix** - overwrite via drop, Open With, open panel and Quick Action in protected folders, iCloud Drive, external APFS and SMB volumes (prompts, post-update behaviour); `EPERM` maps to localised `PermissionDenied` plus Save as copy; `docs/macos-permissions.md`.
- [ ] **M8.37 Translocation and quarantine** - detect `/AppTranslocation/` and show "Move Auto Crop to Applications" (updates refused there); never strip quarantine on user files (M8.24) or the app; from the mounted DMG and Downloads on macOS 15 and 26 the prompt appears and the app works.
- [ ] **M8.38 macOS fuzz targets** - cargo-fuzz `sniff`, `heif_probe`, `worker_protocol`, `decode_pipeline` on `aarch64-apple-darwin` (nightly, ASan) plus new `imageio_glue`; 60 s smoke on PRs touching `codecs/heic`, 1 h per target nightly, 0 crashes or hangs, crashers replayed as tests (M6.63).
- [ ] **M8.39 Network audit on macOS** - Extends M5.52: a scripted session (open, process, save, quit) with default settings makes no non-loopback connection, audited by process tree (WebKit's networking helper owns webview sockets); opt-in weekly check makes exactly one github.com request.
- [ ] **M8.40 HEVC note and no-hevc build** - M6.04 patent note on the macOS install page and in the DMG hint; a `no-hevc` DMG (owner-decided fallback only, A-5) defaults to `heic.engine = system`, builds, passes `doctor --self-test` with `system` and gives `HevcDecoderMissing` under `bundled`.
- [ ] **M8.41 LGPL relink drill on macOS** - `xtask relink-test` (M6.12) on arm64: rebuild libheif from `native-sources` with the relink patch, swap the dylib into a copy of the `.app`, re-sign, launch, decode the fixture; the macOS steps in `docs/replacing-libheif.md` (M6.79) work on a clean Mac.
- [ ] **M8.42 Apple Developer Program record** - `docs/adr/NNNN-apple-developer-program.md`: "not pursued (B15: never pay)", owner-reopenable; lists benefits (no Open Anyway, stable identity, `homebrew/cask`) and the $99 yearly cost; linked from the install page; CI holds no Apple credentials.

### Core / ML runtime
- [ ] **M8.43 ONNX Runtime arm64 in the bundle** - ORT 1.28 via `ort` 2.0.0-rc.13 (`load-dynamic` from `Contents/Frameworks`, or static, per M0.35); check `minos` (prebuilt may exceed 12.0): if so build at 12.0 or raise the floor as a user decision (C1); M4.59 passes (corners within 0.1 px).
- [ ] **M8.44 Int8 versus fp32 on Apple silicon** - rerun M4 corner and orientation suites on the arm64 CPU provider, fp32 vs int8: mean IoU within 0.3 pt overall and per slice n >= 30, p95 corner-error rise <= 0.05% of the diagonal, orientation top-1 delta <= 0.3 pt (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.45 CoreML benchmark (opt-in only)** - CPU vs ONNX Runtime CoreML on the corner and orientation nets (compile time, fp16/ANE drift): opt-in `Advanced > Inference` switch only if p50 >= 2x faster over 100 images and IoU delta <= 0.1 pt (PROVISIONAL), else record "not worthwhile" (M4.58).
- [ ] **M8.46 Intel Mac ADR** - from the M0.35 ADR decide for `x86_64-apple-darwin`: rten with converted models, source-built ONNX Runtime, `ort` `load-dynamic` with a user dylib, or no binary (ort ships none for macOS x86_64); ADR in `docs/adr/` with build cost and CI-minute estimate.
- [ ] **M8.47 Intel plan implementation** - implement M8.46: an rten, source-built or load-dynamic build of `core` and `cli` for `x86_64-apple-darwin` in the M8.03 job with ML on; M4.59 and the M4 suite run once (mean IoU within 0.5 pt of arm64, PROVISIONAL); docs state what ships.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M8.48 WebKit gesture layer** - WebKit `gesture*` events feed `scale`, `rotation` and the anchor into the M3 `ViewTransform` reducer; rotation engages after 8 degrees (PROVISIONAL); recorded-sequence tests pass and a real trackpad moves a 24 MP proxy about the anchor without jumps.
- [ ] **M8.49 Native zoom and swipe suppression** - `WKWebView.allowsMagnification` is false, Cmd +/-/0 do not zoom, `overscroll-behavior: none` stops rubber-banding, swipe navigation and smart zoom are off; a macOS 14/26 matrix shows only our transform changing (leaks fixed in `with_webview`).
- [ ] **M8.50 Trackpad gesture map** - scroll pans, pinch zooms, twist rotates, double-click toggles fit/100%, swipe steps through Review, Cmd-click multi-selects, two-finger click opens the context menu; no three-finger or edge gestures; every row run on MacBook and Magic Trackpad (M3.60 format).
- [ ] **M8.51 60 fps check on Apple silicon** - M0.56 workload (24 MP proxy pinch, pan, rotate) on a built-in (up to 120 Hz) and an external 60 Hz display, replacing any UNMEASURED M0.59 cell; frame median <= 17 ms, p95 <= 20 ms (PROVISIONAL) per display, a miss files a perf issue.
- [ ] **M8.52 Native menu bar** - Tauri menu: App (About, Settings Cmd+comma, Hide, Quit), File (Open Files Cmd+O, Open Folder Cmd+Shift+O, Save all Cmd+S, Export Cmd+E, Close Cmd+W), Edit, View, Window, Help; `mod` maps Ctrl to Cmd, no clash with macOS shortcuts (audit committed).
- [ ] **M8.53 Named Undo and Redo in the Edit menu** - custom Undo/Redo on `CmdOrCtrl+Z`/`CmdOrCtrl+Shift+Z` (not responder items) titled from History labels via i18n (M3.36, M5.36), e.g. "Undo Move Corner"; text fields undo text; UI test (mocked IPC) and a manual pass show the title change.
- [ ] **M8.54 Open With, Dock drop and cold start** - handle `RunEvent::Opened { urls }` (cold launch, warm open, Dock drops) merged by the M5.25 300 ms window into one run; 12 files dropped on the Dock (app closed, running, full-screen) make one session, folders recurse; verified on macOS 12-26.
- [ ] **M8.55 File associations** - `CFBundleDocumentTypes` for the shared extension list (M3.19) plus `public.folder` for Dock drops, with `LSHandlerRank = Alternate`, never default; Finder Open With lists Auto Crop, Preview and Photos stay default, `lsregister -dump` shows no Default claim.
- [ ] **M8.56 Finder Quick Action** - Automator workflow `Crop and straighten with Auto Crop.workflow` (shell `open -b io.github.welfedted.AutoCrop "$@"`), Settings buttons add/remove it in `~/Library/Services`; no Finder Sync (B15); 20 selected images open one session on macOS 14/15/26.
- [ ] **M8.57 Dialogs, drag-drop, Show in Finder** - Rust-run pickers (NSOpenPanel, NSSavePanel for Export), Finder drag-drop of files, folders, symlinks, aliases, iCloud placeholders, M5.44 "Show in folder" in Finder; matrix passes, the webview gets only opaque IDs, never paths (PLAN 8.6.4).
- [ ] **M8.58 Dark mode** - `prefers-color-scheme` follows System, Light, Dark live; title bar/dialogs follow, canvas neutral grey, `prefers-contrast` and `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured; Grid, Review and Adjust screenshots in both themes on macOS 15 and 26, axe-core (Playwright WebKit) clean.
- [ ] **M8.59 HiDPI and full screen** - canvas and 512 px tiles follow `devicePixelRatio`, re-rendering when a window moves between Retina and non-Retina screens; full screen keeps Esc as cancel and clears the notch, Cmd+W closes only the window, Cmd+Q confirms in a batch; macOS 15/26 checklist.
- [ ] **M8.60 Target size and density** - trackpad and mouse get `data-input=mouse` density (32 px controls), handle hit areas stay 44-48 px, "Always touch" works; Playwright WebKit asserts sizes and a trackpad handle-grab test starts >= 90% of drags on the right handle (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.61 VoiceOver smoke pass** - script (`docs/accessibility/voiceover-smoke.md`) on macOS 15 and 26: folder, Adjust handles and number fields, `aria-valuetext`, live regions, Undo names, Save all, Tab reachability (shortcut if Tab skips); runs unaided (T10 smoke); full audit in M13.

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M8.62 Public accuracy suite parity** - M2-M7 public/synthetic accuracy suites on `macos-latest` (IoU, corners, skew, orientation, CER, DIBCO): mean IoU within 0.2 pt of Windows, per-slice failures within one image (PROVISIONAL); each delta root-caused (NEON, FMA, provider) in metrics JSON.
- [ ] **M8.63 Private golden set on real Apple silicon** - `auto-crop-eval` on the private golden set, only on the maintainer's Mac (encrypted disk, never CI), publishing aggregates only (B21); mean IoU within 0.2 pt of Windows, silent failures not above Windows, slices n >= 30 only.
- [ ] **M8.64 Enhancement kernels and tolerances** - rerun M7 kernel oracles on arm64 (u8 Lanczos warp PSNR >= 45 dB, Sauvola, overflow guards above 16.8 MP, NEON vs scalar) and the Tesseract CER and DIBCO gates; snapshot SSIM >= 0.98 across platforms (M7.62); M7 gates keep their thresholds.
- [ ] **M8.65 Apple silicon performance baseline** - Table A spans on a real M1-class Mac at 12/48/100 MP JPEG and 12/48 MP HEIC (closes M7.65), results in `docs/perf/macos-arm64.md`; 12 MP auto-process p50 <= 700 ms for T1 exits, cold start <= 1.0 s, peak RSS <= 3x RGB8 + 64 MB (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.66 HEIC decode benchmarks** - Extends M6.66: arm64 libheif/libde265 vs ImageIO on 12/48 MP HEIC (decode, draft latency, JPG throughput); HEIC to JPG >= 2.5 images/s (PROVISIONAL); if ImageIO is >= 2x faster with M8.22 green, recommend `auto` to the owner, default stays `bundled`.
- [ ] **M8.67 Tier-M budget table** - Apple silicon Tier-M column in PLAN 7.1 Tables A and B with a reason per changed budget (unmeasured PROVISIONAL); `docs/perf/README.md`: no gungraun on Apple silicon (Linux instruction gate plus advisory `hyperfine` A/B nightly), no macOS wall-clock gate on PRs.
- [ ] **M8.68 Batch behaviour on Apple silicon** - pool from performance cores plus a few efficiency cores, thread QoS, App Nap assertion; occluded 300-image batch within 10% of foreground, UI p95 <= 20 ms, `analyse` <= 40 ms p95 (T1 exits), M5.61/M6.65 batches finish on 8 GB (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.69 Re-run UNMEASURED M0 cells** - repeat M0.65 tile serving, M0.66 IPC latency and M0.68 folder-drop fixtures on the packaged app for every cell left UNMEASURED; each becomes PASS or FAIL with numbers (grid scroll >= 30 fps, no UI stall > 250 ms; PROVISIONAL), ADRs updated.
- [ ] **M8.70 Full-matrix re-run and delta report** - run data-safety (M5.56-M5.58), fault-injection, hostile, fuzz and accuracy suites on macOS (private golden set: maintainer's Mac only); `docs/quality/macos-deltas.md` lists every Windows difference with cause and owner; 0 crashes or hangs.
- [ ] **M8.71 macOS UX test round** - moderated MacBook-trackpad round on T1-T8 of PLAN 6.14 (T9/T10 gate 1.0), 5-8 participants (PROVISIONAL), a VoiceOver user if recruitable; T1-T8 criteria met or each miss goes to the owner as an approved delta; results in `docs/ux/rounds/`.
- [ ] **M8.72 Hardware and testers** - recruit >= 2 volunteer testers, borrow a real Apple silicon Mac (B15), check launch-and-convert on macOS 12-14, coverage in `docs/testing/macos-hardware.md`; no real Mac: hardware gates stay open, publishing past them needs the owner's delta (A-9, M13.42).

### Docs
- [ ] **M8.73 macOS install page** - `docs/install/macos.md`: checksum/attestation verify, Open Anyway screenshots (Control-click on 12-14, Privacy & Security on 15+), `xattr` fallback, translocation, HEVC note, uninstall (backups stay); a new tester installs unaided on clean macOS 15 and 26.
- [ ] **M8.74 Finder integration guide** - `docs/finder-integration.md`: Open With, Dock drop, Quick Action install and removal, why there is no Finder Sync extension, iCloud placeholder behaviour; steps verified on a clean account and linked from Settings.
- [ ] **M8.75 macOS known limitations** - ad-hoc signing friction, file-access prompts that may return after updates, Intel status, macOS 12 coverage (M8.72), no WKWebView UI automation, ImageIO opt-in, resource forks, Live Photo video and gain maps dropped; linked from install page and notes.
- [ ] **M8.76 Build from source on macOS** - `docs/BUILDING-macos.md` and the `xtask doctor` macOS checks (Xcode CLT, CMake, Ninja, Node; NASM only for Intel) with Homebrew hints; a fresh Apple silicon machine builds and launches the app from the document alone in < 45 min (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M8.77 Results page, README row, release notes** - results page (M8.70 deltas, baselines), README install row (DMG, tap, "ad-hoc signed, not notarised", Intel status), ROADMAP counts in the same PR; v0.7.0 notes state caveats and which hardware gates ran on real hardware; lychee passes.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Accuracy - M2-M7 accuracy gates re-run on `macos-latest` and the maintainer's real Mac: mean IoU within 0.2 pt of Windows, per-slice failures within one image, kernel and enhancement gates at the same thresholds, every delta explained (M8.62, M8.63, M8.64, M8.70).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Int8 - on Apple silicon int8 vs fp32 mean IoU delta <= 0.3 pt overall and per slice n >= 30, p95 corner-error rise <= 0.05% of the diagonal (PROVISIONAL), CoreML decision recorded (M8.44, M8.45).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Performance - Apple silicon baselines recorded (12, 48, 100 MP JPEG; 12, 48 MP HEIC); on an M1-class Mac 12 MP auto-process p50 <= 700 ms for T1 exits (G6 bound: within x1.25) and cold start <= 1.0 s (PROVISIONAL); Tier-M budget table updated (M8.65, M8.67).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Install - the ad-hoc DMG installs and runs on macOS 15 and 26 from `docs/install/macos.md` alone, `codesign --verify --deep --strict` passes, the own tap installs it, no Apple fee and no Apple credential in CI (B15) (M8.05, M8.09, M8.42, M8.73).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** HEIC - corpus re-run on macOS via bundled libheif and ImageIO: dE2000 mean <= 0.5, p99 <= 1.5, max <= 3 (PROVISIONAL), Exif/ICC/orientation/notices 100%, blobs byte-identical across backends; private corpus only on the maintainer's Mac (M8.16, M8.20, M8.22).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Sandbox - the macOS worker survives injected crashes, hangs, unbounded allocation and bombs: 0 host crashes, item-only failures, byte-identical sources; blocked actions denied at the level `doctor` reports; macOS fuzzing 0 crashes or hangs (M8.32, M8.33, M8.38).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Native and LGPL - libheif >= 1.23.5 with libde265 as a separate dylib, `check-native` Mach-O audit passes (arm64, `minos <= 12.0`, no x265/x264, allowed libraries only), macOS relink drill passes (M8.15, M8.41).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Interaction - trackpad pinch and rotate hold frame median <= 17 ms, p95 <= 20 ms on the 24 MP proxy (PROVISIONAL), VoiceOver smoke passes, Open With, Dock drop and Quick Action open one session, Edit menu names Undo/Redo, dark mode, HiDPI, full screen verified (M8.48-M8.61).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** UX - T1-T8 (PLAN 6.14) pass on macOS via trackpad, or each miss is an owner-approved recorded delta; T9 and T10 remain 1.0 gates (M8.71).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** APFS - clonefile backups on the same volume; xattrs, Finder tags, ACLs and timestamps identical before and after overwrite and Restore original; resource-fork policy tested; NFC/NFD/case-fold collision tests pass on APFS, HFS+ and exFAT images (M8.23-M8.26).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Decisions - the Intel Mac decision (M8.46, implemented or documented as M8.47) and the updater decision (M8.11, shipped as M8.12) are recorded as ADRs and docs and CI match them.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Regression - the full suite matrix is green on macOS, Windows and Linux CI, nothing from earlier gates regressed, and every difference from Windows is explained (M8.70).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Release - v0.7.0 is tagged only when every gate above is ticked or the owner has recorded an approved delta; a missed gate is never relaxed silently (A-4, A-9); notes state which hardware gates ran on real hardware (M8.72, M8.77, M8.78).

## M9 - Linux preview

<!-- ms: version=v0.8.0 | platforms=Linux x86_64 (Ubuntu 22.04+, Fedora; arm64 best-effort) | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Bring Auto Crop to Linux with the WebKitGTK workarounds proven on a recorded distro, session and GPU matrix, a Landlock and seccomp sandbox around the decode worker, and free packaging channels (AppImage, deb, rpm, own Flatpak remote). Mouse, trackpad and keyboard are guaranteed; Linux touch is best-effort (B8), recorded and documented rather than promised.
**Release:** `v0.8.0` (via `v0.8.0-rc.N` if needed); Linux x86_64 on Ubuntu 22.04+ (24.04, 26.04 (verify)) and current Fedora; ARM64 on Linux is best-effort until 1.0 (C1). Artifacts: AppImage, `.deb`, `.rpm`, an own Flatpak remote under `/flatpak/` on the single Pages site plus a per-release `.flatpak` bundle, `SHA256SUMS`, attestations and an SBOM; no apt or dnf repositories and no Flathub before 1.0 (B17).
**Depends on:** M6 (worker pool, `Shm` trait, libheif and libde265 recipe M6.70-M6.73) and M7 (enhancement gates), and through them M0-M5; M0.70 (B8 verdict, UNMEASURED cells) feeds M9.28 and M9.78. M8 (macOS) is not required.
**Size:** XL (9-16 part-time weeks, definition in X.40; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G6 (Linux preview, all lines below): every required matrix row starts and renders with the shims and GPU rows hold the pointer gate (median frame <= 22 ms, p95 <= 33 ms at 24 MP, x86_64; PROVISIONAL); M2-M7 accuracy gates re-run on Linux; packages install on clean VMs with the updater split; a `landlock+seccomp` sandbox with a visible degradation notice; shell integration, dialogs and folder drop on Wayland and X11. Linux touch is recorded as best-effort and never blocks the release (B8).
**Risks / spikes:**
- WebKitGTK behaviour varies by distro, GPU driver and compositor and touch is unmeasured; mitigations are the recorded matrix (M9.62), shims plus a toggle (M9.25), community reports (M9.71) and supported/unsupported/unverified labels (M9.74).
- One maintainer cannot test every GPU, compositor and distro, and Flathub's AI policy flipped three times in 2026; the own remote is the 1.0 channel, the manifest is only linted (M9.16) and Tauri 3 or CEF stays a watch item (M9.28, X.19).
- Time-boxed spikes (1-2 days, negative outcomes recorded): M9.04, M9.05, M9.13, M9.18, M9.26, M9.27, M9.56; deferred on purpose: apt and dnf repositories, Snap, Flathub submission.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M9.01 Linux x86_64 CI on the glibc floor** - required `ubuntu-22.04` job builds core, engine, cli, worker and shell and runs nextest; `xtask check-elf` fails if any shipped ELF needs GLIBC > 2.35 or a newer libstdc++ than 22.04; cold build time recorded (PROVISIONAL budget 25 min).
- [ ] **M9.02 Linux arm64 CI (best-effort)** - `linux-arm64` job on `ubuntu-22.04-arm` (same glibc floor) builds and tests the same crates, nextest green; a red arm64 job blocks only arm64 artifacts, never the x86_64 release (C1).
- [ ] **M9.03 Fedora container job** - `fedora:latest` job builds the workspace, runs nextest and the CLI accuracy smoke, installs the rpm and converts a tiny checked-in HEIC to JPG; an Ubuntu 26.04 (verify) job does the same with the deb; previous Fedora nightly only (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M9.04 ONNX Runtime linking on the floor image** - SPIKE (1 day): check `ort` 2.0.0-rc.13 static libs link and run on `ubuntu-22.04` x86_64 and aarch64, else newer GCC with `-static-libstdc++` or `load-dynamic`; `check-elf` green with both models on both arches, ADR in `docs/adr/`.
- [ ] **M9.05 Headless GUI smoke in CI** - extend `--smoke-test <dir>` (M3.75) with a 10 s fps probe on a synthetic 24 MP proxy, JSON report, under `xvfb-run` and headless `weston` (PROVISIONAL); `tauri-driver` E2E only if its API fits WebKitGTK 4.1; asserts `visualViewport.scale === 1`.
- [ ] **M9.06 Linux fuzz and instruction-count gates** - `fuzz.yml` on both Linux arches: 60 s PR smoke, 1 h per target nightly (counts toward >= 72 h before 1.0) plus `%F`/`%U`/`file://`, Thunar `uca.xml` and Nautilus-script hostile-name targets; per-arch gungraun within 5%; 0 crashes.
- [ ] **M9.07 Loopback filesystem matrix job** - `fs-matrix` job mounts ext4, btrfs, xfs, vfat, exfat, tmpfs and NFS loopback volumes (privileged-container fallback) and runs the safe-write, backup and collision tests per volume and cross-volume pair; backup method asserted, skips listed.
- [ ] **M9.08 Scheduled recheck workflow** - weekly `webkitgtk-watch.yml` compares WebKitGTK versions (Ubuntu, Fedora, GNOME runtime) with `docs/linux/webkitgtk-versions.json`; on change it opens an issue and runs the Linux smoke; WebKitGTK only (natives: M0.10); a faked old version triggers both.
- [ ] **M9.09 Release workflow Linux legs** - `release.yml` legs run `cargo tauri build --bundles appimage,deb,rpm` (arm64 AppImage best-effort), attach `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, SBOM and native sources; verify job runs `sha256sum -c` and `gh attestation verify`; `v0.8.0-rc.0` dry run passes.
- [ ] **M9.10 Publish the Linux channel** - add Linux to `release-platforms.toml`, cut `v0.8.0-rc.1`, fix findings, tag `v0.8.0` with known issues; the release stays a draft until every GATE below is ticked and lists AppImage, deb, rpm, the Flatpak remote URL and checksums.
- [ ] **M9.79 Linux packaged-artifact guards** - Linux legs run via `xtask package` (M6.03 legal gate); `check-native` (M6.72), `check-elf` and `audit-bundle` scan every package tree, and each installs in a clean container and passes `doctor --self-test`; a planted GPL library or old libheif fails.

### Packaging
- [ ] **M9.11 Tauri Linux bundle configuration** - `tauri.linux.conf.json` overlay: deb `depends` (`libwebkit2gtk-4.1-0`, `libgtk-3-0`), rpm `depends` (`webkit2gtk4.1`, `gtk3`), `files` maps, no tray dependency; `dpkg -I` and `rpm -qp --requires` list only distro libraries; both arches build.
- [ ] **M9.12 Private native libraries layout** - libheif, libde265 (plugin) and dav1d ship as separate shared libs in a private directory (`/usr/lib/auto-crop/`, AppImage `usr/lib`, Flatpak `/app/lib`) with `$ORIGIN` RPATH; `maps` show only private copies; HEIC corpus decodes from every package.
- [ ] **M9.13 AppImage hygiene and cross-distro run** - build on the 22.04 leg (`libfuse2` or `APPIMAGE_EXTRACT_AND_RUN=1`, pinned linuxdeploy, `bundleMediaFramework` off); SPIKE (2 days): run on Ubuntu 24.04, 26.04 (verify) and Fedora for Mesa/EGL mismatches; fix by exclude list or known issue.
- [ ] **M9.14 Desktop entry and icons** - `.desktop` file: `MimeType=` from the one extension list (M3.19, M6.85), `Exec=AutoCrop %F`, `Categories=Graphics;Photography;`, `StartupWMClass`, preset actions, hicolor icons; `desktop-file-validate` clean, entry shows on GNOME and KDE without re-login.
- [ ] **M9.15 AppStream metainfo** - metainfo with `MIT OR Apache-2.0`, CC0 metadata, OARS 1.1, pointing/keyboard/touch `supports`, releases, real light and dark screenshots, and text stating AI-assisted development (B17) and the bundled HEVC decoder; `appstreamcli validate --pedantic` clean.
- [ ] **M9.16 Packaging lint gates** - CI runs `desktop-file-validate`, `appstreamcli validate --pedantic`, `lintian`, `rpmlint` and `flatpak-builder-lint` as dev-time linters only (no Flathub before 1.0, B17); zero errors, warnings triaged with a reason each in `docs/packaging/lint-waivers.md`.
- [ ] **M9.17 Flatpak manifest** - hand-written `packaging/flatpak/` manifest (Tauri makes none): pinned GNOME runtime, sha256-pinned modules for libde265 and decode-only libheif (the SDK's lacks libde265), dav1d if needed, then the release `.deb`; a HEIC decodes, WebKitGTK version recorded.
- [ ] **M9.18 Flatpak permissions and portals** - finish-args (Wayland, fallback X11, ipc, dri, Pictures/Documents/Download; PROVISIONAL), no network (B18); test portal chooser, folder drop, Show in folder, Report issue, Save-as-copy fallback; SPIKE: nested WebKitGTK sandbox, not disabled silently.
- [ ] **M9.19 Own Flatpak remote** - GPG-signed OSTree repo and `.flatpakrepo` published under `/flatpak/` on the single Pages site (`pages.yml`, M1.64), pruned to two revisions (PROVISIONAL), plus a `.flatpak` bundle; add, install and update work on clean Ubuntu 22.04, 24.04 and Fedora VMs.
- [ ] **M9.20 no-hevc variant** - CI-only deb and rpm without the libde265 plugin (`xtask build-native --no-hevc`, M6.73) for distro packagers (B12); HEIC reports `HevcDecoderMissing` with Linux help text, other formats work and there is no crash; not a release asset by default.
- [ ] **M9.21 AppImage updater** - full `tauri-plugin-updater` in the AppImage: manual and opt-in weekly check (B18), install on click only, minisign-verified; static `latest.json` gains `linux-x86_64`/`linux-aarch64`; rc.1 to rc.2 round trip, tampered `.sig` rejected, read-only directory handled.
- [ ] **M9.22 Updater: notify-only deb and rpm, none in Flatpak** - own deb and rpm are notify-only (manual and opt-in weekly check, no Install); Flatpak, distro and no-hevc builds compile the updater out and point to the package manager; CI asserts: no plugin, capability or URL where compiled out.
- [ ] **M9.23 AUR -bin package (optional)** - `packaging/aur/auto-crop-bin/PKGBUILD` repacking the release deb with a pinned sha256 and `depends=(webkit2gtk-4.1 gtk3)`; `makepkg` and `namcap` clean in an `archlinux` container; publishing waits for the maintainer's AUR account (not scripted).

### Platform / WebKitGTK
- [ ] **M9.24 Graphics probe and shim decision table** - pure `decide_shims(GfxProbe, UserSettings) -> Shims` in `shell`; the probe reads session env vars, nvidia/DRM sysfs and `/dev/dri/renderD*`, never a GL driver in-process; tests over NVIDIA/Intel/AMD/no-node x Wayland/X11 x overrides.
- [ ] **M9.25 Apply shims in main()** - set `WEBKIT_DISABLE_DMABUF_RENDERER`, `__NV_DISABLE_EXPLICIT_SYNC`, `WEBKIT_DISABLE_COMPOSITING_MODE` before threads start (`unsafe`) unless user-set; Settings toggle (auto/safe/off), `AUTOCROP_GFX`, `--safe-graphics` override; blank window, Error 71 fixed.
- [ ] **M9.26 Software-rendering detection and warning** - a ~1 s pan/zoom micro-benchmark at first paint (M0.62) plus the no-render-node signal shows a dismissible banner linking troubleshooting and the toggle; fires on llvmpipe, 0 false positives on GPU rows; thresholds PROVISIONAL.
- [ ] **M9.27 Pinch-zoom hijack (wry#544, tauri#13115)** - 2-day spike then build (`unsafe` only in `with_webview`): `touch-action: none`, WebKitGTK zoom reset, `GDK_TOUCHPAD_PINCH` to JS; pinch zooms only the canvas (`visualViewport.scale`, DPR unchanged in 20 gestures) or the limit is documented.
- [ ] **M9.28 WebKitGTK versions and Tauri 3 watch** - record WebKitGTK/GTK3/libsoup3 versions in `docs/linux/webkitgtk-versions.json` and the M0.70 B8 verdict in `docs/adr/`; no second Slint trigger (touch gaps: B8 limits, S.03); a Slint, Tauri 3 or CEF move is the owner's (X.19), none merged.
- [ ] **M9.29 Session, HiDPI and window behaviour** - on GNOME and KDE (Wayland, X11) and Xfce (X11): app_id and WM_CLASS match the desktop entry, 100-200% scaling, mixed-scale move, maximise and snap, dark/light follow; results in `docs/linux/matrix.md`, each defect has a workaround or issue.
- [ ] **M9.30 Linux diagnostics** - extend `doctor` and Copy diagnostics with session, compositor, GPU and driver, WebKitGTK/GTK/libsoup versions, shims and why, package format, glibc, kernel, Landlock ABI and sandbox level; stable, no paths or file names, within the 4 KB Report issue block.
- [ ] **M9.31 Launch arguments and single instance** - handle `%F` paths, `%U`/`file://` URIs (percent-decoding, GVfs) and multi-selection via `tauri-plugin-single-instance`, 300 ms gather window; 50 files via Open With in Nautilus, Dolphin, Thunar open one session, cold and warm; URI fuzz green.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M9.32 Native dialogs and OS hand-off** - on GNOME and KDE, Wayland and X11 verify file and folder pickers (check the `rfd` backend), output folder, Show in folder (`FileManager1.ShowItems`, `xdg-open` fallback) and Report issue; gaps fall back to repeated single-folder picks, documented.
- [ ] **M9.33 Drag-and-drop matrix** - drop 1, 500, mixed and 10,000 files (PROVISIONAL), folders and non-images from Nautilus, Dolphin, Thunar, Nemo (best-effort) on Wayland and X11 via `WindowEvent::DragDrop`; position at 100% and 200%, names with spaces, `#`, `%`, non-ASCII; no UI freeze.
- [ ] **M9.34 Folder edge cases** - trees with a symlink loop, bind-mount loop, unreadable directory, NFS loopback and GVfs smb/sftp mounts (manual); each file processed once, no endless walk, mounts not crossed by default, unreadable folders in the Issues tray, network time to first item recorded.
- [ ] **M9.35 Real-touchscreen validation** - best-effort (B8) pass on >= 1 real touchscreen, Wayland and X11 (`docs/linux/touch-test-plan.md`): tap, handle drag (loupe), pan/zoom/rotate, swipe, long-press compare, edge swipes; each step works/partial/fails per device, session, WebKitGTK version.
- [ ] **M9.36 Touch fallbacks** - when pointer events or pinch fail, promote the M3 single-pointer controls (+/-, rotate and fine-angle buttons at 44-48 px, nudging, numeric fields), reset gestures on `pointercancel`; the review flow is recorded works/partial/fails and never blocks a release (B8).
- [ ] **M9.37 Keyboard, mouse, trackpad and IME** - M3-M7 flows keyboard-only, mouse-only and trackpad-only (no page zoom); GNOME/KDE shortcut and AltGr conflicts; Japanese input (ibus, fcitx5) in the name-template field; all actions work without touch, focus ring visible, IME works or is listed.
- [ ] **M9.38 Orca and AT-SPI smoke** - walk Import, Batch Review, Adjust and Export with Orca and `accerciser` (names, roles, live regions, slider and handle values), also in Flatpak; Orca is best-effort and does not gate 1.0 (A-11); results in `docs/linux/accessibility.md`, blocking gaps to M13.
- [ ] **M9.39 Nautilus integration** - opt-in script "Crop and straighten with Auto Crop" running `AutoCrop -- "$@"` (argv only, NUL-safe, injection-proof: no newline splitting of `NAUTILUS_SCRIPT_SELECTED_FILE_PATHS`, no `eval`); a name with newline, leading dash, quote, space opens as one item.
- [ ] **M9.40 Dolphin and Nemo entries** - deb and rpm ship a KDE service menu and a Nemo action (`auto-crop.nemo_action`) with `Exec=AutoCrop %F`, label "Crop and straighten with Auto Crop", images only; Flatpak export checked; shown on Fedora KDE (Plasma 6) and, best-effort, Mint Cinnamon.
- [ ] **M9.41 Thunar custom action** - opt-in merger for `~/.config/Thunar/uca.xml`: inserts one `<action>` (`<image-files/>`, `%F`), leaves others untouched, writes atomically with a timestamped backup, removes only its own; real-sample round-trip tests, fuzz green, Xubuntu works.
- [ ] **M9.42 Integration settings panel** - Settings > Integration (Linux) shows installed entries (package Dolphin and Nemo read-only) with opt-in Add/Remove for the Nautilus script and Thunar action, previewing each write; never changes default handlers (B19); idempotent, click-only writes.
- [ ] **M9.80 Touch capability probe** - at the first two-pointer sequence check that both pointers keep moving without `pointercancel` and `visualViewport.scale`/DPR stay put; persist it; on failure hide pinch hints, promote +/- and the slider (M3.24), go single-pointer; vitest replays as M3.23.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M9.43 Linux native build recipe** - Extends M6.71: ship `xtask build-native --target` on `ubuntu-22.04` x86_64, arm64 and Fedora (libde265 plugin, dav1d, no encoders, `native-deps.toml` pins); `readelf -d` shows no x265 or x264, M6 HEIC corpus within tolerance, pin drift fails.
- [ ] **M9.44 Linux shared-memory transport** - implement the M6.42 `Shm` trait with `memfd_create` and `F_SEAL_SHRINK`/`F_SEAL_GROW` (inherited fd), working under the empty Landlock ruleset; 100 MP RGB8 returns without a copy, 1,000 injected crashes leave no descriptors or `/dev/shm` entries.
- [ ] **M9.45 Backup method selection** - Extends M2.34: classify `EXDEV`, `EPERM`, `EOPNOTSUPP`, `EMLINK`, `fs.protected_hardlinks`; Btrfs subvolumes reflink only; FAT, exFAT, network (`statfs`) copy; M9.07 asserts the method per pair, Restore works from each, a hardlink backup survives replace.
- [ ] **M9.46 Atomic replace and metadata on Linux** - Extends M2.30/M2.31: copy mode, mtime, ACLs and xattrs (`user.*`, `security.selinux`); `getfattr`, `getfacl`, `ls -Z` unchanged on ext4, btrfs, xfs; Fedora SELinux enforcing keeps the label, no AVC denial; a failed label copy is a notice.
- [ ] **M9.47 Special files and volume quirks** - Extends M2.27/M2.29: warn when `nlink > 1` (other links keep the old content); immutable (`chattr +i`) files fail, original untouched; case-folding volumes (vfat, exFAT, CIFS, ext4 `+F`) use the global case-fold key; one fs-matrix test each.
- [ ] **M9.48 XDG paths and SQLite on network homes** - Extends M2.33: store at `$XDG_DATA_HOME/auto-crop`; settings, logs, caches follow XDG variables (Flatpak `~/.var/app`; never inside the AppImage), all `0700`; SQLite `journal_mode=DELETE` on NFS/SMB; XDG and NFS-home tests, `kill -9` recovery.
- [ ] **M9.49 Non-UTF-8 filenames end to end** - the shell sends opaque `SourceId`s plus a display name (serde rejects non-UTF-8 `Path`s) while `engine` and SQLite keep `OsString` and raw bytes; a folder with invalid UTF-8, newline and leading-dash names round-trips byte-identically, no panic.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M9.50 Landlock and seccomp worker sandbox** - `auto-crop-worker`, after plugins load and before untrusted bytes: `PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS`, an empty `landlock` ruleset, a `seccompiler` allow-list (not GPL `birdcage`); the corpus decodes inside on both arches, no file open, socket or exec.
