- [ ] **M9.51 Seccomp allowlist derivation** - per-arch allow-lists from traces (`strace -f`, `SECCOMP_RET_LOG`) of the corpus on Ubuntu 22.04 and Fedora, `ENOSYS` for `clone3`, kill mode in release and log mode in a `--sandbox-debug` build; zero `SIGSYS` on both, CI runs kill mode.
- [ ] **M9.52 Kernel feature detection, tiers and notice** - probe Landlock ABI and seccomp; levels `landlock+seccomp`, `seccomp-only`, `process-only`; `doctor` prints the level, one-time UI notice below full, `--require-sandbox` exits 6; forced-level tests, `docs/security/linux-sandbox.md`.
- [ ] **M9.53 Decode helper resource caps** - worker `RLIMIT_AS` (pixel cap plus headroom, PROVISIONAL), CPU, NOFILE, no core dumps (`PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0`), `MALLOC_ARENA_MAX=2`, RSS watchdog, optional cgroup v2; a 100 MP HEIC decodes under the cap on 32 threads, an over-cap job fails `MemoryLimit`.
- [ ] **M9.54 Crash and fault-injection tests** - run the M6.10 fault-injection worker (segfault, abort, spin, over-allocate, hang, pipe closed) on x86_64 and arm64: 1,000 faults each fail only their item (`DecoderCrashed`/`DecodeTimeout`), the worker respawns, the hash is not retried, no orphans.
- [ ] **M9.55 Decompression-bomb tests** - `xtask gen-bombs` (< 5 MB): huge-dimension headers, deflate, strip-count, GIF-frame, EXIF-loop, ICC and HEIC `ispe`/grid/`iloc` bombs; each rejected at probe (1 s, 64 MB) or killed within 10 s + 1 s/MP (PROVISIONAL); batch continues, no temp files.
- [ ] **M9.56 Sandbox denial probes and nested sandboxes** - a probe mode tries `socket`, `openat`, `execve`, `ptrace`, `mount`, `unshare`, each denied per level; run natively, in Docker, Flatpak and Ubuntu 24.04 with AppArmor (SPIKE, 1 day); a blocked seccomp degrades one level with the notice.
- [ ] **M9.57 Linux no-network test** - run deb, AppImage and Flatpak under `strace -f -e trace=connect,sendto` (Flatpak also `--unshare=network`) while opening, processing and exporting; no non-loopback connection except an explicit update check (never in the Flatpak); WebKitGTK traffic recorded.
- [ ] **M9.58 Bundle licence audit** - `xtask audit-bundle` maps each AppImage ELF to its Debian package and copyright file and fails on GPL-only libraries; Linux `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` list exact Ubuntu source versions of bundled LGPL libraries plus our libheif, libde265, dav1d source archive.
- [ ] **M9.59 LGPL relink drill on Linux** - extend `xtask relink-test` (M6.12) to Linux: AppImage (extract, swap `libheif.so.1` and plugin, repack), deb and rpm (swap private libs), Flatpak (rebuilt module); a HEIC still decodes from the replacement; fix `docs/replacing-libheif.md` where wrong.
- [ ] **M9.60 HEVC notices in Linux packages** - deb and rpm descriptions and `docs/linux/hevc.md` say the packages include an HEVC decoder (libde265), patent exposure is unresolved pending the legal read (B12, A-5), Fedora excludes HEVC, and how to get `no-hevc`; matches README and SECURITY.md.

- [ ] **M9.64 Accuracy gates on Fedora and the HEIC corpus** - gates on Fedora (public and synthetic sets; private set via the A-8 workflow) plus a HEIC convert check on Ubuntu and Fedora on public HEIC sets (dE2000 mean <= 0.5, p99 <= 1.5, PROVISIONAL); the private device corpus stays on the Mac.

- [ ] **M9.67 Linux performance baselines** - record criterion, hyperfine (12 MP end to end) and gungraun numbers on the Linux dev machine (Tier-M class) and CI runners plus sandbox overhead; budgets stay PROVISIONAL (700 ms p50 per 12 MP, CLI >= 4 img/s on 6 cores), overhead <= 5% of decode.
- [ ] **M9.68 arm64 performance record** - aarch64 ONNX Runtime latency (corner, orientation nets), `fast_image_resize` NEON, decode and 12 MP end to end on `ubuntu-22.04-arm` and, if available, a real arm64 board; `docs/linux/arm64.md` names hardware, best-effort (C1), runner numbers trend-only.

- [ ] **M9.78 Re-run UNMEASURED Linux cells** - repeat the M0.60, M0.61, M0.63, M0.64, M0.66 and M0.68 cells left UNMEASURED by the M0 ADRs on the packaged app (M9.62 rows, real touchscreen); PASS or FAIL with numbers; a touch failure is a B8 limitation (S.03), never a Slint trigger.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Matrix launch and render - every required M9.62 row starts, renders and passes the `--smoke-test` probe with the `main()` shims; the software-rendering banner fires on the GPU-less VM and on no GPU row; a required row marked unsupported needs a recorded owner decision (A-4).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Pan/zoom - on GPU rows (x86_64) the 24 MP workload (2-4 MP proxy plus 512 px tiles) holds median frame <= 22 ms and p95 <= 33 ms with mouse and trackpad (PROVISIONAL, unmeasured today); software-rasteriser rows need the banner and a 30 fps mean; touch fps is recorded only (B8).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Touch recorded - results from >= 1 real touchscreen, on Wayland and X11 where the device allows, are published in `docs/linux/touch-known-issues.md` as works/partial/fails best-effort notes; touch never blocks the release (B8).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Accuracy re-run - all M2-M7 accuracy gates (G1-G3) and the HEIC dE check (G10) pass on `ubuntu-22.04` and Fedora at the originating thresholds, and int8 shows p95 corner-error rise <= 0.05% of the diagonal and mean IoU delta <= 0.3 pt on every slice and platform.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Fuzz, fault and instruction gates - the Linux fuzz smoke (0 crashes or hangs), fault-injection and gungraun (within 5%) jobs are green on x86_64 and arm64.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Packages on clean VMs - AppImage, deb and rpm install (or run) and convert a HEIC on clean Ubuntu 22.04, 24.04, 26.04 (verify) and Fedora VMs; the own Flatpak remote installs and runs with its bundled libde265; `check-elf` shows GLIBC <= 2.35 for every shipped ELF.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Updater split - AppImage builds update in-app on an explicit click; own deb and rpm builds are notify-only (no Install); Flatpak, distro and no-hevc builds are proven by CI assertions to contain no updater plugin, capability or endpoint URL.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Sandbox - `landlock+seccomp` is reached on a kernel with Landlock; older kernels degrade to `seccomp-only` or `process-only` with the visible notice; crash and bomb injection suites pass on x86_64 and arm64 with no orphan processes.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Shell integration and input - Open With and context actions work on Nautilus (hostile-name fixture opens one item), Dolphin and Thunar; dialogs and folder drop pass on Wayland and X11; pinch-zoom hijack handled or documented; keyboard, mouse and trackpad flows complete.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Accessibility recorded - Orca and AT-SPI smoke results (pass or documented gaps) are committed to `docs/linux/accessibility.md`; Orca is best-effort and does not gate 1.0 (A-11).
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Re-run cells - every Linux cell the M0 ADRs left UNMEASURED is PASS or FAIL with numbers (M9.78), and a Linux touch failure is recorded as a B8 limitation, never an automatic Slint trigger.
- [ ] **GATE (G6):** Release hygiene - Linux assets carry `SHA256SUMS` and verified attestations; M9.79 fails a planted GPL library or old libheif; the bundle audit finds no GPL-only library; no non-loopback connection outside an update check; relink drill and WebKitGTK recheck dry run pass.

## M10 - Multi-item splitting

<!-- ms: version=v0.9.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64, macOS arm64, Linux x64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Detect and split several photos or receipts lying on one flatbed scan into separate, straightened outputs: classical detection, every accepted item a quad that runs the single-item pipeline, and touching, overlapping or doubtful items flagged for review, never merged or written. Under B3 a scan becomes N files: the scan moves to Backups, N template-named outputs take its place, and Restore offers Keep or Remove for the derived files (A-2).  
**Release:** `v0.9.0` (0.x preview). Windows 10/11 x64 (NSIS installer, portable zip; signed if SignPath has approved, B15), macOS 12+ (DMG, ad-hoc signed) and Linux Ubuntu 22.04+/Fedora (AppImage, deb, rpm, own Flatpak remote); Apple silicon (macOS arm64) is first-class, ARM64 on Windows and Linux and Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0. Every artefact has `SHA256SUMS` and GitHub attestations; no new models or native libraries.  
**Depends on:** M7 (per-item enhancement uses the enhancer and its validity mask), M9 (all three OS previews exist); builds on M2 (atomic writes, journal, CLI), M3 (quad editor), M4 (detectors, confidence, orientation net, M4.80 `snap_corner`), M5 (review grid, Backups panel, Restore original); the X.41 gate-miss ADR (A-4) must exist before the gates are run.  
**Size:** XL (definition in X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL).  
**Design:** [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md) (4.6, 4.7), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md) (2.7), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md) (3.2.3, 3.5), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md) (7.4, 7.11), [PLAN 5](docs/plan/05-enhancement.md), [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md).  
**Exit gate:** G4: on well-separated items exact item count >= 97%, recall >= 95% and precision >= 97% at IoU 0.9, >= 90% of touching or overlapping scans routed to review, and silent wrong splits <= 1.0% of auto-accepted scans (one-sided 95% bound reported; real n < 80 advisory). Single-item throughput within 5% of v0.8.0 and 0 partial sets in 1-to-N fault injection on all three OSes; all PROVISIONAL until measured, and a missed gate is never relaxed silently (A-4).  
**Risks / spikes:**
- **Data loss.** Replacing one scan with N files risks partial sets, name collisions, an early source unlink and clobbered edits; M10.22-M10.27, fault injection M10.64 and the hazard checklist M10.66 (written first, blocks the release) cover it. N-to-1 (`ManyToOne`) is M11.11's.
- **Hard cases and statistics.** Touching, overlapping, soft-shadow and white-on-white scans are flagged, never solved; ~50 real scans cannot certify 1% (zero failures gives a ~6% bound, ~300 are needed) and synthetic data never does, so auto-save ships Experimental; hardware gates stay open until measured (A-9).
- **Order.** Data and metrics first (M10.51-M10.56), then spikes M10.57-M10.60; every threshold is a PROVISIONAL starting value until they report; the real slice keeps growing toward >= 80 scans (golden v2, M13).

### Detection
- [ ] **M10.01 Scan triage (bed-like)** - Extends M2.16: replaces its thresholds, retires `MultiItemSuspected` (M2.05; held rows re-analysed). Four 1.5% border strips on the 1024 px proxy; bed-like if all four sides (or three) agree (dE76 <= 8). Accept: >= 95% correct on lid fixtures, <= 3 ms.
- [ ] **M10.02 Scanner border and edge-line trim** - mask platen frame, lid shadow and 1-6 px edge lines in the first 3% of each side (rows differing from the bed along >= 90% of their length); never trim more than 3% per side or eat items within 1%. Accept: no border component, item IoU >= 0.97.
- [ ] **M10.03 Bed model and crisp-edge mask** - bed = median Lab of border strips; dE76 map at 1024 px; Otsu with floor dE 6, seeds dE 12, hysteresis; keep a boundary only if its 10-90% ramp is <= 3 px (shadow ramps >= 6 px drop). Accept: edge within 2 px on >= 95% of shadow fixtures; <= 10 ms.
- [ ] **M10.04 Morphology and components** - close at 0.5-1% of the long side but below half the minimum gap (1.5% of the shorter side), fill holes off the border, open 2-3 px, label 8-connected, cap 2,000 components. Accept: sky-hole photo recovered; items 1.6% apart not bridged.
- [ ] **M10.05 Candidate filters: area, dust, lid edge** - drop components < 1% of the scan and lid-edge or hinge artefacts (thin shapes spanning >= 50% of a side, hairlines > 15:1); rejects go to `Analysis.diagnostics.rejected[]` for 'Add as item'. Accept: 0 false items on dust fixtures.
- [ ] **M10.06 Rectangle fit and clipped items** - `min_area_rect` per component (verify in `imageproc` 0.27), accept at fill >= 0.9, bed margin +0.5% of the shorter side (max 12 px at 12 MP), |angle| <= 45 degrees; clipped items get `PARTIAL_FRAME` and Check. Accept: IoU >= 0.97 to GT.
- [ ] **M10.07 Split candidates: watershed and line cuts** - for fill < 0.9 or deep convexity defects: distance-transform watershed and LSD/EDLines cuts (LSD per M1.28); each cut re-runs M10.06 on both pieces (fill >= 0.9, area >= 1%). Accept: abutting rectangles at 5 degrees split, IoU >= 0.9.
- [ ] **M10.08 Cut acceptance gate (crisp support)** - cut only with crisp support on source pixels (gradient >= 3x noise MAD over >= 70% of the cut, ramp <= 3 px), else keep it whole with `TOUCHING_ITEMS`; best 2 cuts kept as alternatives. Accept: shadow bridge not cut; seam cut within 2 px.
- [ ] **M10.09 Overlap and cluster flagging** - a component still non-rectangular after cuts becomes one `Cluster` (count range, alternatives) flagged `OVERLAPPING_ITEMS` or `TOUCHING_ITEMS`, never an accepted rectangle. Accept: >= 90% of overlap scans routed to review; count range right on >= 80%.
- [ ] **M10.10 Split stability check** - re-run mask and components at 0.7x and 1.4x the low dE threshold (<= 8 ms extra); a different item count or pair IoU < 0.9 sets `SPLIT_UNSTABLE` (Check). Accept: sensitive fixtures flagged, >= 95% of clean ones not; 1.4x pass dropped if M10.57 shows no gain.
- [ ] **M10.11 Low-contrast boundary guard** - median dE across an edge under ~8, or no crisp support, sets `LOW_CONTRAST_EDGE` and caps the item at Check; receipts also get Detector B as second opinion, never sole basis for Good. Accept: white-on-white slice: 0 auto-accepted items with IoU < 0.9.
- [ ] **M10.12 Photos and Receipts profiles** - presets: Documents and receipts = Auto + Receipts, several items = Auto + Photos, Photos = Never; Photos keep placed orientation, aspect <= 4:1 else `ODD_ASPECT`; Receipts: 12:1, orientation net. Accept: 3-6 receipts reach >= 95% precision and recall.
- [ ] **M10.13 Outcome rules: zero, one, many** - zero items on a bed-like scan = `NO_DOCUMENT` (Check, no write); one item >= 95% of the scan or `split: Never` = single-item route; > 32 keeps the largest (`TOO_MANY_ITEMS`); `Always` on a non-bed scan = Check (`BED_UNCERTAIN`). Accept: table test.
- [ ] **M10.14 Per-item hand-off to the pipeline** - each accepted item is an `Item` through the existing stages (S5-S8, B14 crop, one Lanczos3 resample; orientation for Receipts only), one `render` path. Accept: a one-item scan via `Always` equals `Never` within 1 level and 0.5 px.
- [ ] **M10.15 Per-item hold codes and confidence** - every code raised in M10.04-M10.13 plus `ITEMS_TOO_CLOSE` (clear gap < 1.5% of the shorter side) and `ANALYSIS_LIMIT` caps an item at Check, whatever its score; scan = minimum over items. Accept: cap tests; a 1.2% gap is held, unwritten.
- [ ] **M10.16 Multi-item calibration** - extend `calibration.json` (<= 6 multi-item features) fitted on the real dev split only, cutoffs from the risk-coverage curve; until a 1% claim holds M10.29's Strict rule applies and auto-save is labelled Experimental. Accept: reliability table (Wilson, n).

### Core
- [ ] **M10.17 N-item EditState and migration** - `EditState.items` for N > 1 (`order`, `auto`; turns and mirror stay in `geometry`) and `SplitState` (policy, profile, order mode, next id), pure migration. Accept: proptest undo/redo; old-session and `MultiItemSuspected` fixtures; `Never` = M2.16.
- [ ] **M10.18 Item operations and re-detection** - `core::items` ops: add, remove or restore (`include`), merge, split (pieces >= 2%), reorder; `redetect` keeps Manual and edited items, drops overlapping auto ones. Accept: proptest: order is a permutation, ids unique, merge then split within 2 px.
- [ ] **M10.19 Per-item history and revert** - History labels name the item; gesture ids include the ItemId; `revert_item(Auto | Step(n))` and Reset to auto touch that item only. Accept: reverting item k leaves others byte-identical; one undo reverts a preset applied to 20 scans.
- [ ] **M10.20 Reading order and manual reorder** - rows by >= 50% vertical overlap, then centroid x, ties by ItemId; a manual reorder sets `order_mode = Manual` and survives `redetect`; numbering stays left-to-right in RTL. Accept: order invariant to detection permutation and +-2% jitter.
- [ ] **M10.21 Output naming template for 1-to-N** - Extends M2.27: adds the `{n}` token and the 1-to-N default `{name}_{n}` (1-based rank among included items, zero-padded to max(2, digits(N))); one included item keeps the single-item name. Accept: proptest: no escaping or reserved path.
- [ ] **M10.22 FsPlan two-phase collision plan** - Extends M2.27 (NFC + case-fold key on all OSes): plans a scan's N outputs as one group, also against queued sources; phase 2 re-stats before each commit (`PlanStale` aborts). Accept: adversarial batches never collide.
- [ ] **M10.23 Group commit protocol for 1-to-N** - Extends PLAN 2.7: N verified temps, backup journalled, source re-stat (`SourceChanged` aborts), N no-clobber renames, fsync, source unlinked last (`SavedSourceInUse` if locked). Accept: N outputs verify, backup exists; 3-page TIFF scan untouched.
- [ ] **M10.24 Group crash recovery** - Extends PLAN 2.7 recovery: pre-Committing rows delete temps and requeue; Committing rolls forward only if all N temps match journalled hashes, else back to original plus backup, never a partial set. Accept: fail-points at every step and rename pair (M10.64).
- [ ] **M10.25 ExportRecord and manifest migration** - Extends M2.33 (outputs already a list): additive migration only, adding `kind` (`OneToN`) and per-output `item_id`, `index`; the idempotency guard checks every output hash. Accept: migrates a real v0.8.0 store; guard sees all N outputs.
- [ ] **M10.26 Restore original for 1-to-N** - Owns derived-file Restore (A-2): `restore(backup, derived: Keep | Remove)` returns the scan byte-identical (occupied path: `name (restored).ext`); Remove is reversible; changed files default to Keep. Accept: states x {Keep, Remove}, with restart.
- [ ] **M10.27 Re-save from the pristine backup** - Edits after a 1-to-N save re-render from the backup and replace the recorded set by group commit: surplus ones move to the store, a user-edited derived file is never replaced (suffix, notice). Accept: N 4 to 3 and 4 to 5, external edit, restart.
- [ ] **M10.28 Per-item render API and tile keys** - `render_item` and `render()`; interactive pool parallel per item, batch pool sequential per scan; tile URL and cache key gain the item id. Accept: editing item 2 keeps item 1's cache; 1 vs 8 threads identical; an item panic fails only its scan.
- [ ] **M10.29 Scan-level triage and job state** - `Approved(Auto)` only if every item is Good at the Strict cutoff (M4.44) and no M10.15 code fires, else `HeldForReview{ItemsNeedCheck(n)}`, nothing written (0.x preview rule). Accept: predicate table test; output is all or nothing.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M10.30 Lossless path off for split items** - `lossless_plan` returns `NotApplicable(MultiItem)` for 2+ items (crops rotated or off the MCU grid); items re-encode once at source-estimated quality (M2.25), no 'lossless' badge; one item keeps M2.24. Accept: no transform path for a 2-item scan.
- [ ] **M10.31 Per-item encode, verify and metadata** - items use the M2 encode and verify path; metadata per C2 and M2.23 (`ImageUniqueID` dropped); a never-replaced source (multi-page TIFF, PLAN 3.2.3) is never moved: skipped with `tiff.multi_page`, copies only on opt-in. Accept: N valid files.

### Enhancement
- [ ] **M10.32 Per-item enhancement suggestion** - `suggest_enhance` (M7.15) runs per item on its proxy with the item as validity mask (M7.03), so estimates never mix items or bed; Photos never suggest. Accept: a mixed scan suggests for receipts only; paper estimate within 2 levels of a solo scan.
- [ ] **M10.33 Per-item enhance override** - `Item.enhance_override` overrides the scan default; the M7.40 scope selector gains 'This item' and 'All items in this scan'; the M7.41 banner counts items; never forced (B13). Accept: changing item 2 leaves item 1's bytes and cache unchanged.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M10.34 IPC contract for items** - extend `set_edit` and the TS types with item patches (add, remove, restore, merge, split, reorder, revert, set_split) and `detect_item_at`; ids only, stale results dropped, no pixels over IPC. Accept: bindings diffed in CI; a 100-op script never desyncs.
- [ ] **M10.35 Item overlay on the source view** - SVG overlay: a chip (order, band icon and word, reason) selects on tap; the selected item gets 16 px handles with 48 px hit areas and wins overlaps; excluded items ghosted, rejects 'Add as item'. Accept: Playwright, 8 items; drags need no IPC.
- [ ] **M10.36 Item chip bar, selection and menu** - chip bar (icon, band, reason, output name, roving tabindex) auto-zooms to the item; chip menu (press-hold, Shift+F10): Remove, Reset, Move, Merge, Cut, Restore; reorder by drag or buttons (WCAG 2.5.7). Accept: same EditState by touch and menu.
- [ ] **M10.37 Add and remove item** - tap a missed item to run `detect_item_at` (`snap_corner` magnet M4.80; 20% inset box if nothing found) or drag a box; `origin: Manual`, one undo entry; Remove sets `include = false` ('Removed items' list). Accept: added item IoU >= 0.95; undo restores the id.
- [ ] **M10.38 Merge tool** - select two or more items (Shift, long-press, or Merge then tap), preview the minimum-area rectangle of the union, confirm; key `M` (proposed, rebindable). Accept: two halves of a split photo merge to IoU >= 0.97; undo restores both items; an excluded item is refused.
- [ ] **M10.39 Cut (split) tool** - 'Cut' mode: two points across the selected item, live preview of both rectangles (snapped to the nearer axis, pieces >= 2%), Confirm or Cancel; 'Split in halves' and a slider (WCAG 2.5.7); key `K`. Accept: merged prints cut within 2 px of GT by touch.
- [ ] **M10.40 Treat-as-one and Split presets** - inspector 'Items: Auto | Treat as one item | Split into items' and Photos | Receipts, also as grid menu and batch actions; each runs `redetect` as one undo entry. Accept: one undo restores 20 scans; 'Treat as one item' equals the single-item output.
- [ ] **M10.41 Review reasons, alternatives and banners** - code-to-copy table: every M10.13 and M10.15 code has a message key and fixture; 'Try suggested split' cycles the M10.08 alternatives; zero-item banner offers Draw items, Treat as one item, Skip. Accept: held scans are never written.
- [ ] **M10.42 Batch grid integration** - count badge ('x4'); tile band and sort use the minimum over items, Expand shows per-item sub-tiles, Needs review lists scans with any Check item first, Save all counts files and scans. Accept: E2E on 20 mixed scans; one Check item keeps the scan unwritten.
- [ ] **M10.43 Split saving settings and first-split sheet** - Extends M3.41: 'Keep the scan' setting (off, A-2) with profile, template, limits; first-split sheet (Replace / Keep); never-replaced sources: copies on opt-in only. Accept: E2E both choices; `--in-place` moves, `--output` keeps.
- [ ] **M10.44 Backups panel and Restore dialog** - UI for M10.26: run entry 'scan.jpg to 4 files' lists derived files with status; dialog 'Keep them / Remove them' (changed files listed, Keep preselected); 'Restore all from this run' handles mixed entries. Accept: E2E per state; axe clean.
- [ ] **M10.45 Accessibility and i18n pass** - item-named handles and live announcements; keyboard-only add, remove, merge, cut, reorder; shortcuts (`I`, `M`, `K`) join the rebindable table (lint); pseudo-locale +40%, RTL mirrors the chip bar only. Accept: axe 0 serious; NVDA, VoiceOver runs.
- [ ] **M10.46 Usability tasks MI-1 and MI-2** - MI-1: fix a wrong merge, a missed and a dust item in a 6-item scan; MI-2: restore after a split, pick Keep or Remove; >= 5 participants (Surface, Mac trackpad, keyboard). Accept: >= 80% unaided, >= 90% of drags on target; Linux touch recorded.
- [ ] **M10.79 Per-item rotate and flip** - Turn 90 L/R, the M3.32 angle ruler and Flip act on the selected item's QuadWarp (turns, `fine_deg`, mirror), one undo entry each, with keyboard alternatives; patches `rotate_item`, `set_item_angle`. Accept: rotating item 2 changes only its bytes.

### CLI
- [ ] **M10.47 `--split` flags** - `process|analyze --split auto|always|never`, `--split-profile photos|receipts`, `--max-items N`; `--preset`: flatbed = auto/photos, receipt = auto/receipts, others never; `--in-place` (default) moves the scan, `--output`/`--suffix` keep it. Accept: clap snapshots.
- [ ] **M10.48 JSON schema for items** - `--json` adds `outcome: Split(n)`, split settings, `items[] {id, order, quad, include, confidence, state, output, exit}` and `diagnostics.rejected[]`; `--ndjson` emits `item` events; additive under `v:1`, CI-checked schema. Accept: golden 4-item JSON.
- [ ] **M10.49 Item exit codes and hold semantics** - `items[].exit` reuses M2.45 codes: any held item holds the scan (exit 4, nothing written); new codes `PlanStale`, `GroupCommitFailed`, `SavedSourceInUse`; `--dry-run` (M2.42) lists the names. Accept: code matrix; dry-run equals written names.
- [ ] **M10.50 `render --item` and `restore --derived`** - `render` (M2.81) gains `--item <id>`; `restore <id> --derived keep|remove` (required without a TTY) uses M10.26 and never deletes; `backups show` lists derived outputs. Accept: render equals GUI export (`--deterministic`).

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M10.51 Synthetic multi-item generator** - Extends M1.30: `tools/synth/multi_item.py` places 2-8 items (photos, receipts) on white, black, gradient and lid-shaded beds, separated to overlapping, clipped, white-on-white, with shadows and dust. Accept: >= 500 scans, >= 50 per hard slice.
- [ ] **M10.52 PD photo set and licence register** - `testdata/pd-photos/` (<= 2 MB) and a pinned fetch-script set; each asset has a register row (M7.69), provenance and REUSE metadata; unclear, non-commercial and share-alike licences excluded. Accept: CI rejects an image without a row.
- [ ] **M10.53 Real multi-item flatbed slice** - Extends M1.39: grow to >= 50 real multi-item scans on 2+ scanners; strata: separated >= 20, touching >= 10, overlapping >= 5, receipts >= 10, white-on-white >= 5, dust/edge >= 5; images stay on the encrypted disk (A-8, B21).
- [ ] **M10.54 Labelling protocol and tool** - Extends M1.40-M1.43: per-item labels (corners, class, `touching_with`, overlap order, `ignore` under 1%), blank-quad tool (M1.41), validator (convex, no GT pair IoU >= 0.8), 20% double-labelled. Accept: first 20 scans labelled, hours logged.
- [ ] **M10.55 Scene-disjoint split for multi-item** - Extends M1.44 and M4.47: one `scene_id` per physical item set (rescans share it), disjoint across dev and golden, synthetic bases included; a golden result that guides a change retires those scans. Accept: CI checker fails a violating manifest.
- [ ] **M10.56 Item metrics and silent failures** - `auto-crop-eval` adds item precision, recall, exact-count, over-split and routing rates (IoU 0.9); a silent wrong split (auto-accepted, wrong count or item IoU < 0.90) is k/n with a one-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson bound. Accept: n < 30 suppressed.
- [ ] **M10.57 Spike MI-1: bed and morphology sweep** - sweep dE thresholds, ramp limit, close radius, hole fill, area floor and fill threshold on synthetic and real dev data; choose by the worst slice, confirm once on the locked split. Accept: `docs/multi-item-sweep.md`; tested `defaults` module.
- [ ] **M10.58 Spike MI-2: watershed vs line cuts** - compare watershed, LSD/EDLines cuts, both and flag-only on touching slices (correct and wrong splits, review rate, ms); default = lowest wrong-split rate within 40 ms p50 (else flag-only). Accept: table, ADR; M10.07 and M10.08 defaults set.
- [ ] **M10.59 Spike MI-3: ROI net pass** - test Detector A on each item's ROI + 15% (and the 512x128 pass for narrow receipts) per profile on the dev split; on only where it raises item IoU at <= 25 ms per item; confirm the 4:1 and 12:1 limits. Accept: per-profile table; M10.12 defaults set.
- [ ] **M10.60 Spike MI-4: shadows, white on white** - measure ramp-width and edge-ring separation of edges from shadows and the white-on-white wrong-accept rate; set the `LOW_CONTRAST_EDGE` threshold; decide if a bright lid (luma > ~235) caps at Check. Accept: M10.03 and M10.11 updated.
- [ ] **M10.61 Benchmarks and instruction counts** - `criterion` benches per split stage, total analysis at 1024 px and per-item render at 12 and 48 MP; gungraun `main` vs PR head in one job (> 5% fails). Accept: split analysis <= 40 ms p50 (Tier-M); a 10% slowdown canary fails.
- [ ] **M10.62 Single-item regression vs v0.8.0** - hyperfine (warm, cold) and gungraun on the 12 MP single-item JPEG set, v0.8.0 vs branch head in one job on Windows Tier-M and Linux; triage <= 5 ms at 1024 px. Accept: throughput regression <= 5%; blocks the release; canary-tested.
- [ ] **M10.63 Memory and parallelism tests** - a 48 MP scan with 8 items peaks under 3x decoded RGB8 + 64 MB RSS (Windows, Linux); `MemoryBudget` (M1.10) weight = pixels x 3 + K x largest item x 6 + 64 MB; 4 concurrent 48 MP scans fit, a 100 MP scan runs alone under 1.0 GB. Accept: nightly, blocks.
- [ ] **M10.64 Fault-injection suite for 1-to-N** - Extends M2.58: fail-points at every M10.23 step and between renames, kills (>= 500 per OS), disk full, bad temp, changed or locked source, cross-volume, 3-page TIFF. Accept: 0 partial sets, lost originals or stray temps; Restore byte-identical.
- [ ] **M10.65 Kernel correctness, oracles and fuzz** - proptest of split kernels vs naive references, no panics on degenerate input; `cv2` fixtures within 1 px; `cargo-fuzz` on `bed_analyze`, `split_watershed`, `naming_template`, `restore_plan` (60 s PR, 1 h nightly). Accept: no crashes or hangs.

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M10.66 Threat model and data-loss checklist** - `docs/design/one-to-n-safety.md` before the commit code: hazards (partial sets, collisions, early unlink, clobbered user files, purge outside the store) each mapped to a test (M10.22-M10.27, M10.64). Accept: open hazards block the release.
- [ ] **M10.67 Resource caps and typed limits** - `MAX_ITEMS` 32, 2,000 components (`TOO_MANY_ITEMS`), a watershed step cap and a 200 ms analysis cap (Check, `ANALYSIS_LIMIT`) plus the pixel caps; per-scan `catch_unwind`. Accept: noise and 5,000-speck scans finish; a splitter panic fails one scan.
- [ ] **M10.68 Privacy guard for real scans** - public CI fails on an image outside `testdata/synthetic` and `testdata/pd-photos` (5 MB, EXIF/GPS scrub); the aggregate-only test covers `multi_item` (no names or per-scan rows); contact sheets never leave (B21). Accept: a real-looking image fails.
- [ ] **M10.69 Licence and provenance audit** - no new normal dependency (`cargo deny check`, `cargo-about` regenerated); dev tools (Label Studio, Augraphy, OpenCV) in the M7.69 register; no GPL or AGPL-derived splitting code (Scan Tailor Advanced, original LSD). Accept: deny, REUSE lint green.

### Docs
- [ ] **M10.70 Multi-item guide** - `docs/guide/multi-item.md`: scan tips (gap >= 5 mm, dark backing, items off the platen edge, 300-600 dpi: A4 at 1200 dpi is ~139 MP, over the 100 MP default cap), review, Restore (Keep or Remove), JPEG re-encode, numbers with n. Accept: a non-author passes MI-1.

Accept: each cites a test ID.
- [ ] **M10.72 Design doc and ADRs** - `docs/design/multi-item.md` (pipeline, `defaults`-generated parameters, reason codes, schemas) and ADRs: classical splitting only, 1-to-N per B3 and A-2, placed photo orientation, cap 32, no overlap solver. Accept: table equals `defaults`.
- [ ] **M10.73 README, CHANGELOG and CLI reference** - README feature list and synthetic screenshots ('experimental until the slice grows'), CHANGELOG, CLI reference and man page for the split flags and `restore --derived`, JSON Schema. Accept: link check green; `--help` equals the reference.
- [ ] **M10.74 Publish aggregate evaluation results** - `docs/multi-item-eval.md` (aggregates only): item precision, recall, exact-count, routing and silent wrong-split rates per slice with n and bounds, real vs synthetic; says ~50 scans cannot certify 1%. Accept: matches the gate table.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M10.75 Per-PR smoke and 3-OS checks** - multi-item smoke eval on 20 synthetic scans (<= 2 MB, fork-safe) blocks on precision or recall down > 1 pt, a silent wrong split or a cross-OS count difference; FsPlan, group-commit and recovery tests run on 3 OSes. Accept: a broken cut gate fails.
- [ ] **M10.76 Nightly and private evaluation** - Extends M7.77: adds 500 synthetic scans and wall-clock split runs to the nightly; the real slice runs only via the private `auto-crop-golden` self-hosted workflow (A-8), aggregates only; new multi-item panels. Accept: no names or paths in output.
- [ ] **M10.77 Release v0.9.0** - release-plz tag; Windows (NSIS, zip; signed if SignPath approved, B15), macOS arm64 (DMG, ad-hoc) and Linux (AppImage, deb, rpm, Flatpak) builds with `SHA256SUMS` and attestations; notes state the Experimental label. Accept: `gh attestation verify` passes.
- [ ] **M10.78 Clean-machine smoke test** - on clean Windows 10/11, macOS arm64, Ubuntu 22.04 and Fedora: install, split, fix one item, Save, Restore (Keep, Remove), uninstall, backups remain; Flatpak without folder access saves copies; self-tests gain a 2-item scan. Accept: per-OS checklist.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Separated items - on the multi-item slice (>= 50 real flatbed scans, advisory below n = 80, plus >= 500 synthetic) exact item count >= 97%, recall >= 95%, precision >= 97% at IoU 0.9, real and synthetic apart with Clopper-Pearson intervals (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Touching and silent errors - >= 90% of touching or overlapping scans routed to review; silent wrong splits <= 1.0% of auto-accepted scans with the one-sided 95% bound printed; a bound above 2.0% ships auto-save Experimental (Strict-equivalent), owner decides 1.0 (A-4).
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Speed and regression - single-item throughput regresses <= 5% vs v0.8.0 on the 12 MP set; split analysis <= 40 ms p50 on the 1024 px proxy (Tier-M); a 48 MP scan with 8 items stays within the RSS budget (PROVISIONAL until re-baselined).
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** 1-to-N semantics - N template-named outputs with the batch-wide collision plan; the scan moves to Backups (never-replaced sources stay untouched); Restore returns it byte-identical and offers Keep or Remove; M10.64 shows 0 partial sets and 0 lost originals on all three OSes.
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Review workflow - per-item confidence, chips, add, remove, merge, cut, rotate, flip, both presets and per-item undo pass E2E; MI-1 and MI-2 have no unrecovered failures on Windows touch and Mac trackpad (Linux touch recorded, not gating).
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Quality parity - the split path for a one-item scan equals the single-item pipeline within 1 level per channel and 0.5 px; changing one item's enhancement changes no other item's bytes.
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Harness - item metrics per slice (precision, recall, IoU, over-split, under-split, silent rate) in the metrics JSON and dashboard; cross-OS parity <= 0.2 pt mean item IoU; smoke and nightly workflows green on all three OSes.
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Golden set and data hygiene - the multi-item slice is labelled and scene-disjoint (checker green); no real scans in the public repo (guard green); the licence register covers every new asset and tool; `cargo deny check` is green.
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Docs and honesty - the multi-item guide and known limitations are published with measured numbers and their n; PROVISIONAL tags are removed only where measured.
- [ ] **GATE (G4):** Release - `v0.9.0` artefacts for Windows, macOS and Linux have checksums and attestations and the clean-machine smoke test passes on all three OSes.

## M11 - Output formats, PDF and metadata policy

<!-- ms: version=v0.10.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64, macOS 12+ arm64, Linux x86_64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Write every 1.0 export format (JPEG, PNG, WebP lossy and lossless, AVIF, JPEG XL, TIFF incl. multi-page and 1-bit CCITT G4, PDF single and multi-page) through one export sheet and the CLI, with the metadata, colour and dpi policy (C2), each encoder checked by independent readers on three OSes. No HEVC is encoded and no GPL dependency is added: JPEG XL uses our own libjxl FFI (never `jpegxl-rs`), AVIF uses ravif and rav1e.
**Release:** `v0.10.0` - Windows 10/11 x64 (NSIS installer, portable zip; unsigned unless SignPath has approved, B15), macOS 12+ arm64 (ad-hoc signed DMG), Linux x86_64 (Ubuntu 22.04+, Fedora: AppImage, deb, rpm, own Flatpak remote); Apple silicon is first-class, ARM64 on Windows and Linux and Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0; `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, SBOM, `native-sources` archive extended with libwebp and libjxl (static link, no new shared library).
**Depends on:** M7 (bilevel raster, 1-bit PNG, TIFF G4 wrapper M7.28/M7.29), M9 (Linux preview) and M10 (name grammar M10.21/M10.22, ExportRecord M10.25, item outputs); extends M2 (M2.27 FsPlan, M2.23 metadata, commit protocol, CLI), M5 (review grid, Backups, Save-as-copy setting) and M6 (worker pool, tone-mapping, `codecs::meta`).
**Size:** XL (definition in X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL).
**Design:** [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 5](docs/plan/05-enhancement.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md), [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md).
**Exit gate:** G11 (exports): every format decodes in >= 2 independent readers on three OSes (100% of the interop matrix), PDF opens in >= 3 viewers per OS and a 200-page export stays <= 500 MB RSS (PROVISIONAL), size estimates are within +-15%, the AVIF default is <= 10 s at 12 MP on Tier-M (PROVISIONAL), GPS-leak and licence checks are green and CLI and GUI outputs match. A missed gate is never relaxed silently (A-4); M11.28 does not gate.
**Risks / spikes:**
- Schedule (XL): the own libjxl FFI with a static C++ build on three OSes, AVIF encode speed and the lossy WebP route are the long poles, so spikes S1-S5 (M11.01-M11.05) run first; a failed encoder gate delays 1.0 or goes to the owner with numbers (A-4), never a silent cut or relabel.
- Oracle independence: Chrome and Pillow share libwebp and dav1d, so each format needs a reader from another code base; release browsers may ship JPEG XL off. `catch_unwind` cannot stop a C++ `abort()`: FFI wrappers are fuzzed and a reachable abort moves that encoder into the worker.
- Data and metadata: lossy conversion under B3 adds a generation of loss (verified backup, notices, Restore); edits can corrupt EXIF offsets, thumbnails and MPF (in-place patcher, GPS-leak suite); OCR and JBIG2 stay out of scope (B19); the C2 default lives in one constant (M11.15).

### Spikes
- [ ] **M11.01 Spike S1: libjxl static build** - Build libjxl 0.12.x statically on windows-2025 (MSVC, clang-cl), macos-latest arm64, ubuntu-22.04; record time, sizes, licences. Pass: ADR; a C program encodes 64x64 and `djxl` decodes it on all three; a failing OS goes to the owner (A-4).
- [ ] **M11.02 Spike S2: ravif and rav1e** - At 12 MP on Tier-M measure time, CPU, peak RSS across speeds, threads, depth, chroma; check `colr`, Exif, XMP, monochrome, cancel latency. Pass: ADR fixes defaults, metadata route (M11.23), cancel plan (M11.24); 10 s budget confirmed or amended.
- [ ] **M11.03 Spike S3: lossy WebP route** - Compare `libwebp-sys`, the `webp` crate and an own ~12-call FFI (check licences); pin libwebp >= 1.3.2 (CVE-2023-4863); build on three OSes. Pass: draft ADR; an OS that cannot build it goes to the owner (A-4), no silent 'WebP (lossless)' relabel.
- [ ] **M11.04 Spike S4: PDF streaming, embeddings** - Test if `pdf-writer` 0.15 streams pages with own xref offsets, else write a ~400-line one; prototype DCT, PNG `IDAT` (`/Predictor 15`) and CCITT G4 pages in pdf.js, Poppler, PDFium, OS viewer. Pass: 200-page peak RSS measured; ADR (M11.72).
- [ ] **M11.05 Spike S5: TIFF multi-page route** - Choose `tiff` 0.11 plus the M7 G4 wrapper (1-bit applies to every page) or an own IFD-chain writer so pages can mix; test Exif, XMP, ICC, 16-bit predictor, BigTIFF. Pass: ADR; 12-page fixture read by libtiff, Pillow, ImageMagick, `tiff`.

### Core
- [ ] **M11.07 Extend `OutputSpec` and presets** - Extends M1.06: format, quality, resize, `allow_upscale`, metadata, colour, icc, dpi, `gain_map`, `concurrency`, `collision` (Rename|Skip|Replace), `verify` (Full|Fast; never Off, not a setting), presets table. Pass: snapshot, proptest, defaults.
- [ ] **M11.08 Encoder trait and registry** - `codecs::encode::Encoder` per format reports caps (size, alpha, 16-bit, gray, lossless, multi-page, weight), streams to `Write + Seek`, honours `CancelToken`; UI, CLI, FsPlan read it. Pass: conformance suite; typed `ErrKind` failures, no panics.
- [ ] **M11.09 Name-template tokens** - Extends M2.27 and M10.21 (one grammar and NFC + case-fold key; `{name}` 1-to-1, `{name}_{n}` 1-to-N; `{n}` = item rank, else batch index): adds `{date}`, `{ext}`, `{count}`, `{n:3}`, typed errors. Pass: >= 40 table cases + proptest (legal, idempotent).
- [ ] **M11.10 `plan_export` and collisions** - Extends M2.27: `plan_export` lists names, collisions and backup bytes before any write; `collision` = Rename ('Keep both', default), Skip or Replace (backup-then-swap); no Ask. Pass: 50 files/10 collisions per policy; mid-swap crash keeps a file.
- [ ] **M11.11 Output-mapping rules** - 1-to-1 replaces the source with backup (B3); N-to-1 (PDF, TIFF) writes a new file, sources kept unless 'Move sources to Backups' (A-2); 1-to-N per M10; held items excluded, listed (B4). Pass: mapping x mode table; crash injection keeps sources.
- [ ] **M11.12 Default-output table, notices** - Extends M2.27 (D3): `resolve_format` finalises the default-output table; a format whose writer is not shipped (lossy WebP, AVIF, JXL) stays `NotReplaceable(format.write_unavailable)`; typed `encode.*` notices (B20). Pass: >= 25 (source, format) pairs.
- [ ] **M11.13 Resize in the render pass** - `Resize::{Original, LongestEdge, Percent}` folds into the render scale (one resample); no upscaling unless `allow_upscale`; dpi scales; resize disables the lossless JPEG path. Pass: 12 MP to 2000 px within 1 level of a single-warp reference.
- [ ] **M11.14 DPI mapping through the pipeline** - `Dpi {x, y, unit, origin}` read from JFIF, EXIF, `pHYs`, TIFF, kept through crop, deskew, resize and written per format; never invented outside PDF (nominal 300). Pass: 72/150/300/600/unknown dpi round trip within +-0.5% in two readers.
- [ ] **M11.15 Metadata policy engine (C2)** - Extends M2.23: KeepAll (all but Orientation, thumbnails, MPF), KeepStripLocation, StripAll (ICC, dpi kept); `ImageUniqueID` dropped on splits; one `DEFAULT_METADATA`. Pass: property test: tags per policy, no GPS bytes, Orientation 1.
- [ ] **M11.16 Colour policy, ICC/CICP routing** - sRGB default only for HEIC/HEIF to JPG and enhanced output; other sources keep profile and pixels (ICC byte-exact, else sRGB with notice); CICP for known profiles. Pass: P3 chart deltaE2000 <= 1 (PROVISIONAL); preserve keeps ICC/CICP.
- [ ] **M11.17 Alpha, depth, gray per format** - JPEG and PDF flatten alpha on white; PNG, WebP, AVIF, JXL, TIFF keep alpha, clean hidden RGB; 16-bit only in PNG, TIFF, JXL (else 8-bit, dither, notice); gray stays one channel where supported. Pass: RGBA, LA, 16-bit cases read back right.
- [ ] **M11.18 HDR and gain-map export policy** - `GainMapPolicy::Drop` + notice; PQ/HLG tone-mapped to SDR (M6); Ultra HDR/MPF sources lose MPF and secondary image; `KeepUltraHdr` reserved, greyed; no HDR output in 1.0. Pass: Ultra HDR fixture exports to every format with no MPF, Orientation 1.
- [ ] **M11.81 ManyToOne records and Restore** - `ExportRecord` `ManyToOne`: all sources, one backup id each (M10.25); restore of one source keeps the combined file; run restore handles the group; idempotency guard knows the output. Pass: fault injection backs up all sources before any move.
- [ ] **M11.82 Items as pages in combined outputs** - A multi-item scan adds its included items, in order, as consecutive pages; a scan with a held or Check item is excluded (B4); `PageSink` takes item rasters. Pass: scans with 2, 3 and 1 items give a 6-page PDF in Order-mode order.

### Codecs/I/O
- [ ] **M11.19 JPEG encoder options** - Extends M2.25: JPEG sources use MatchSource (Small/Balanced/Best = clamp(q_est-10, 60, 85)/(+5, 80, 95)/(+10, 90, 97)), others q80/q90/q95; chroma Auto|444|422|420; progressive off; optimised Huffman. Pass: ICC byte-exact; JPEG-to-JPEG follows M2.25.
- [ ] **M11.20 PNG options (oxipng moved to B.11)** - `png` 0.18 presets (fast, balanced, high), adaptive filtering; 8/16-bit gray, gray+alpha, RGB, RGBA; `iCCP`, `cICP`, `pHYs`, `eXIf`, `iTXt` XMP; no interlacing; oxipng is post-1.0 (B.11). Pass: pixel-exact round trip in three readers.
- [ ] **M11.21 WebP encoders** - Lossless via `image-webp`; lossy via libwebp (S3, encode only): q75/85/92; metadata via mux; 16383 px cap gives `DimensionCap`. Pass: lossless bit-exact in `dwebp`, Pillow; lossy within 0.5 dB of `cwebp`; lossy-WebP sources replaceable after this (D3).
- [ ] **M11.22 AVIF encoder** - `ravif` over rav1e with the S2 choices: chroma (4:2:0 photos, 4:4:4 text), quality/speed 60/6, 75/6, 88/5 (PROVISIONAL), nclx-matched matrix; no HEVC path. Pass: decodes in dav1d, Pillow; PSNR >= 38 dB at Balanced; AVIF sources replaceable after this (D3).
- [ ] **M11.23 AVIF metadata boxes** - `avif-serialize` hooks or an own ISOBMFF inserter: `colr` (nclx for sRGB and P3, else `prof`), Exif item (4-byte prefix), XMP, no `irot`/`imir`, right `ispe`/`pixi`. Pass: box test; `avifdec`, libheif, Pillow read Exif/ICC; fuzz never corrupts `iloc`.
- [ ] **M11.24 AVIF cancel and timeout** - Bounded pool (rav1e cannot stop mid-frame): cancel frees UI and queue in < 200 ms; timeout `max(30 s, 6 s per MP)` gives `EncodeTimeout`; 1-2 jobs via `MemoryBudget`; worker fallback if cancel CPU > ~2 s. Pass: 200-image batch cancel leaves no temp.
- [ ] **M11.25 libjxl static build in `xtask`** - `xtask build-native` builds libjxl (SHA-256 pin); `native-deps.toml` rows for libjxl, highway, brotli, skcms; static link into the JXL crate only. Pass: clean build on 3 OSes (cold <= 20 min); version equals the pin; no libjxl shared library.
- [ ] **M11.26 `codecs-jxl-sys`: own FFI** - ~15 `JxlEncoder*` bindings plus a safe `LibjxlBackend` behind `JxlEncoderBackend`; no `jpegxl-*` dependency; jxl-rs stays decode-only. Pass: gray, RGB, RGBA, lossless and lossy; bad sizes are typed errors, no aborts; `cargo tree -i jpegxl-sys` empty.
- [ ] **M11.27 JXL presets, colour, metadata** - Small d1.8/e5, Balanced d1.0/e6, Best d0.5/e7 (PROVISIONAL) plus lossless; `JxlColorEncoding` for sRGB and P3, else ICC; Exif and `xml ` boxes. Pass: lossless bit-exact in `djxl`, jxl-rs; JXL sources replaceable after M11.25-M11.27 (D3).
- [ ] **M11.28 JXL JPEG recompression (stretch)** - Non-gating stretch: `AddJPEGFrame` plus `StoreJPEGMetadata` for JPEG to JXL when no pixel edit applies, after the M11.15 patch; Advanced only; 3-day time-box, else B.43. Pass: `djxl` rebuilds the patched JPEG byte-exactly for 20 fixtures.
- [ ] **M11.29 TIFF writer** - LZW (16-bit predictor), Deflate, PackBits, none; 8/16-bit gray, RGB, RGBA; ~256 KB strips streamed; ICC, Exif, XMP, IPTC, resolution tags; BigTIFF above ~3.5 GiB. Pass: pixel-exact in `tiff`, libtiff, Pillow, ImageMagick; 100 MP RGB <= raster + 64 MB RSS.
- [ ] **M11.30 TIFF multi-page and G4** - IFD chain with `PageNumber`; per-page dpi, ICC, policy; G4 pages reuse the M7 wrapper; 1-bit and colour mix only if S5 picks the unified writer, else a typed error. Pass: 50-page fixture in 4 readers: order, dpi, no inversion, no GPS bytes.
- [ ] **M11.31 `PageSink` and orchestrator** - `PageSink` (`begin`, `add_page`, `finish`) for PDF and TIFF; pages render in parallel (`MemoryBudget`), reorder in a window, stream in order; one failed page fails the file. Pass: deterministic order; page 7 of 20 failing leaves no output.
- [ ] **M11.32 PDF writer core** - `codecs::pdf` on `pdf-writer` 0.15 or the S4 writer: one image per page, classic xref, deterministic objects, incremental output; `Info` has `Title`, `Producer`, `CreationDate` only. Pass: `qpdf --check` clean; `pdfinfo` agrees; 1000 pages in constant memory.
- [ ] **M11.33 PDF JPEG pass-through** - Encoder JPEG bytes (8-bit baseline) embedded unchanged after our parser drops APPn and COM; `DeviceRGB`, `DeviceGray` or `ICCBased`. Pass: `pdfimages -list` shows `jpeg` at the expected ppi; bytes equal the stripped encoder output; no GPS bytes.
- [ ] **M11.34 PDF PNG pass-through** - Lossless pages embed the PNG `IDAT` stream as `/FlateDecode` with `/Predictor 15`; non-interlaced 8-bit gray or RGB (alpha flattened, 16-bit reduced). Pass: `pdfimages -all` gives a pixel-exact raster; PDFium and pdf.js SSIM >= 0.98; size within 2% of PNG.
- [ ] **M11.35 PDF CCITT G4 pages** - Bilevel pages reuse the M7 G4 bytes (`/CCITTFaxDecode`, `/K -1`); polarity is the classic bug, so render-and-compare. Pass: Poppler, PDFium, pdf.js agree (<= 0.1% pixels differ), no inversion; size = G4 payload + <= 2 KB per page; bilevel only.

## M12 - Curved-page dewarp

<!-- ms: version=v0.11.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64, macOS 12+ arm64, Linux x86_64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Deliver opt-in curved-page dewarping (B10): a UVDoc-class dense-grid model whose flow map is applied to the full-resolution image in one resample, suggested when a page looks curved, never forced and never a batch default, with cleared weight provenance and a measured, relative quality gate. It is the highest schedule risk in the plan: a missed gate delays 1.0 and any other outcome is the owner's decision (A-4).
**Release:** `v0.11.0` - Windows 10/11 x64 (unsigned unless SignPath has approved, B15), macOS 12+ arm64 (ad-hoc signed DMG) and Linux x86_64 (Ubuntu 22.04+, Fedora: AppImage, deb, rpm, own Flatpak remote); Apple silicon is first-class, ARM64 on Windows and Linux and Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0; the dewarp model is bundled or a hash-pinned Dewarp pack installed from file (M12.69, no in-app download); `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, SBOM, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES`.
**Depends on:** M4 (`InferenceBackend` and pinned ONNX runtime, `calibration.json`, provenance log and models repo, M4.25 dense-map warp, M4.81 audit rows) and M11 (export encoders, PDF sizing, metadata policy); by release order also M3, M5, M7, M8, M9 and M10.
**Size:** XL (definition in X.40: part-time weeks S 1-2, M 3-4, L 5-8, XL 9-16; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 4](docs/plan/04-detection-geometry.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 5](docs/plan/05-enhancement.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md), [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G5 (dewarp): weights `cleared` or under an owner-granted `exception` (A-7); on the private curved slice and test-only benchmarks median relative CER cut >= 20% vs perspective-only, worse by > 1 CER point on <= 2%, >= 95% of flat pages skipped byte-identical; <= 1 s per 12 MP page on Tier-M; Suggest default, Auto opt-in. All numbers PROVISIONAL until measured; a missed gate delays 1.0 and any other outcome is the owner's decision with measured numbers (A-4, M12.68).
**Risks / spikes:**
- Provenance and order: UVDoc's data is third-party or research-only and its repo is quiet since 2024-07-28, so path A needs an owner-granted `exception` (A-7) and path B ($0 compute) may outrun the milestone; M12.03 comes first, dev-only spikes and weight-free core work run in parallel, performance and cross-OS last.
- Speed and harm: PaddleX measures about 870 ms on a Xeon Gold 6271C, so 0.4-1 s is unmeasured (ladder M12.65); folds, spreads, hands and non-page content are beyond a smooth-surface model, so verification, a Check cap and leave-unchanged guard flat pages (M12.21-M12.26, M12.59).
- Evidence and scope: 30 curved images cannot certify 2% (n < 80 advisory, n < 30 never public; the pooled benchmark carries the statistic); Tesseract is a dev-only oracle (OCR stays a non-goal, B19); no upstream, so pin commit and hash (M12.11). Out of scope: editable mesh, spine splitting (B.42), lens distortion (A-11).

### Security/Legal
- [ ] **M12.01 Provenance audit of the UVDoc lineage** - Extends M4.81: confirm a final log row for every dataset and checkpoint behind the candidate weights; none `pending`; verdict `cleared`, `blocked`, `banned` or `exception`, citing licence text or "no licence stated". _(gate: G5)_
- [ ] **M12.02 Author outreach for a data statement** - Extends M4.81: read the outcome of the UVDoc authors' data-statement request (DTD and Doc3D limits on the weights; 14-day time-box, PROVISIONAL): reply or dated no-reply is logged; silence is "no statement", never consent.
- [ ] **M12.03 Provenance decision gate** - ADR before M12 starts (S.21), one path: (A) UVDoc weights under an owner-granted `exception` (residual-risk analysis, model-card note; A-7; G5 accepts it), (B) own-trained Track B weights, (C) gate-miss protocol (A-4, M12.68); B or C triggers M12.18.
- [ ] **M12.04 Exclusions and test-only rows** - Extends M4.04: `banned` rows for unlicensed third-party UVDoc ONNX re-uploads; `test-only` rows (pinned SHA-256) for DocUNet, DIR300, UVDoc benchmark. `check-models` and models-repo CI fail if one reaches a manifest, requirements or `models.lock`.
- [ ] **M12.05 Dev-only quarantine for unaudited weights** - Feasibility checkpoints (M12.11-M12.16, M12.57) stay outside the repo, pinned as use `dev-only`; `check-models`, packaging and release refuse them; only a `dev-weights` build loads them; a release build bundling one fails (test).
- [ ] **M12.06 Weight integrity and pinned hashes** - Extends M4.08: `models.lock` pins SHA-256, size, version, opset, commit, converters; the loader hashes then builds from memory (bundled file or pack). A flipped byte gives `ModelLoadFailed{model: dewarp}` and `DEWARP_MODEL_MISSING`, no crash.
- [ ] **M12.07 Licence texts, NOTICE and model card** - Model licence notice in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` and `MODEL_CARD.md` (commit, hashes, data statement or third-party lineage, any exception with ADR link, failure modes)
- [ ] **M12.08 cargo-deny and notice regeneration** - New crates (candidates `half`, `base64`, `crc32fast`; none unless needed) pass the `deny.toml` allow-list; regenerate and diff cargo-about; `opencv` and Tesseract bindings stay out of the shipped graph. `cargo deny check` green on 3 OS targets.
- [ ] **M12.09 Dewarp fuzz targets** - cargo-fuzz `dewarp_prep`, `grid_post`, `grid_serde` (reject before allocating), `grid_warp` (NaN, out-of-frame maps), seeded; 60 s PR smoke, 1 h nightly. >= 30 min per target clean before v0.11.0 (PROVISIONAL), counting toward 72 h before 1.0.
- [ ] **M12.10 Network and privacy check for dewarp** - Extends M4.09: no HTTP client reachable from `engine`, `cli` or the pack installer; a firewall-block run of analysis, install-from-file and CLI `--dewarp` passes on 3 OSes; 'Get Dewarp pack' only opens the release page in the browser.

### Models
- [ ] **M12.11 Reference checkpoint and I/O contract** - Run the MIT PyTorch checkpoint (pinned SHA-256, dev-only) on 10 synthetic pages; `docs/models/dewarp-io.md` records grid nodes (believed 45 x 31), 488x712 orientation, colour order, coordinate convention, each verified or not.
- [ ] **M12.12 ONNX export without the sampler** - Export the 8M-parameter net (opset 17, fp32, static 488x712, ~31 MB) returning only the coordinate grid (no `GridSample`). Size within 10% (PROVISIONAL); PyTorch and ORT CPU grids agree within 1e-3 max abs on 20 images in models-repo CI.
- [ ] **M12.13 Backend operator coverage and parity** - Load the export with the M0.35 backend (ort `=2.0.0-rc.13` unless changed), rten and tract; add 5 tensors to M4.59. Nodes within 0.1% of the diagonal (PROVISIONAL), else the backend is unsupported for dewarp (ADR; decides Intel Mac).
- [ ] **M12.14 fp16 storage and int8 study** - fp16 weights upcast at load (~15 MB) and static QDQ int8 (~200 non-golden images) on Windows x64 (with and without VNNI), macOS arm64, Linux x64.
- [ ] **M12.15 Synthetic curved-page generator** - Python and OpenCV (no Blender): curved surfaces bend flat renders (OFL fonts, CC0 backgrounds, no DTD or UVDoc textures) with exact flow stored.
- [ ] **M12.16 Input-preparation and aspect spike** - On the real curved dev set with dev-only weights compare whole photo, rectified ROI and masked ROI, and stretch, letterbox or tiles for receipts above 2.5:1 (CER, straightness, n).
- [ ] **M12.17 Track B: train from scratch** - If M12.03 picks B (else 'not triggered'): MIT architecture code, random init (no ImageNet, Doc3D or UVDoc weights), M12.15 data, $0 compute (B15), 6-week first cycle (PROVISIONAL). Held-out node error p95 within 20% of the reference, else M12.18.
- [ ] **M12.18 Track B: schedule-impact report** - If M12.03 picks B or C, or the M12.17 time-box lapses: put the schedule impact (weeks, compute, 1.0 date; `docs/adr/`) to the owner: continue (delays 1.0, the default), Experimental or 1.x (owner's call, A-4). Reply recorded, never inferred.

### Detection
- [ ] **M12.19 Curvature score** - <= 15 ms on the 1024 px proxy (PROVISIONAL): `curvature_score` from edge bow (start 0.5%) and text-line bend (M4.36, M1.28); emits `dewarp_suggested` and `CURVED_PAGE_SUSPECTED` (info chip, never changes the band).
- [ ] **M12.20 Curvature thresholds** - Fit `dewarp_suggest_cutoff` on the real curved dev set (never golden, test-only or synthetic; A1): flat pages skipped >= 95%, curved recall reported (target >= 80%); Suggest never holds an item. ROC and n go to `calibration.json`; golden confirms once.
- [ ] **M12.21 Applicability gate** - `dewarp_applicable` refuses (`DEWARP_NOT_APPLICABLE{why}`) on `NO_DOCUMENT`, Failed quad, `partial_frame`, `ORIENT_UNSURE`, aspect outside the M12.16 range or short side < ~600 px (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M12.22 Dewarp inference stage** - `engine::dewarp::run(pyramid, item, cancel) -> RawGrid`: input per M12.16, reference normalisation, lazy `InferenceBackend` session, cancel by run-option terminate (if rc.13 has it) or a generation counter.
- [ ] **M12.23 Grid verification** - `verify_grid` rejects non-finite grids, a non-positive Jacobian, local scale outside 0.5x-2x, no edge-curvature gain or aspect > 15% off Zhang-He; residual < 0.3% of the diagonal = `DEWARP_NEAR_FLAT` skip (PROVISIONAL).
- [ ] **M12.24 Post-warp straightness check** - Compute the M12.56 residual before and after on the display-proxy render (shared with the eval); keep the grid only if after <= 0.9 x before (PROVISIONAL); no measurable lines = inconclusive, capped at Check. Rejects mirrored and over-strength grids.
- [ ] **M12.25 Failure-mode detectors** - Cheap detectors route non-page content, folds and creases (`DEWARP_REJECTED{fold}`), book spreads (`DEWARP_REJECTED{spread}`) and hands over the outline to leave-unchanged or Check. Rates with n on the M12.60 fixtures; never an auto-accepted worse result.
- [ ] **M12.26 Dewarp confidence and the Check band** - `dewarp_confidence` (signals M12.23-M12.25, calibrated M12.61). Below `t_apply` the dewarp is discarded (`DEWARP_REJECTED`, byte-identical to homography-only)

### Core
- [ ] **M12.27 GridWarp type and `Geometry::Grid`** - `Geometry::Grid(Arc<GridWarp>)` {outline `QuadWarp`, cols, rows, nodes, `ModelRef{id, sha256}`, strength, `post_rotate_deg`} is the only dewarped form (no stale Quad); constructors validate; `render_hash()` excludes the verdict.
- [ ] **M12.28 Grid serialisation, caps, migration** - Nodes as little-endian f32 (base64 + CRC32 in JSON, blob in the journal), byte-stable; caps 256 x 256 nodes and 1 MiB (PROVISIONAL) checked before allocating; pure schema migration; a saved grid resumes without inference.
- [ ] **M12.29 Composition order** - ADR fixes one order: source (EXIF once), quarter turns and mirror (in the outline `QuadWarp`), homography, flow G, per-route margin and crop (A-11), `post_rotate_deg`.
- [ ] **M12.30 Map-driven strip-wise resampler** - Extends M4.25 with a per-pixel map provider: ~64-row strips, node interpolation per strip, per-strip source box, pre-shrink above local scale 1.5, Lanczos3, u8 and u16, no full-frame f32 map.
- [ ] **M12.31 Quality vs the homography path** - Identity grid equals the M4.25 warp at >= 45 dB PSNR (PROVISIONAL); dense-map oracle vs OpenCV `remap` (dev-only) on 10 maps; ground-truth flow recovers the page at SSIM >= 0.98.
- [ ] **M12.32 Output size, aspect and validity mask** - Output size from grid arc lengths, never upscaled, capped by `max_pixels`; grid vs Zhang-He aspect differing > 3% uses the grid, > 8% is flagged (PROVISIONAL). The render returns a u8 validity mask (0 outside frame or outline).
- [ ] **M12.33 Grid cache and stage keys** - Cache `RawGrid` by (source, model sha256, prep, outline hash, `algo_ver`) in a 64-entry LRU (PROVISIONAL), keep the chosen grid in `EditState`, key the proxy render by `render_hash` (M3.11).
- [ ] **M12.34 Engine API and Analysis fields** - `DewarpPolicy {Off, Suggest, Auto}` in `AnalyzeOptions` (default Suggest: never applies; Off skips the curvature score); `Analysis` gains `curvature_score`, `dewarp_suggested`, `dewarp{...}`; `schema_version` bump.
- [ ] **M12.35 Preview, strength blend and cancellation** - Strength blend `nodes' = I + s x (G - I)` (0 = perspective-only). A tick re-warps only the 2-4 MP display proxy in Rust, no inference (<= 50 ms at 2 MP, PROVISIONAL), full render on release; no grid sampler in TypeScript.
- [ ] **M12.36 History: undo and redo** - Entries `Flatten`, `Unflatten`, `Strength` (coalesced per gesture, M3.12), re-flatten after an outline change; committed only when the grid is ready. Undo restores the prior `Geometry::Quad` and keeps the cached grid for instant redo.
- [ ] **M12.37 Outline edits on Grid items** - A corner drag previews with the homography; on release the grid is re-expressed in the new outline. A move > 1.5% of the diagonal or aspect change > 5% (PROVISIONAL) marks it `stale` and queues a cancellable re-inference ('Keep previous flattening').
- [ ] **M12.38 Batch integration and memory admission** - Dewarp is a bounded stage in `Analysing`: one inference at a time, shared session, per-job timeout, admission weighted by measured memory (M12.64) on the M6.45 budget; `Save all` never waits on it.
- [ ] **M12.39 Save path and lossless-JPEG bypass** - Grid items always take the full render, encode and verify path (never the lossless JPEG path); verify checks decoded size against `out_size`. Re-editing a saved dewarped file renders from the backup original, never dewarping twice.

### Enhancement
- [ ] **M12.40 Dewarp before flatten** - Flatten and tone run on the dewarped raster: stage keys include the grid hash (M7.02), the validity mask feeds M7.03 (B&W fills outside white), `dpi_eq` (M7.05) uses it.
- [ ] **M12.41 Order ablation on the curved slice** - Compare dewarp-then-enhance (decided) with enhance-then-dewarp on the real curved dev set for CER and artefacts (seams, halo, smear). The decided order is not worse on median CER, or the ADR records the gap and why it stays.
- [ ] **M12.42 Per-item dewarp with multi-item splits** - Each M10 `Item` has its own `Geometry`: dewarp runs per item on its ROI with its own suggestion, confidence and history; a file's confidence is the minimum, N items cost N inferences, M10 naming and B3 backups unchanged.

### UI/UX
- [ ] **M12.43 Curved-page suggestion chip** - Adjust and Review show a chip (icon plus text) 'This page looks curved. Flatten it?' with **Flatten** and **Not now** (remembered per image) when `dewarp_suggested` is set; nothing applies without a tap; a Settings toggle turns it off (policy Off).
- [ ] **M12.44 Flatten control and its states** - Adjust row **Flatten curved page**: Off, Working (progress, Cancel), On, Skipped (reason chip by code, e.g. 'Not applied: this looks folded'), Unavailable (links to Settings > Models). One history entry per apply; a skip leaves the crop untouched.
- [ ] **M12.45 Strength control** - **Strength** slider 0-100%: one history entry per gesture, numeric entry, reset; 0% equals Off without re-inference; default 100% unless the M12.58 curve says otherwise (in `defaults`).
- [ ] **M12.46 Compare view** - Extends M3.34 with Original, Crop only (the `strength = 0` path, no extra inference) and Flattened: segmented control, hold-to-compare, draggable split, clear labels.
- [ ] **M12.47 Batch and review-grid controls** - Batch option **Curved pages: Off, Suggest (default), Auto (experimental)**, 'About 1 s per flagged page' (PROVISIONAL). Review grid: 'Flattened' badge (icon plus word), `DEWARP_UNCERTAIN` and `DEWARP_REJECTED` chips, **Curved (n)** filter, counts.
- [ ] **M12.48 Model settings and first-use prompt** - Settings > Models dewarp row (bundled, pack or absent; version, size, short SHA-256); pack: **Get Dewarp pack** (URL, size, SHA-256, opens the browser), **Install from file...** (Rust picker, hash-pinned), **Remove**.
- [ ] **M12.49 Accessibility, i18n and touch pass** - 44-48 px hit areas in touch mode (>= 24 px always), keyboard paths, `aria-live` ('Page flattened. Undo available.'), an operable Cancel, a native range input with `aria-valuetext`, pseudo-locale and pseudo-RTL screenshots.
- [ ] **M12.50 Usability round for flattening** - At least 5 testers on a Windows touch device (PROVISIONAL): success if >= 4 of 5 find Flatten from the suggestion chip, tell flattened from crop-only with Compare and undo it unaided; findings feed the wording in M12.43-M12.47.

### CLI
- [ ] **M12.51 CLI `--dewarp` and strength** - `--dewarp[=off|suggest|auto]` (bare = `auto`; default `suggest`, report only) and `--dewarp-strength 0-100`. `--dewarp auto` without the model exits non-zero with `DEWARP_MODEL_MISSING`, never a silent skip.
- [ ] **M12.52 CLI JSON, doctor and pack commands** - `analyze --json` adds `curvature_score`, `dewarp_suggested` and the `dewarp` block; `doctor` prints model id, hash, source (bundled, pack, missing), backend.

### Quality/Perf
- [ ] **M12.53 Curved-page golden stratum and dev set** - Add a `curved` stratum to the private golden set (A-8): >= 30 real images (target 60 by 1.0; n < 80 advisory), each with a flat reference (scan or >= 200-word transcript), quad and subtype tag, plus a real curved dev set >= 30.
- [ ] **M12.54 Test-only benchmark adapters** - `xtask fetch-testonly` fetches DocUNet, DIR300 and the UVDoc benchmark by pinned SHA-256 into a gitignored cache and converts them to the eval manifest (`test-only`)
- [ ] **M12.55 Tesseract CER harness (dev-only)** - Extends M1.66 and M7.50: Tesseract 5 and `eng` data pinned by hash, fixed segmentation and thresholding; CER = normalised Levenshtein vs the hand transcript (fallback: flat-reference OCR), median for original.
- [ ] **M12.56 Line-straightness metric** - One `imgproc::straightness` shared by the engine (M12.24) and the eval: text-line baselines (projection profile, LSD where sparse), median RMS residual in text heights plus page-edge deviation.
- [ ] **M12.57 Early feasibility checkpoint** - Once M12.12 and M12.55 exist, run dev-only weights on the curved dev set and test-only benchmarks (never golden): is >= 20% relative CER gain and <= 1 s plausible? Publish the numbers (labelled dev-only) and a written go or no-go.
- [ ] **M12.58 Quality evaluation run** - On the shipping weights only: CER, straightness, strength-versus-CER (0-100%) and the count worse than baseline (CER up > 1 point) on the private curved slice (>= 30) and test-only benchmarks, Clopper-Pearson intervals.
- [ ] **M12.59 Do-no-harm on flat pages** - Run detection and Auto dewarp on >= 100 real flat pages plus the 500 synthetic set: >= 95% skipped (PROVISIONAL) and byte-identical to perspective-only; no non-skipped page with CER worse by > 1 point; failures listed by image id.
- [ ] **M12.60 Failure-mode fixture set and evaluation** - >= 5 fixtures per category (PROVISIONAL): non-page content, folded receipts, book spreads, hands or fingers, blank pages (synthetic or own CC0 photos; also feed M12.77).
- [ ] **M12.61 Dewarp confidence calibration** - Fit `dewarp_confidence` (logistic on the M12.23-M12.25 signals plus curvature, then isotonic) on the real curved dev set only (never synthetic, golden or test-only; A1); golden confirms once.
- [ ] **M12.62 Inference latency on Tier-M and Apple silicon** - On Windows Tier-M (M0.47) time the 488x712 forward pass (median and p95 over >= 30 warm runs at 1, 4, 6 threads, cold load, preprocessing); repeat on Apple silicon (a Mac, A-9) and Linux.
- [ ] **M12.63 Warp cost and 12 MP end-to-end** - Criterion at 12, 48, 100 MP; gungraun counts on Linux CI for resampler, `verify_grid`, straightness (> 5% blocks); end-to-end 12 MP with `tracing` spans.
- [ ] **M12.64 Memory and batch-budget test** - Peak RSS (session loaded, inference, warp) at 12, 48, 100 MP, plus model and activation memory as the M12.38 admission cost. Per image <= 175 MB at 12 MP plus that cost, 500 MB at 48 MP, 1.0 GB at 100 MP (PROVISIONAL)
- [ ] **M12.65 Speed contingency ladder** - If Tier-M misses 1 s, try in order and record: more threads and a pre-optimised `.ort`, M12.14 fp16 or int8 where they pass, a smaller input (CER-checked)
- [ ] **M12.66 PR smoke gate and nightly or private runs** - Per PR (no weights): analytic-grid kernel tests, identity-grid equality, 20 curved and 20 flat fixtures for skip rate; inference smoke only with `cleared` or `exception` weights. Nightly: 500-image set.
- [ ] **M12.67 Cross-OS parity** - Run the same 10 fixtures on Windows, macOS arm64 and Linux with the shipped model: nodes within 0.1% of the page diagonal and output SSIM >= 0.99 between OSes (PROVISIONAL); deterministic per platform, and the docs say results are not bit-identical across CPUs.
- [ ] **M12.68 Gate-miss (descope) protocol** - Dewarp instance of X.41: `cargo xtask gate-report dewarp` prints metric, target, actual, n and interval per gate. A missed gate delays 1.0; Experimental.

### CI/Release
- [ ] **M12.69 Model delivery decision** - ADR: bundled or a **Dewarp pack**, with measured sizes (fp32 ~31 MB, fp16 ~15 MB, int8 if it qualifies) and installer delta per OS. Proposed default (PROVISIONAL, veto-able): bundle if total models stay <= ~40 MB.
- [ ] **M12.70 Bundled delivery** - If M12.69 chose bundling: the `models.lock` dewarp file ships in the Tauri resources on all three OSes (fetched by `xtask fetch-models` at build time, hash-checked) with its licence.
- [ ] **M12.71 Explicit Dewarp pack** - If M12.69 chose the pack: release asset `auto-crop-dewarp-<model_ver>.onnx` plus `.sha256` and attestation, per-user install directory, hash pinned in the app, one verifier for `Install from file...` and `dewarp-pack install`, removable, shown in `doctor`
- [ ] **M12.72 Installer size and per-OS packaging** - Record installer and package sizes before and after (NSIS, zip, DMG, AppImage, deb, rpm, Flatpak) in `docs/perf/installer-size.md`; the macOS ad-hoc signature covers the bundled model, the Flatpak carries it in its own remote.
- [ ] **M12.73 Intel Mac, no-ML and distro builds** - Dewarp follows the ONNX backend M8 chose for Intel Macs; with none it is unavailable with a plain in-app message (`DEWARP_MODEL_MISSING` semantics), never silent.
- [ ] **M12.74 Installed-artifact self-test with dewarp** - `auto-crop doctor --self-test` gains one embedded curved page: verify the model hash, run inference and the grid warp, check nodes within 0.1% and SSIM against the stored flat page.
- [ ] **M12.75 Release pipeline v0.11.0** - release-plz bump; three OS artifacts, `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, SBOM, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` with the dewarp licence, the `models.lock` hashes and, if the pack ships, the pack asset with checksum.

### Docs
- [ ] **M12.76 Dewarp user guide** - `docs/dewarp.md`: what flattening does and does not do, when it is suggested, Strength and Compare, batch policies and time cost, delivery, the offline promise, weight provenance.
- [ ] **M12.77 Known failure gallery** - `docs/dewarp-failures.md`: before and after images (synthetic or own CC0 photos, never private specimens) per M12.60 failure mode, incl. applied-and-wrong cases, with what the app did and why.
- [ ] **M12.78 README accuracy row and limitations page** - Add measured dewarp results (CER, straightness, worse-than-baseline count, flat-page skip rate, Tier-M time) with n and intervals, noting the curved slice is private and 30 images cannot certify 2%.
- [ ] **M12.79 Design doc and ADRs** - `docs/design/dewarp.md` (pipeline, composition order, cache keys, verification, confidence policy, delivery) plus ADRs: provenance, ONNX backend and precision, input preparation, composition order, model delivery, gate result.

### Exit gate
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Provenance - every dataset and weight behind the shipped model is `cleared` or, path A only, an owner-granted `exception` (A-7); banned or blocked assets cannot reach any bundled or packed file (CI); the M12.03 ADR names the path; for B or C the owner has answered M12.18.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Quality (PROVISIONAL) - shipping weights, private curved slice (>= 30) plus test-only benchmarks: median relative CER cut >= 20% vs perspective-only, straightness improves, worse by > 1 CER point on <= 2% of images.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Do-no-harm (PROVISIONAL) - >= 100 real plus 500 synthetic flat pages: >= 95% skipped byte-identical to perspective-only, none non-skipped > 1 CER point worse; failure fixtures (folds, spreads, non-page, hands; >= 5 each) end Unchanged or Check, never auto-accepted and worse.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Speed and memory (PROVISIONAL) - <= 1 s per 12 MP page on Windows Tier-M (prep, inference, verification, full-resolution warp; cost stated); Apple silicon and Linux recorded; RSS <= 175 MB at 12 MP plus model cost, 500 MB at 48 MP, 1.0 GB at 100 MP; Suggest default, Auto opt-in.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Edit model - a `Geometry::Grid` item previews, cancels, undoes, redoes, serialises and migrates (proptest, journal-resume green); an identity grid matches the homography path at >= 45 dB PSNR (PROVISIONAL); one resample only (a test fails on any intermediate raster).
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Delivery - the delivery ADR is implemented (bundled, or a hash-pinned Dewarp pack installed from file); no automatic or in-app download exists (firewall-block and dependency-graph checks green); installer size impact per OS recorded in `docs/perf/installer-size.md`.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Legal and security - `cargo deny check` green on three OSes, notices regenerated with the model licence, `xtask check-models` green, every fuzz target >= 30 minutes clean (PROVISIONAL; >= 72 h cumulative per target before 1.0).
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** All three OSes - quality run (private Linux workflow), per-OS speed and memory runs and parity pass; v0.11.0 artifacts pass `doctor --self-test` with a dewarp fixture on clean Windows 10 and 11, macOS 12+ arm64, Ubuntu 22.04, Fedora.
- [ ] **GATE (G5):** Honest reporting - README accuracy table, limitations page and v0.11.0 notes state every gate result, measured or missed; a missed gate delays 1.0 unless the owner decides Experimental or 1.x with measured numbers (A-4, M12.68); no GATE box is ticked by inference.

## M13 - Hardening, accessibility, i18n, docs, legal and 1.0 release

<!-- ms: version=v1.0.0 | platforms=Windows 10/11 x64, macOS 12+ arm64, Linux x86_64 | size=XL -->

**Goal:** Turn the feature-complete previews into a trustworthy 1.0: accessibility, i18n, security and fuzz hardening, docs, legal closure, verified shell integration and channels, and two clean release-candidate windows. Only a few things are new builds (rebinding UI, optional Explorer handler, Store MSIX, release tooling); the rest verifies, hardens, documents or ships M2-M12.
**Release:** `v1.0.0` via `v1.0.0-rc.N`; Windows 10 22H2/11 x64, macOS 12+ arm64, Linux Ubuntu 22.04+/Fedora x86_64 (B9: all three solid). Apple silicon (macOS arm64) is first-class; ARM64 on Windows and Linux and Intel Macs are best-effort until 1.0. Artifacts: NSIS (direct and managed), portable zip, Store MSIX, DMG plus own tap, AppImage, deb, rpm, own Flatpak remote, `SHA256SUMS`, attestations, CycloneDX SBOM, `latest.json` + `.sig`, `native-sources-1.0.0.tar.zst`; Windows SignPath-signed if approved, else the documented pending state; Flathub prepared, not submitted (B17).
**Depends on:** M8, M9, M10, M11, M12 and transitively M0-M7; named: M6.81 (Store dry run), M2.28 and M5.82 (Store data layout), M5.39 (shortcut registry), M5.68 (signing status), M1.64 (`pages.yml`), X.41 (gate-miss ADR).
**Size:** XL (9-16 part-time weeks, definition in X.40; PROVISIONAL)
**Design:** [PLAN 1](docs/plan/01-vision-decisions.md), [PLAN 2](docs/plan/02-architecture.md), [PLAN 3](docs/plan/03-image-io-formats.md), [PLAN 6](docs/plan/06-ux-touch.md), [PLAN 7](docs/plan/07-performance-accuracy-quality.md), [PLAN 8](docs/plan/08-oss-release-security-risks.md); [decision log](docs/plan/00-decision-log.md)
**Exit gate:** G7 (all lines below): every M2-M12 accuracy, speed and safety target is met on all three OSes or carries an owner-approved delta (A-4, A-9); golden v2 (>= 800 locked) shows silent failures at point <= 1.0% and Clopper-Pearson bound <= 2.0%; two RC windows of 14 days (3 for a native-bump-only RC, A-12) close with 0 open P0/P1 and data-loss bugs; a 1,000-image dogfood loses 0 originals. Accessibility, i18n, security, licence and distribution gates pass (Store and SignPath as live or documented state); PROVISIONAL unless measured.
**Risks / spikes:**
- Hardware and time: a real Mac, Windows touch device, Tier-M/L machines and testers gate B9; a missing one blocks the tag or needs an owner-recorded delta (M13.91, A-9); the RC windows alone take >= 28 days and a native bump restarts the clock (native-only: 3 days, A-12).
- External timelines: Store certification, SignPath approval and Flathub's AI policy (flipped three times since May) are outside our control, so Store work starts by M6 and nothing waits on them; MSIX virtualisation could delete backups, so M2.28 and M5.82 settle it early and M13.60 verifies.
- Unverified: screen readers on the canvas and WebKitGTK (Orca best-effort, A-11), the Windows 11 modern menu (optional stretch), and labelling 800 golden images in time (slices under 80 stay advisory; a shortfall is the owner's call via M13.42).

### Long-lead items (start by M6)
- [ ] **M13.01 Partner Center registration and Store name** - start by M6 (dry run M6.81), owner of the Store submission: free individual account (any fee stops and goes to the owner, B15/A-6); reserve "Auto Crop" plus two tagline fallbacks; record identity values in `docs/dev/store-status.md`.
